# pg-cdc Milestone 1 (Stream) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Stream every committed change in one Postgres publication through a logical replication slot to stdout as version 1 JSON envelopes, and acknowledge the slot only after each transaction is delivered.

**Architecture:** A Cargo workspace with five crates: `pg-cdc` (library and binary), `pg-cdc-decoder` (pure pgoutput and value decoding), `pg-cdc-replication` (the vendored pgwire-replication transport), `pg-cdc-testkit` (containers and fixtures for tests) and a detached `fuzz/` crate. One streaming session per connection reads transport events in order, decodes each pgoutput message, builds envelopes, writes them to the stdout sink, flushes at Commit, and only then advances the acknowledged LSN. A supervisor reconnects with backoff, halts after three decode faults at one LSN, and exits cleanly on SIGTERM.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 1.99.0 (edition 2024), tokio 1.53, tokio-postgres 0.7 over rustls 0.23 (ring), pgwire-replication 0.4.1 (vendored), serde_json 1.0 (`arbitrary_precision`, `preserve_order`), serde-saphyr 2.0, aws-sdk-rds 1.154, testcontainers 0.28, moto 5.2.4, proptest 1.12, cargo-fuzz 0.13, mise 2026.10.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md` (binding). This plan implements spec §19 item 1 and the parts of §5, §6, §7, §9, §11.1, §11.7, §14, §15, §16.1, §17.4 and §18 that item 1 needs.

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements implicitly include this section.

- The spec is the binding authority. Where this plan and the spec disagree, the rulings below record why.
- The repository is public and open source, licensed `MIT OR Apache-2.0`. CI must run on pull requests from forks without any secret.
- Toolchain: Rust `1.99.0` pinned in `rust-toolchain.toml` with components `rustfmt`, `clippy`, `llvm-tools-preview`; edition `2024`; workspace `rust-version = "1.99"`. Nightly is used only by `mise run fuzz-smoke`, pinned as `FUZZ_TOOLCHAIN = "nightly-2026-10-01"`.
- In any worktree, run `mise trust && mise install` once before using mise.
- Run `mise run fmt` before `mise run check`; code blocks in this plan are correct but not always in rustfmt's exact layout.
- `mise run check` is the single gate: `cargo fmt --all --check`; `taplo fmt --check`; `typos`; `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`; `RUSTDOCFLAGS="-D warnings" cargo doc --workspace --no-deps --all-features`; `cargo nextest run --workspace --all-features`; `cargo deny check`; `cargo machete`. The last step of every task runs it and it must pass. If it outlives the 10-minute Bash timeout, run it in the background with output in `/Users/rajpatel/pg-cdc-work/integration/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-m1-stream/<task>-check.log` and poll the log.
- Lints live in `[workspace.lints]` and every crate sets `[lints] workspace = true`. Never weaken a lint. An exception is `#[expect(<lint>, reason = "...")]` at the narrowest scope.
- Clippy's test exemptions (`allow-unwrap-in-tests` and friends) apply only inside `#[test]` functions and `#[cfg(test)]` modules. Helper functions in `crates/*/tests/*.rs` that sit outside a `#[test]` function return `Result` and never call `unwrap`, `expect`, `panic!` or index slices.
- Clippy pedantic is on: keep functions under 100 lines (`too_many_lines`), convert numbers with `try_from`, `u64::from` or `cast_unsigned`, never with `as` between types of different sign or width, and never index or slice (`get`, `split_first`, `split_at_checked`, `split_first_chunk` instead).
- Zero code comments, doc comments included. The only allowed comment states a genuine exception or anomaly the code cannot express. Vendored code in `crates/replication/src` keeps its upstream comments (Ruling R2).
- Errors: `thiserror` in libraries, `anyhow` only in `crates/pg-cdc/src/main.rs`. No panics outside tests.
- Async: tokio only; no blocking calls in async code; never hold a lock across `.await`. Logs go through `tracing`.
- Secrets: never commit one; never log a password or token. `ConnectParams.password` is a `secrecy::SecretString`. Never `Debug`-format a `pg_cdc_replication::ReplicationConfig`, which holds the password in a plain `String`.
- TLS is rustls with the ring provider only. OpenSSL, native-tls and aws-lc are banned in `deny.toml`.
- Dependencies: Task 1 declares every third-party dependency of the milestone in `[workspace.dependencies]` at the latest stable version as of 2026-10-14, with `default-features = false` where practical. Crates use `<dep>.workspace = true` and add features locally. A new dependency is added there with an exact `x.y.z` requirement. `Cargo.lock` is committed. When `cargo deny` rejects a license, replace the crate; never widen the allow list.
- Pinned session settings, sent as the startup `options` of every connection pg-cdc opens: `-c DateStyle=ISO,MDY -c TimeZone=UTC -c IntervalStyle=postgres -c extra_float_digits=1 -c bytea_output=hex` (spec §6.1).
- pg-cdc never creates, drops or advances a slot or publication except through standby status updates (spec §14.4, S1). Only tests create them, through `pg-cdc-testkit`.
- Event id: commit LSN as 16 lowercase hex digits, `-`, ordinal as 8 lowercase hex digits (`000000001a2b3c4d-00000003`). LSN text in envelopes: `{high:X}/{low:X}` (`0/1A2B3C4D`).
- Tests run with cargo-nextest. Unit tests sit beside the code; integration tests live in `crates/*/tests` and use `pg-cdc-testkit` (Docker required). `PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION` selects Postgres 14 to 18 and defaults to 17. Tests are deterministic and print nothing on success.
- Coverage floors, enforced by `mise run cov`: `pg-cdc-decoder` at least 90% of lines; the workspace at least 80% of lines excluding `crates/testkit`, `crates/loadgen` and the vendored `crates/replication`.
- Shared files: tasks in parallel lanes edit the same manifests (the root `Cargo.toml`, `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`). Keep every dependency table in alphabetical order, add only the lines the task needs, and when a merge conflicts in a manifest, keep the union of both sides (one entry per dependency, features merged) and regenerate `Cargo.lock` with `cargo metadata --format-version 1 > /dev/null` rather than hand-merging it.
- Commits: conventional commits (`feat`, `fix`, `test`, `refactor`, `perf`, `build`, `ci`, `docs`, `chore`), small and focused, on the task's own branch `task/m1-t<N>`. Every commit message ends with exactly:

  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_0111KrCFecpy7EHrGXLaNCHr
  ```

  Never push, never force-push, never touch `/Users/rajpatel/pg-cdc` or `main`.

## Rulings

Each ruling is "decision — why — cost if wrong".

- **R1. The transport patch also drops `messages 'true'`.** Upstream pgwire-replication 0.4.1 sends `messages 'true'` in `START_REPLICATION`; spec §6.2 and invariant D1 forbid negotiating `messages`. §5's "patched only to expose the backend PID" is read as "keep patches minimal", and NOTICE lists both patches. Cost if wrong: one extra line of divergence from upstream.
- **R2. The vendored crate is adapted mechanically, not rewritten.** It moves to edition 2024 with `cargo fix --edition`, is formatted by the workspace rustfmt, inherits workspace lints, and lists the lints its upstream code triggers in crate-level `#![expect]` attributes (the crate is the narrowest scope that fits third-party code). It keeps its upstream comments and dependency requirements. It drops the upstream examples, benches, integration test, the `integration-tests` and `md5` features, and the unused `anyhow` and `tokio-util` dependencies. Cost if wrong: a larger NOTICE and a harder upstream diff.
- **R3. The AWS emulator is moto `motoserver/moto:5.2.4`, not LocalStack.** The spec names LocalStack (§18.2) but §18.4 already admits another backend. Moto runs SNS, SQS and S3 in one image with no account or auth token, which suits a public repository's fork CI, and a probe on 2026-10-14 showed it returns 412 for `If-None-Match: *` on an existing key and for a stale `If-Match`. Task 2 pins this with a test. Cost if wrong: swap the image in `crates/testkit/src/aws.rs`.
- **R4. `oid` maps to a JSON number.** The §6.4 table says number; `to_jsonb` renders `oid` as a string. The specific table wins over the general rule. The reference test compares `oid` columns against `to_jsonb(col::int8)`. Cost if wrong: consumers of `oid` columns see a number instead of a string.
- **R5. `json` values are embedded as parsed, with numbers normalized.** Key order is kept as sent and duplicate keys keep the last value (as `jsonb` does); numbers are rewritten to the plain-decimal form `to_jsonb` produces (`1e2` becomes `100`). Cost if wrong: key order differs from `to_jsonb`, which no JSON consumer may rely on.
- **R6. Floats are rendered as `to_jsonb` renders them.** `to_jsonb` routes floats through `numeric`, so `1e+100` becomes 1 followed by 100 zeros, `1.5e-07` becomes `0.00000015`, and `-0` becomes `0`. Verified on Postgres 17 on 2026-10-14. Cost if wrong: very large or small floats change textual form.
- **R7. JSON nested deeper than serde_json's limit of 128 falls back to its text form** and counts as a decode fallback (D2). Cost if wrong: such documents arrive as strings.
- **R8. Logs go to stderr while the stdout sink is selected.** §17.1 says logs go to stdout; with the stdout sink, events and logs would interleave on one stream. The rule lives in one `match` on the sink type, so the SNS sink in M2 logs to stdout. Cost if wrong: one match arm.
- **R9. M1 acknowledges sequentially.** `AckProgress` holds one open transaction: an event counts as settled only from a `Delivered` receipt that only the sink's flush can create; Commit advances the confirmed LSN to its end LSN only with zero unsettled events; keepalives advance it only when no transaction is open. Milestone 2 replaces it with the full ack tracker. §17 metrics are milestone 5, so M1 logs the non-FULL identity warning and decode fallbacks but sets no metric. Cost if wrong: the M2 ack tracker replaces about 60 lines.
- **R10. Envelope LSN text is unpadded `X/X`**, the Postgres 14–17 notation; Postgres 18 pads the low half to 8 digits. Parsers in this plan accept both. Cost if wrong: cosmetic.
- **R11. The session seeds the transport's applied LSN with the slot's `confirmed_flush_lsn` as soon as `ReplicationClient::connect` returns,** before the worker can finish its startup and authentication round trips, so no status update reports a position below it (§9.2). The transport's applied LSN is monotonic, and `START_REPLICATION` still asks for `0/0` (§6.1). Cost if wrong: a first status update could report `0/0`, which Postgres ignores because `confirmed_flush_lsn` never moves backwards.
- **R12. Configuration is strict.** Unknown keys are rejected; later milestones add their sections. Environment overrides are strings, and `source.port` accepts a string or a number. Slot and publication names must match `[a-z0-9_]{1,63}` and the pipeline name `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,63}`, which keeps them safe to interpolate into `START_REPLICATION` and S3 keys. Durations are an integer followed by `ms`, `s`, `m` or `h`. Cost if wrong: a deployment with an uppercase slot name must rename it.
- **R13. Reconnect backoff is AWS full jitter:** a uniform delay in `[0, min(30 s, 1 s × 2^n)]`, reset after an attempt that lived at least 30 s (§11.7, §15). Cost if wrong: retry timing only.
- **R14. A key is `null` when any key column value is absent** from the image it is taken from (for example an unchanged TOAST key column). Cost if wrong: such events lose their key.
- **R15. `cargo deny` treats unmaintained advisories as errors for direct dependencies only** (`unmaintained = "workspace"`); vulnerable and yanked crates are denied everywhere. Cost if wrong: a transitive unmaintained crate is reported but does not fail the gate.
- **R16. `typos` skips `crates/replication/src/`** because it is vendored text. Cost if wrong: upstream typos go unreported.
- **R17. Task 1 creates every `crates/pg-cdc/src` module file empty** and declares them all in `lib.rs`, so later tasks never edit `lib.rs` and can run in parallel lanes. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R18. TLS modes mirror libpq:** `disable`, `require` (encrypt, verify nothing), `verify-ca` (verify the chain, not the host name) and `verify-full` (default). Cost if wrong: a mode name changes in config.
- **R19. An empty Relation namespace becomes `pg_catalog`,** as the pgoutput format documents. Cost if wrong: cosmetic.
- **R20. Signal handling is Unix-only** (`SIGTERM`, Ctrl-C). Cost if wrong: no Windows build, which the spec never asks for.
- **R21. The relayed user request "it should be public, not private" is honored:** the repository and its CI are treated as public throughout (no secrets in CI, fork-safe workflow, moto instead of an emulator that needs an account), and nothing in this plan publishes anything privately. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R22. `run_session` calls `sink.reset_pending()` before it creates the session's `AckProgress`.** The sink outlives sessions, so a session abandoned mid-transaction leaves written but unsettled events in its pending count; a fresh `AckProgress` would then be settled for more events than it sent, which is `OverSettled` and fatal. The reset flushes the abandoned lines (an at-least-once duplicate, since the server replays that transaction) and zeroes the count. Cost if wrong: a reconnect mid-transaction ends the process.

## Review Focus

The five inputs the spec implies but its listed tests do not exercise, most likely to bite first. Each is pinned by a test in the owning task.

1. **Database or role defaults for `TimeZone`, `DateStyle`, `IntervalStyle`, `bytea_output` or `extra_float_digits` differ from the pinned values.** Expected: envelopes are byte-identical to a default session. Tests: Task 7 `control_connection_pins_session_settings`, Task 11 `pinned_session_settings_override_database_defaults`.
2. **The schema changes mid-stream (`ADD COLUMN`, `DROP COLUMN`).** Expected: the next Relation message replaces the cached columns and keys, and later events carry the new shape. Test: Task 12 `schema_change_mid_stream_updates_relation`.
3. **Identifiers need quoting (spaces, capitals, non-ASCII).** Expected: envelopes carry the exact names and key lookups work, because catalog queries go by OID. Tests: Task 9 `key_columns_handle_quoted_identifiers`, Task 12 `quoted_identifiers_stream_with_exact_names`.
4. **One transaction holds tens of thousands of rows.** Expected: events stream in order without buffering the transaction, and the slot is acknowledged only after the last one is flushed. Test: Task 12 `o2_large_transaction_streams_in_stream_order`.
5. **The password contains characters such as space, `@`, `:`, `/`, quotes, `&`, `=` and `%`, or comes from an IAM token.** Expected: both connections authenticate, and neither the password nor the token appears in `Debug` output or logs. Tests: Task 6 `password_is_redacted_in_debug`, Task 7 `connects_with_special_character_password` and `connect_params_debug_redacts_password`, Task 11 `special_character_password_streams`, Task 13 `password_in_config_file_is_rejected`.

## Invariants Pinned in This Milestone

| Invariant | Test (task) |
|---|---|
| D1 closed message set | `start_replication_negotiates_no_optional_features` (3), `d1_every_message_in_the_closed_set_decodes` and `d1_unknown_tag_is_rejected` (4) |
| D2 total value decoding | `d2_map_value_is_total` (5), `d2_to_jsonb_reference_matches_every_builtin_type` (9) |
| D3 malformed input is a fault, three at one LSN halt | `d3_truncated_messages_are_errors_not_panics` (4), `d3_tuple_with_wrong_column_count_is_a_fault` (8), `d3_three_consecutive_faults_at_same_lsn_halt` (13) |
| D4 caches per connection | `d4_reconnect_rebuilds_relation_cache_and_replays_unacknowledged` (13) |
| O1 ids depend only on the WAL | `o1_event_ids_are_deterministic_and_strictly_increasing` (8), `o1_replayed_transaction_keeps_its_ids` (11) |
| O2 per-key order at the sink | `o2_large_transaction_streams_in_stream_order` (12) |
| A1 only delivery settles | `a1_only_a_delivery_receipt_settles_events` (10), `a_new_session_starts_from_a_sink_with_no_pending_events` (11) |
| A2 confirmed LSN is safe | `a2_random_transactions_keep_the_confirmed_lsn_safe` (10), `a2_slot_not_acknowledged_before_delivery` (11) |
| A3 keepalives only when idle | `a3_keepalive_ignored_while_transaction_open` (10), `a3_idle_publication_advances_slot` (11) |
| A4 status updates are independent | `a4_status_updates_continue_while_consumer_stalls` (3) |
| S1 never creates a slot | `s1_missing_slot_is_never_created` (13) |

O3 and L1–L3 belong to milestones 3 and 4; M1 has no parking and no leadership.

---
### Task 1: Workspace, toolchain and quality gate
**Depends on:** none
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** normal

Builds the foundation every later task validates with: the Cargo workspace with the `pg-cdc` crate skeleton, pinned toolchain and tools, lints, supply-chain policy, CI, licenses and the engineering standards document. After this task `mise run check` passes on a compiling workspace.

**Files:**
- Create: `Cargo.toml`, `rust-toolchain.toml`, `mise.toml`, `rustfmt.toml`, `clippy.toml`, `deny.toml`, `_typos.toml`, `.taplo.toml`, `.gitignore`, `.config/nextest.toml`
- Create: `LICENSE-MIT`, `LICENSE-APACHE`, `README.md`, `docs/engineering.md`
- Create: `.github/dependabot.yml`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lib.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/main.rs`
- Create (empty files): `crates/pg-cdc/src/ack.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/catalog.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/config.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/session.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/sink.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/supervisor.rs`
- Test: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/workspace_policy.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces: the workspace (`members = ["crates/*"]`, `exclude = ["fuzz"]`); `[workspace.dependencies]` entries named exactly as below; mise tasks `fmt`, `lint`, `test`, `test-integration`, `check`, `actionlint`, `cov`, `fuzz-smoke`, `ci`; the module list of `pg_cdc` (`ack`, `catalog`, `config`, `connection`, `event`, `session`, `sink`, `supervisor`), each an empty file owned by a later task.

- [ ] **Step 1: Pin the toolchain and tools**

`rust-toolchain.toml`:

```toml
[toolchain]
channel = "1.99.0"
components = ["rustfmt", "clippy", "llvm-tools-preview"]
profile = "minimal"
```

`mise.toml`:

```toml
[tools]
cargo-binstall = "1.25.3"
"cargo:cargo-nextest" = "0.9.148"
cargo-deny = "0.20.2"
"cargo:cargo-machete" = "0.9.2"
typos = "1.51.1"
taplo = "0.10.0"
actionlint = "1.7.12"
"cargo:cargo-llvm-cov" = "0.9.1"
"cargo:cargo-fuzz" = "0.13.2"

[env]
FUZZ_TOOLCHAIN = "nightly-2026-10-01"

[tasks.fmt]
description = "Format Rust and TOML sources"
run = ["cargo fmt --all", "taplo fmt"]

[tasks.lint]
description = "The gate without tests"
env = { RUSTDOCFLAGS = "-D warnings" }
run = [
  "cargo fmt --all --check",
  "taplo fmt --check",
  "typos",
  "cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings",
  "cargo doc --workspace --no-deps --all-features",
  "cargo deny check",
  "cargo machete",
]

[tasks.test]
description = "All tests, Docker-backed integration tests included"
run = "cargo nextest run --workspace --all-features"

[tasks.test-integration]
description = "Integration tests only, for the Postgres version matrix"
run = "cargo nextest run --workspace --all-features -E 'kind(test)'"

[tasks.check]
description = "The single local gate"
run = ["mise run lint", "mise run test"]

[tasks.actionlint]
description = "Lint GitHub Actions workflows"
run = "actionlint"

[tasks.cov]
description = "Coverage with floors: decoder 90%, workspace 80%"
run = '''
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
cargo llvm-cov clean --workspace
cargo llvm-cov nextest --workspace --all-features --no-report
cargo llvm-cov report --ignore-filename-regex '(crates/(testkit|loadgen|replication)/|/tests/)' --fail-under-lines 80
if [ -d crates/decoder ]; then
  cargo llvm-cov report --ignore-filename-regex '(crates/(pg-cdc|replication|testkit|loadgen)/|/tests/)' --fail-under-lines 90
fi
'''

[tasks.fuzz-smoke]
description = "Run every fuzz target for 60 seconds on the pinned nightly"
run = '''
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
if [ ! -d fuzz ]; then
  exit 0
fi
rustup toolchain install "$FUZZ_TOOLCHAIN" --profile minimal
for target in $(cargo +"$FUZZ_TOOLCHAIN" fuzz list); do
  cargo +"$FUZZ_TOOLCHAIN" fuzz run "$target" -- -max_total_time=60
done
'''

[tasks.ci]
description = "Everything CI runs, locally"
run = ["mise run lint", "mise run actionlint", "mise run test", "mise run cov"]
```

```rust
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

#[test]
fn every_member_crate_inherits_workspace_policy() {
    let crates_dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("..");
    let mut checked = 0;
    for entry in fs::read_dir(&crates_dir).unwrap() {
        let manifest = entry.unwrap().path().join("Cargo.toml");
        if !manifest.exists() {
            continue;
        }
        let text = fs::read_to_string(&manifest).unwrap();
        for required in [
            "[lints]\nworkspace = true",
            "license.workspace = true",
            "edition.workspace = true",
            "rust-version.workspace = true",
        ] {
            assert!(
                text.contains(required),
                "{} must contain {required:?}",
                manifest.display()
            );
        }
        checked += 1;
    }
    assert!(checked >= 1, "no member crates found in {}", crates_dir.display());
}
```

`README.md`:

````markdown
# pg-cdc

pg-cdc streams row changes for the tables in one Postgres publication through a logical replication slot to a sink. Delivery is at least once, and pg-cdc never writes to the source database.

Status: milestone 1 of the [design](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md). Changes stream to stdout as JSON lines, and the slot is acknowledged after each transaction is written.

## Develop

```sh
mise trust && mise install
mise run check
```

`mise run check` is the only gate. Integration tests need Docker; `PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION` selects Postgres 14 to 18 (default 17). See [docs/engineering.md](docs/engineering.md).

## License

Licensed under either of [Apache License, Version 2.0](LICENSE-APACHE) or [MIT license](LICENSE-MIT) at your option. Contributions are dual licensed the same way.
````

`docs/engineering.md`:

```markdown
# Engineering standards

These rules bind every change. `mise run check` enforces most of them.

## Toolchain

- Rust 1.99.0, pinned in `rust-toolchain.toml` with rustfmt, clippy and llvm-tools-preview. Edition 2024; workspace `rust-version = "1.99"`.
- Nightly is used only for cargo-fuzz. `fuzz/` sits outside the workspace.
- Dev tools are pinned to exact versions in `mise.toml`. Every gate is a mise task, and CI installs the same tools with jdx/mise-action and runs the same tasks. Run `mise trust && mise install` once per checkout.

## The gate

`mise run check` runs, in order: `cargo fmt --check`, `taplo fmt --check`, `typos`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo doc --workspace --no-deps` with `RUSTDOCFLAGS="-D warnings"`, `cargo nextest run --workspace --all-features` (Docker-backed integration tests included), `cargo deny check` and `cargo machete`. Work is not done until it passes.

## Lints

- Lints live in `[workspace.lints]`; every crate sets `[lints] workspace = true`.
- Rust: `unsafe_code` forbid, `unused_must_use` deny, `missing_debug_implementations` and `unreachable_pub` warn, `rust_2018_idioms` warn.
- Clippy: `all` and `pedantic` deny; `module_name_repetitions`, `missing_errors_doc`, `missing_panics_doc` and `must_use_candidate` allowed; `unwrap_used`, `expect_used`, `panic`, `todo`, `unimplemented`, `dbg_macro`, `print_stdout`, `print_stderr` and `indexing_slicing` deny. `clippy.toml` allows unwrap, expect, panic, print and indexing in tests.
- An exception is `#[expect(<lint>, reason = "...")]` at the narrowest scope. Never weaken a lint to pass the gate.

## Supply chain

- `deny.toml` denies vulnerable and yanked crates, allows only Apache-2.0, MIT, BSD-2-Clause, BSD-3-Clause, ISC, Unicode-3.0, Zlib, CDLA-Permissive-2.0 and MPL-2.0, allows only crates.io, and bans wildcard versions, OpenSSL, native-tls and aws-lc.
- `Cargo.lock` is committed. TLS is rustls with the ring provider.
- Dependencies are declared once in `[workspace.dependencies]` at exact versions, with `default-features = false` where practical.

## Code

- No code comments, doc comments included, except one that states a genuine exception or anomaly the code cannot express. History and rationale belong in commit messages and pull requests.
- `thiserror` for library errors; `anyhow` only in `main` and the CLI. No panics outside tests.
- tokio for async; no blocking in async code; no lock held across `.await`. `tracing` for logs.
- Never commit secrets and never log credentials.

## Tests

- cargo-nextest runs everything. Unit tests sit beside the code; integration tests live in `crates/*/tests` and use `crates/testkit` (package `pg-cdc-testkit`).
- proptest for property tests; fuzz targets live in `fuzz/`.
- Tests assert behavior, are deterministic and print nothing on success.
- Coverage floors, enforced in CI by `mise run cov`: `pg-cdc-decoder` at least 90% of lines; the workspace at least 80%, excluding testkit, loadgen and the vendored replication crate.

## CI

`.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs on pull requests, pushes to `main` and manual dispatch, with one concurrency group per ref and top-level `contents: read`. Third-party actions are pinned to commit SHAs. Jobs: `lint` (the gate minus tests, plus actionlint), `test` (Postgres 17), `pg-matrix` (Postgres 14, 15, 16 and 18), `coverage` and `fuzz-smoke` (60 seconds per target). Dependabot updates cargo and GitHub Actions weekly. The repository is public, so no job needs a secret.

## Commits

Conventional commits (`feat`, `fix`, `test`, `refactor`, `perf`, `build`, `ci`, `docs`, `chore`), small and focused. Commits written with Claude end with its `Co-Authored-By` and `Claude-Session` lines.
```

```yaml
version: 2
updates:

    schedule:
      interval: weekly
  - package-ecosystem: github-actions
    directory: /

```

`.github/workflows/ci.yml`:

```yaml
name: ci

on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

permissions:
  contents: read

env:
  CARGO_TERM_COLOR: always

jobs:
  lint:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: jdx/mise-action@08ae2a091c038e1e05ec6f5115dc5c69d5a2a6b8 # v5.1.2
        with:
          version: 2026.10.9
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@6323deb102c322ba6fcbdcafc7e3dddab59af2b6 # v2.9.2
      - run: mise run lint
      - run: mise run actionlint

  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 45
    env:
      PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION: "17"
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: jdx/mise-action@08ae2a091c038e1e05ec6f5115dc5c69d5a2a6b8 # v5.1.2
        with:
          version: 2026.10.9
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@6323deb102c322ba6fcbdcafc7e3dddab59af2b6 # v2.9.2
      - run: mise run test

  pg-matrix:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 45
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        pg: ["14", "15", "16", "18"]
    env:
      PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION: ${{ matrix.pg }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: jdx/mise-action@08ae2a091c038e1e05ec6f5115dc5c69d5a2a6b8 # v5.1.2
        with:
          version: 2026.10.9
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@6323deb102c322ba6fcbdcafc7e3dddab59af2b6 # v2.9.2
      - run: mise run test-integration

  coverage:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 45
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: jdx/mise-action@08ae2a091c038e1e05ec6f5115dc5c69d5a2a6b8 # v5.1.2
        with:
          version: 2026.10.9
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@6323deb102c322ba6fcbdcafc7e3dddab59af2b6 # v2.9.2
      - run: mise run cov

  fuzz-smoke:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: jdx/mise-action@08ae2a091c038e1e05ec6f5115dc5c69d5a2a6b8 # v5.1.2
        with:
          version: 2026.10.9
      - run: mise run fuzz-smoke
```

---

### Task 2: Test harness crate
**Depends on:** Task 1
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** normal

Creates `crates/testkit` (package `pg-cdc-testkit`, `publish = false`): a Postgres container with `wal_level=logical` for versions 14 to 18, SQL and slot helpers, raw pgoutput capture for golden fixtures, a TLS-enabled Postgres variant, and a moto container for SNS, SQS and S3 whose conditional writes are proven by a test (Ruling R3).

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/testkit/Cargo.toml`, `crates/testkit/src/lib.rs`, `crates/testkit/src/error.rs`, `crates/testkit/src/postgres.rs`, `crates/testkit/src/tls.rs`, `crates/testkit/src/fixture.rs`, `crates/testkit/src/aws.rs`
- Modify: `Cargo.toml` (add `pg-cdc-testkit = { path = "crates/testkit", version = "0.1.0" }` to `[workspace.dependencies]`)
- Test: `crates/testkit/tests/postgres.rs`, `crates/testkit/tests/aws.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 workspace dependencies.
- Produces (all re-exported from the crate root):
  - `pub const PG_VERSION_ENV: &str = "PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION"`, `pub const DEFAULT_PG_VERSION: u8 = 17`, `pub const SESSION_OPTIONS: &str` (the pinned settings string)
  - `pub fn parse_pg_version(value: Option<&str>) -> Result<u8, TestkitError>`; `pub fn parse_lsn(text: &str) -> Result<u64, TestkitError>` (accepts `0/16B3748` and `0/016B3748`)
  - `pub struct PostgresOptions { pub tls: bool }` (`Default`, `Clone`, `Copy`)
  - `pub struct SlotState { pub confirmed_flush_lsn: Option<u64>, pub active: bool, pub active_pid: Option<i32>, pub wal_status: Option<String> }`
  - `pub struct Postgres` with `start() -> Result<Self, TestkitError>`, `start_with(PostgresOptions)`, `host() -> &'static str` (`"127.0.0.1"`), `port() -> u16`, `user()`, `password()`, `database()` (all `"postgres"`), `version() -> u8`, `ca_file() -> Option<&Path>`, `client() -> Result<tokio_postgres::Client, _>` (NoTls, pinned settings), `execute(&str)` (one `batch_execute` on a fresh client; several statements in one call run as one transaction), `create_publication(name, &[&str])`, `create_slot(name) -> Result<u64, _>`, `slot_state(name) -> Result<Option<SlotState>, _>`, `wait_for_slot(name, Duration, impl Fn(&SlotState) -> bool) -> Result<SlotState, _>`, `current_wal_lsn() -> Result<u64, _>`, `capture_pgoutput(slot, publication) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, _>` (consumes the changes, runs with the pinned settings), `terminate_walsender(slot) -> Result<bool, _>`
  - `pub fn write_fixture(path: &Path, messages: &[Vec<u8>]) -> Result<(), TestkitError>`; `pub fn read_fixture(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, TestkitError>` (each message as a big-endian `u32` length then its bytes)
  - `pub const MOTO_IMAGE: &str = "motoserver/moto"`, `pub const MOTO_TAG: &str = "5.2.4"`; `pub struct AwsEmulator` with `start()`, `endpoint() -> &str`, `sdk_config() -> aws_config::SdkConfig` (async), `s3()`, `sns()`, `sqs()` (async, return the SDK clients; S3 uses path-style addressing)
  - `pub enum TestkitError` (thiserror)

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

`crates/testkit/tests/postgres.rs`:

```rust
use pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, PostgresOptions, parse_lsn, parse_pg_version, read_fixture, write_fixture};

type TestResult = Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

#[test]
fn version_defaults_to_17_and_rejects_out_of_range() {
    assert_eq!(parse_pg_version(None).unwrap(), 17);
    assert_eq!(parse_pg_version(Some("14")).unwrap(), 14);
    assert_eq!(parse_pg_version(Some("18")).unwrap(), 18);
    for bad in ["13", "19", "latest", ""] {
        assert!(parse_pg_version(Some(bad)).is_err(), "{bad:?} must be rejected");
    }
}

#[test]
fn lsn_parsing_accepts_padded_and_unpadded_forms() {
    assert_eq!(parse_lsn("0/1A2B3C4D").unwrap(), 0x1A2B_3C4D);
    assert_eq!(parse_lsn("0/016B3748").unwrap(), 0x016B_3748);
    assert_eq!(parse_lsn("1/FF").unwrap(), 0x1_0000_00FF);
    for bad in ["", "1", "x/1", "1/", "/1", "1/100000000"] {
        assert!(parse_lsn(bad).is_err(), "{bad:?} must be rejected");
    }
}

#[test]
fn fixtures_round_trip_length_prefixed_messages() -> TestResult {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
    let path = dir.path().join("nested").join("scenario.bin");
    let messages = vec![b"B123".to_vec(), Vec::new(), vec![7u8; 300]];
    write_fixture(&path, &messages)?;
    assert_eq!(read_fixture(&path)?, messages);
    std::fs::write(&path, [0, 0, 0, 9, 1])?;
    assert!(read_fixture(&path).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn starts_requested_version_with_logical_wal_level_and_pinned_settings() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    let client = pg.client().await?;
    let wal_level: String = client.query_one("SHOW wal_level", &[]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    assert_eq!(wal_level, "logical");
    let version: String = client.query_one("SHOW server_version_num", &[]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    assert_eq!(version.get(..2), Some(pg.version().to_string().as_str()));
    let timezone: String = client.query_one("SHOW TimeZone", &[]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    assert_eq!(timezone, "UTC");
    let autovacuum: String = client.query_one("SHOW autovacuum", &[]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    assert_eq!(autovacuum, "off");
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn slot_state_and_capture_follow_the_slot() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute("CREATE TABLE probe (id int PRIMARY KEY)").await?;
    pg.create_publication("probe_pub", &["probe"]).await?;
    let created = pg.create_slot("probe_slot").await?;
    let state = pg.slot_state("probe_slot").await?.ok_or("slot missing")?;
    assert!(!state.active);
    assert_eq!(state.active_pid, None);
    assert_eq!(state.wal_status.as_deref(), Some("reserved"));
    assert!(state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn >= created));
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO probe VALUES (1)").await?;
    let messages = pg.capture_pgoutput("probe_slot", "probe_pub").await?;
    let tags: Vec<u8> = messages.iter().filter_map(|message| message.first().copied()).collect();
    assert_eq!(tags, b"BRIC".to_vec());
    let after = pg.slot_state("probe_slot").await?.ok_or("slot missing")?;
    assert!(after.confirmed_flush_lsn > state.confirmed_flush_lsn);
    assert!(pg.slot_state("absent_slot").await?.is_none());
    assert!(!pg.terminate_walsender("probe_slot").await?);
    assert!(pg.current_wal_lsn().await? >= created);
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn tls_option_enables_ssl_with_generated_ca() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start_with(PostgresOptions { tls: true }).await?;
    let ssl: String = pg.client().await?.query_one("SHOW ssl", &[]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    assert_eq!(ssl, "on");
    let ca = std::fs::read_to_string(pg.ca_file().ok_or("no CA file")?)?;
    assert!(ca.starts_with("-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----"));
    Ok(())
}
```

`crates/testkit/tests/aws.rs`:

```rust
use aws_sdk_s3::error::SdkError;
use aws_sdk_s3::primitives::ByteStream;
use aws_sdk_sqs::types::QueueAttributeName;
use pg_cdc_testkit::AwsEmulator;

type TestResult = Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

const BUCKET: &str = "pgcdc-conditional";
const KEY: &str = "leader.json";

fn status_of<T, E>(result: &Result<T, SdkError<E>>) -> Option<u16> {
    result
        .as_ref()
        .err()
        .and_then(SdkError::raw_response)
        .map(|response| response.status().as_u16())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn s3_conditional_writes_are_honored() -> TestResult {
    let aws = AwsEmulator::start().await?;
    let s3 = aws.s3().await;
    s3.create_bucket().bucket(BUCKET).send().await?;
    let created = s3
        .put_object()
        .bucket(BUCKET)
        .key(KEY)
        .body(ByteStream::from_static(b"1"))
        .if_none_match("*")
        .send()
        .await?;
    let etag = created.e_tag().ok_or("missing etag")?.to_owned();
    let duplicate = s3
        .put_object()
        .bucket(BUCKET)
        .key(KEY)
        .body(ByteStream::from_static(b"2"))
        .if_none_match("*")
        .send()
        .await;
    assert_eq!(status_of(&duplicate), Some(412));
    let stale = s3
        .put_object()
        .bucket(BUCKET)
        .key(KEY)
        .body(ByteStream::from_static(b"3"))
        .if_match("\"00000000000000000000000000000000\"")
        .send()
        .await;
    assert_eq!(status_of(&stale), Some(412));
    s3.put_object()
        .bucket(BUCKET)
        .key(KEY)
        .body(ByteStream::from_static(b"4"))
        .if_match(etag)
        .send()
        .await?;
    let body = s3.get_object().bucket(BUCKET).key(KEY).send().await?.body.collect().await?.into_bytes();
    assert_eq!(body.as_ref(), b"4");
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn sns_delivers_to_sqs_subscription() -> TestResult {
    let aws = AwsEmulator::start().await?;
    let sns = aws.sns().await;
    let sqs = aws.sqs().await;
    let queue_url = sqs
        .create_queue()
        .queue_name("pgcdc-events")
        .send()
        .await?
        .queue_url()
        .ok_or("missing queue url")?
        .to_owned();
    let attributes = sqs
        .get_queue_attributes()
        .queue_url(&queue_url)
        .attribute_names(QueueAttributeName::QueueArn)
        .send()
        .await?;
    let queue_arn = attributes
        .attributes()
        .and_then(|map| map.get(&QueueAttributeName::QueueArn))
        .ok_or("missing queue arn")?
        .clone();
    let topic_arn = sns
        .create_topic()
        .name("pgcdc-events")
        .send()
        .await?
        .topic_arn()
        .ok_or("missing topic arn")?
        .to_owned();
    sns.subscribe()
        .topic_arn(&topic_arn)
        .protocol("sqs")
        .endpoint(&queue_arn)
        .attributes("RawMessageDelivery", "true")
        .send()
        .await?;
    sns.publish().topic_arn(&topic_arn).message(r#"{"op":"insert"}"#).send().await?;
    let received = sqs
        .receive_message()
        .queue_url(&queue_url)
        .wait_time_seconds(5)
        .max_number_of_messages(1)
        .send()
        .await?;
    let bodies: Vec<&str> = received.messages().iter().filter_map(|message| message.body()).collect();
    assert_eq!(bodies, vec![r#"{"op":"insert"}"#]);
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum TestkitError {
    #[error("PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION must be 14 to 18, got {0:?}")]
    UnsupportedVersion(String),
    #[error("container: {0}")]
    Container(#[from] testcontainers::TestcontainersError),
    #[error("postgres: {0}")]
    Postgres(#[from] tokio_postgres::Error),
    #[error("timed out waiting for {0}")]
    Timeout(&'static str),
    #[error("invalid LSN {0:?}")]
    InvalidLsn(String),
    #[error("io: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
    #[error("fixture {0} is malformed")]
    MalformedFixture(String),
    #[error("certificate: {0}")]
    Certificate(#[from] rcgen::Error),
}
```

`crates/testkit/src/fixture.rs`:

```rust
use std::path::Path;

use crate::error::TestkitError;

pub fn write_fixture(path: &Path, messages: &[Vec<u8>]) -> Result<(), TestkitError> {
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    for message in messages {
        let len = u32::try_from(message.len())
            .map_err(|_| TestkitError::MalformedFixture(path.display().to_string()))?;
        bytes.extend_from_slice(&len.to_be_bytes());
        bytes.extend_from_slice(message);
    }
    std::fs::write(path, bytes)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub fn read_fixture(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, TestkitError> {
    let bytes = std::fs::read(path)?;
    let malformed = || TestkitError::MalformedFixture(path.display().to_string());
    let mut rest = bytes.as_slice();
    let mut messages = Vec::new();
    while !rest.is_empty() {
        let (len, tail) = rest.split_first_chunk::<4>().ok_or_else(malformed)?;
        let len = usize::try_from(u32::from_be_bytes(*len)).map_err(|_| malformed())?;
        let (message, tail) = tail.split_at_checked(len).ok_or_else(malformed)?;
        messages.push(message.to_vec());
        rest = tail;
    }
    Ok(messages)
}
```

`crates/testkit/src/tls.rs` (a CA plus a server certificate for `localhost` only, so tests can connect to `127.0.0.1` to exercise a host-name mismatch):

```rust
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use rcgen::{BasicConstraints, CertificateParams, DnType, IsCa, Issuer, KeyPair};
use tempfile::TempDir;

use crate::error::TestkitError;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub(crate) struct TestCertificates {
    server_pem: String,
    server_key_pem: String,
    ca_file: PathBuf,
    _dir: TempDir,
}

impl TestCertificates {
    pub(crate) fn generate() -> Result<Self, TestkitError> {
        let ca_key = KeyPair::generate()?;
        let mut ca_params = CertificateParams::new(Vec::<String>::new())?;
        ca_params.is_ca = IsCa::Ca(BasicConstraints::Unconstrained);
        ca_params.distinguished_name.push(DnType::CommonName, "pg-cdc test CA");
        let ca_cert = ca_params.self_signed(&ca_key)?;
        let issuer = Issuer::new(ca_params, ca_key);
        let server_key = KeyPair::generate()?;
        let server_cert = CertificateParams::new(vec!["localhost".to_owned()])?.signed_by(&server_key, &issuer)?;
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
        let ca_file = dir.path().join("ca.pem");
        std::fs::write(&ca_file, ca_cert.pem())?;
        Ok(Self {
            server_pem: server_cert.pem(),
            server_key_pem: server_key.serialize_pem(),
            ca_file,
            _dir: dir,
        })
    }

    pub(crate) fn server_pem(&self) -> &str {
        &self.server_pem
    }

    pub(crate) fn server_key_pem(&self) -> &str {
        &self.server_key_pem
    }

    pub(crate) fn ca_file(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.ca_file
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the Postgres container**

`crates/testkit/src/postgres.rs`:

```rust
use std::path::Path;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use testcontainers::core::{ContainerPort, WaitFor};
use testcontainers::runners::AsyncRunner;
use testcontainers::{ContainerAsync, GenericImage, ImageExt};
use tokio_postgres::{Client, NoTls};

use crate::error::TestkitError;
use crate::tls::TestCertificates;

pub const PG_VERSION_ENV: &str = "PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION";
pub const DEFAULT_PG_VERSION: u8 = 17;
pub const SESSION_OPTIONS: &str =
    "-c DateStyle=ISO,MDY -c TimeZone=UTC -c IntervalStyle=postgres -c extra_float_digits=1 -c bytea_output=hex";

const HOST: &str = "127.0.0.1";
const USER: &str = "postgres";
const PASSWORD: &str = "postgres";
const DATABASE: &str = "postgres";
const READY_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(90);
const POLL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);
const SERVER_ARGS: [&str; 11] = [
    "postgres",
    "-c",
    "wal_level=logical",
    "-c",
    "fsync=off",
    "-c",
    "autovacuum=off",
    "-c",
    "max_wal_senders=20",
    "-c",
    "max_replication_slots=20",
];

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PostgresOptions {
    pub tls: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SlotState {
    pub confirmed_flush_lsn: Option<u64>,
    pub active: bool,
    pub active_pid: Option<i32>,
    pub wal_status: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Postgres {
    _container: ContainerAsync<GenericImage>,
    port: u16,
    version: u8,
    certificates: Option<TestCertificates>,
}

pub fn parse_pg_version(value: Option<&str>) -> Result<u8, TestkitError> {
    let Some(text) = value else {
        return Ok(DEFAULT_PG_VERSION);
    };
    match text.trim().parse::<u8>() {
        Ok(version @ 14..=18) => Ok(version),
        _ => Err(TestkitError::UnsupportedVersion(text.to_owned())),
    }
}

pub fn parse_lsn(text: &str) -> Result<u64, TestkitError> {
    let invalid = || TestkitError::InvalidLsn(text.to_owned());
    let (high, low) = text.split_once('/').ok_or_else(invalid)?;
    let high = u32::from_str_radix(high, 16).map_err(|_| invalid())?;
    let low = u32::from_str_radix(low, 16).map_err(|_| invalid())?;
    Ok((u64::from(high) << 32) | u64::from(low))
}

fn tls_script() -> String {
    format!(
        "set -e; mkdir -p /etc/pgtls; cp /tls/server.crt /tls/server.key /etc/pgtls/; \
         chown -R postgres:postgres /etc/pgtls; chmod 600 /etc/pgtls/server.key; \
         exec docker-entrypoint.sh {} -c ssl=on \
         -c ssl_cert_file=/etc/pgtls/server.crt -c ssl_key_file=/etc/pgtls/server.key",
        SERVER_ARGS.join(" ")
    )
}

impl Postgres {
    pub async fn start() -> Result<Self, TestkitError> {
        Self::start_with(PostgresOptions::default()).await
    }

    pub async fn start_with(options: PostgresOptions) -> Result<Self, TestkitError> {
        let version = parse_pg_version(std::env::var(PG_VERSION_ENV).ok().as_deref())?;
        let image = GenericImage::new("postgres", &format!("{version}-alpine"))
            .with_exposed_port(ContainerPort::Tcp(5432))
            .with_wait_for(WaitFor::Nothing);
        let (container, certificates) = if options.tls {
            let certificates = TestCertificates::generate()?;
            let container = image
                .with_entrypoint("sh")
                .with_env_var("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", PASSWORD)
                .with_copy_to("/tls/server.crt", certificates.server_pem().as_bytes().to_vec())
                .with_copy_to("/tls/server.key", certificates.server_key_pem().as_bytes().to_vec())
                .with_cmd(["-c".to_owned(), tls_script()])
                .start()
                .await?;
            (container, Some(certificates))
        } else {
            let container = image
                .with_env_var("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", PASSWORD)
                .with_cmd(SERVER_ARGS)
                .start()
                .await?;
            (container, None)
        };
        let port = container.get_host_port_ipv4(5432).await?;
        let postgres = Self { _container: container, port, version, certificates };
        postgres.wait_until_ready().await?;
        Ok(postgres)
    }

    pub fn host(&self) -> &'static str {
        HOST
    }

    pub fn port(&self) -> u16 {
        self.port
    }

    pub fn user(&self) -> &'static str {
        USER
    }

    pub fn password(&self) -> &'static str {
        PASSWORD
    }

    pub fn database(&self) -> &'static str {
        DATABASE
    }

    pub fn version(&self) -> u8 {
        self.version
    }

    pub fn ca_file(&self) -> Option<&Path> {
        self.certificates.as_ref().map(TestCertificates::ca_file)
    }

    pub async fn client(&self) -> Result<Client, TestkitError> {
        let mut config = tokio_postgres::Config::new();
        config
            .host(HOST)
            .port(self.port)
            .user(USER)
            .password(PASSWORD)
            .dbname(DATABASE)
            .options(SESSION_OPTIONS);
        let (client, connection) = config.connect(NoTls).await?;
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let _ = connection.await;
        });
        Ok(client)
    }

    pub async fn execute(&self, sql: &str) -> Result<(), TestkitError> {
        self.client().await?.batch_execute(sql).await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn create_publication(&self, name: &str, tables: &[&str]) -> Result<(), TestkitError> {
        self.execute(&format!("CREATE PUBLICATION {name} FOR TABLE {}", tables.join(", ")))
            .await
    }

    pub async fn create_slot(&self, name: &str) -> Result<u64, TestkitError> {
        let row = self
            .client()
            .await?
            .query_one(
                "SELECT lsn::text FROM pg_create_logical_replication_slot($1, 'pgoutput')",
                &[&name],
            )
            .await?;
        parse_lsn(&row.try_get::<_, String>(0)?)
    }

    pub async fn slot_state(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Option<SlotState>, TestkitError> {
        let row = self
            .client()
            .await?
            .query_opt(
                "SELECT confirmed_flush_lsn::text, active, active_pid, wal_status \
                 FROM pg_replication_slots WHERE slot_name = $1",
                &[&name],
            )
            .await?;
        let Some(row) = row else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let lsn: Option<String> = row.try_get(0)?;
        Ok(Some(SlotState {
            confirmed_flush_lsn: lsn.as_deref().map(parse_lsn).transpose()?,
            active: row.try_get(1)?,
            active_pid: row.try_get(2)?,
            wal_status: row.try_get(3)?,
        }))
    }

    pub async fn wait_for_slot(
        &self,
        name: &str,
        timeout: Duration,
        ready: impl Fn(&SlotState) -> bool,
    ) -> Result<SlotState, TestkitError> {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + timeout;
        loop {
            if let Some(state) = self.slot_state(name).await?
                && ready(&state)
            {
                return Ok(state);
            }
            if Instant::now() >= deadline {
                return Err(TestkitError::Timeout("replication slot state"));
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(POLL).await;
        }
    }

    pub async fn current_wal_lsn(&self) -> Result<u64, TestkitError> {
        let row = self
            .client()
            .await?
            .query_one("SELECT pg_current_wal_lsn()::text", &[])
            .await?;
        parse_lsn(&row.try_get::<_, String>(0)?)
    }

    pub async fn capture_pgoutput(&self, slot: &str, publication: &str) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, TestkitError> {
        let rows = self
            .client()
            .await?
            .query(
                "SELECT data FROM pg_logical_slot_get_binary_changes($1, NULL, NULL, \
                 'proto_version', '1', 'publication_names', $2)",
                &[&slot, &publication],
            )
            .await?;
        rows.iter()
            .map(|row| row.try_get::<_, Vec<u8>>(0).map_err(TestkitError::from))
            .collect()
    }

    pub async fn terminate_walsender(&self, slot: &str) -> Result<bool, TestkitError> {
        let row = self
            .client()
            .await?
            .query_one(
                "SELECT coalesce(bool_or(pg_terminate_backend(active_pid)), false) \
                 FROM pg_replication_slots WHERE slot_name = $1 AND active_pid IS NOT NULL",
                &[&slot],
            )
            .await?;
        Ok(row.try_get(0)?)
    }

    async fn wait_until_ready(&self) -> Result<(), TestkitError> {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + READY_TIMEOUT;
        loop {
            if let Ok(client) = self.client().await
                && client.simple_query("SELECT 1").await.is_ok()
            {
                return Ok(());
            }
            if Instant::now() >= deadline {
                return Err(TestkitError::Timeout("postgres to accept connections"));
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(250)).await;
        }
    }
}
```

The official image starts a temporary server on a Unix socket only while it initializes, so polling TCP connections waits for the real server. The TLS variant starts the same `SERVER_ARGS` (which begin with `postgres`) through the image's entrypoint, so both variants share one list of server settings.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the AWS emulator and the crate root**

`crates/testkit/src/aws.rs`:

```rust
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use aws_config::{BehaviorVersion, Region, SdkConfig};
use aws_sdk_s3::config::Credentials;
use aws_smithy_http_client::tls::{Provider, rustls_provider::CryptoMode};
use testcontainers::core::{ContainerPort, WaitFor};
use testcontainers::runners::AsyncRunner;
use testcontainers::{ContainerAsync, GenericImage};

use crate::error::TestkitError;

pub const MOTO_IMAGE: &str = "motoserver/moto";
pub const MOTO_TAG: &str = "5.2.4";

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct AwsEmulator {
    _container: ContainerAsync<GenericImage>,
    endpoint: String,
}

impl AwsEmulator {
    pub async fn start() -> Result<Self, TestkitError> {
        let container = GenericImage::new(MOTO_IMAGE, MOTO_TAG)
            .with_exposed_port(ContainerPort::Tcp(5000))
            .with_wait_for(WaitFor::Nothing)
            .start()
            .await?;
        let port = container.get_host_port_ipv4(5000).await?;
        let emulator = Self { _container: container, endpoint: format!("http://127.0.0.1:{port}") };
        emulator.wait_until_ready().await?;
        Ok(emulator)
    }

    pub fn endpoint(&self) -> &str {
        &self.endpoint
    }

    pub async fn sdk_config(&self) -> SdkConfig {
        let http_client = aws_smithy_http_client::Builder::new()
            .tls_provider(Provider::Rustls(CryptoMode::Ring))
            .build_https();
        aws_config::defaults(BehaviorVersion::latest())
            .region(Region::new("us-east-1"))
            .credentials_provider(Credentials::new("test", "test", None, None, "pg-cdc-testkit"))
            .endpoint_url(&self.endpoint)
            .http_client(http_client)
            .load()
            .await
    }

    pub async fn s3(&self) -> aws_sdk_s3::Client {
        let config = self.sdk_config().await;
        aws_sdk_s3::Client::from_conf(aws_sdk_s3::config::Builder::from(&config).force_path_style(true).build())
    }

    pub async fn sns(&self) -> aws_sdk_sns::Client {
        aws_sdk_sns::Client::new(&self.sdk_config().await)
    }

    pub async fn sqs(&self) -> aws_sdk_sqs::Client {
        aws_sdk_sqs::Client::new(&self.sdk_config().await)
    }

    async fn wait_until_ready(&self) -> Result<(), TestkitError> {
        let s3 = self.s3().await;
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(60);
        loop {
            if s3.list_buckets().send().await.is_ok() {
                return Ok(());
            }
            if Instant::now() >= deadline {
                return Err(TestkitError::Timeout("moto to accept requests"));
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(250)).await;
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 3: Vendored replication transport
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 2
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Vendors pgwire-replication 0.4.1 (MIT OR Apache-2.0) from its crates.io source as `crates/replication` (package `pg-cdc-replication`), keeps its license files, adds a NOTICE of origin, and patches it to expose the walsender's backend PID from BackendKeyData and to stop negotiating `messages` (Rulings R1, R2). Its worker already sends standby status updates on its own timer, also while the consumer is backpressured, which is invariant A4; this task pins that with a test.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/replication/Cargo.toml`, `crates/replication/NOTICE`, `crates/replication/LICENSE-MIT`, `crates/replication/LICENSE-APACHE` (upstream, unchanged), `crates/replication/src/**` (upstream `src/` tree)
- Modify (vendored): `crates/replication/src/lib.rs` (lint attributes), `crates/replication/src/client/worker.rs` (BackendKeyData, `START_REPLICATION`), `crates/replication/src/client/tokio_client.rs` (`backend_pid`)
- Modify: `Cargo.toml` (add `pg-cdc-replication` to `[workspace.dependencies]`), `_typos.toml`
- Test: `crates/replication/tests/transport.rs`; unit tests appended to the `tests` module of `crates/replication/src/client/worker.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, SlotState}` (Task 2).
- Produces (crate `pg_cdc_replication`, otherwise the upstream 0.4.1 API):
  - `ReplicationClient::connect(ReplicationConfig) -> Result<ReplicationClient>`, `recv(&mut self) -> Result<Option<ReplicationEvent>>`, `update_applied_lsn(&self, Lsn)`, `stop(&self)`, `metrics(&self) -> Arc<ReplicationMetrics>`, and new `backend_pid(&self) -> Option<u32>` (`None` until authentication completes)
  - `ReplicationConfig::new(host, user, password, database, slot, publication)` with `with_port(u16)`, `with_tls(TlsConfig)`, `with_options(impl Into<String>)`, `with_status_interval(Duration)`, `with_wakeup_interval(Duration)`, `with_buffer_size(usize)`
  - `TlsConfig::{disabled(), require(), verify_ca(Option<PathBuf>), verify_full(Option<PathBuf>)}`
  - `ReplicationEvent::{KeepAlive { wal_end, reply_requested, server_time_micros }, Begin { final_lsn, xid, commit_time_micros }, XLogData { wal_start, wal_end, server_time_micros, data: Bytes }, Commit { lsn, end_lsn, commit_time_micros }, Message { .. }, StoppedAt { reached }}`; `Lsn(pub u64)`; `PgWireError` (`Clone + Send + Sync`)
  - `START_REPLICATION SLOT <slot> LOGICAL <start> (proto_version '1', publication_names '<publication>')` with no other option

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the integration tests**

`crates/replication/tests/transport.rs`:

```rust
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use pg_cdc_replication::{ReplicationClient, ReplicationConfig, ReplicationEvent, TlsConfig};
use pg_cdc_testkit::Postgres;

type TestResult = Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

const SETTINGS: &str = "-c DateStyle=ISO,MDY -c TimeZone=UTC";

fn config(pg: &Postgres, slot: &str, publication: &str) -> ReplicationConfig {
    ReplicationConfig::new(pg.host(), pg.user(), pg.password(), pg.database(), slot, publication)
        .with_port(pg.port())
        .with_tls(TlsConfig::disabled())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn backend_pid_matches_slot_active_pid() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute("CREATE TABLE pid_probe (id int PRIMARY KEY)").await?;
    pg.create_publication("pid_pub", &["pid_probe"]).await?;
    pg.create_slot("pid_slot").await?;
    let client = ReplicationClient::connect(config(&pg, "pid_slot", "pid_pub")).await?;
    let state = pg
        .wait_for_slot("pid_slot", Duration::from_secs(20), |state| state.active_pid.is_some())
        .await?;
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(20);
    while client.backend_pid().is_none() && Instant::now() < deadline {
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    }
    let pid = client.backend_pid().ok_or("backend pid never recorded")?;
    assert_eq!(state.active_pid, Some(i32::try_from(pid)?));
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a4_status_updates_continue_while_consumer_stalls() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute("CREATE TABLE stall (id int PRIMARY KEY)").await?;
    pg.create_publication("stall_pub", &["stall"]).await?;
    pg.create_slot("stall_slot").await?;
    let client = ReplicationClient::connect(
        config(&pg, "stall_slot", "stall_pub")
            .with_status_interval(Duration::from_millis(500))
            .with_wakeup_interval(Duration::from_millis(500))
            .with_buffer_size(1),
    )
    .await?;
    for id in 0..50 {
        pg.execute(&format!("INSERT INTO stall VALUES ({id})")).await?;
    }
    let state = pg
        .wait_for_slot("stall_slot", Duration::from_secs(20), |state| state.active_pid.is_some())
        .await?;
    let pid = state.active_pid.ok_or("no walsender")?;
    let monitor = pg.client().await?;
    let reply_epoch = "SELECT extract(epoch FROM reply_time)::float8 FROM pg_stat_replication WHERE pid = $1";
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
    let first: f64 = monitor.query_one(reply_epoch, &[&pid]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
    let second: f64 = monitor.query_one(reply_epoch, &[&pid]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    assert!(second > first, "no status update while stalled: {first} then {second}");
    assert!(client.metrics().stall_count() >= 1, "the consumer never stalled the worker");
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn startup_options_pin_session_settings() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute("ALTER DATABASE postgres SET timezone = 'America/New_York'").await?;
    pg.execute("CREATE TABLE tz_probe (id int PRIMARY KEY, at timestamptz)").await?;
    pg.create_publication("tz_pub", &["tz_probe"]).await?;
    pg.create_slot("tz_slot").await?;
    let mut client = ReplicationClient::connect(config(&pg, "tz_slot", "tz_pub").with_options(SETTINGS)).await?;
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO tz_probe VALUES (1, '2026-10-14 12:00:00+00')").await?;
    let insert = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(20), async {
        loop {
            match client.recv().await {
                Ok(Some(ReplicationEvent::XLogData { data, .. })) if data.first() == Some(&b'I') => return Ok(data),
                Ok(Some(_)) => {}
                Ok(None) => return Err("stream ended".to_owned()),
                Err(error) => return Err(error.to_string()),
            }
        }
    })
    .await??;
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&insert).contains("2026-10-14 12:00:00+00"));
    Ok(())
}
```

Expected: 3 tests pass.

### Task 4: Decoder: pgoutput messages and golden fixtures
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 2
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** normal

Creates `crates/decoder` (package `pg-cdc-decoder`, no I/O) with the `Lsn` type and a total decoder for the closed pgoutput protocol-1 message set (spec §6.2, D1, D3), golden fixtures captured from Postgres 14 to 18, and the `decode_message` fuzz target.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/decoder/Cargo.toml`, `crates/decoder/src/lib.rs`, `crates/decoder/src/lsn.rs`, `crates/decoder/src/message.rs`
- Create: `crates/decoder/tests/golden.rs`, `crates/decoder/tests/fixtures/pg14/*.bin` … `pg18/*.bin` (generated in Step 6)
- Create: `fuzz/Cargo.toml`, `fuzz/.gitignore`, `fuzz/fuzz_targets/decode_message.rs`
- Modify: `Cargo.toml` (add `pg-cdc-decoder` to `[workspace.dependencies]`), `mise.toml` (add the `fixtures` task)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, read_fixture, write_fixture}` (Task 2, tests only).
- Produces:
  - `pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn(pub u64)` (`Copy`, `Ord`, `Hash`, `Default`), `Display` as `{high:X}/{low:X}`, `FromStr` accepting upper- or lower-case hex with 1 to 8 digits per half; `pg_cdc_decoder::lsn::ParseLsnError`
  - `pg_cdc_decoder::message::decode(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<LogicalMessage, DecodeError>`
  - `pub enum LogicalMessage { Begin(Begin), Commit(Commit), Origin(Origin), Relation(Relation), Type(TypeMessage), Insert(Insert), Update(Update), Delete(Delete), Truncate(Truncate) }` with `fn tag(&self) -> u8`
  - `Begin { final_lsn: Lsn, commit_ts: i64, xid: u32 }`, `Commit { flags: u8, commit_lsn: Lsn, end_lsn: Lsn, commit_ts: i64 }`, `Origin { commit_lsn: Lsn, name: String }`, `Relation { oid: u32, namespace: String, name: String, replica_identity: ReplicaIdentity, columns: Vec<RelationColumn> }`, `RelationColumn { is_key: bool, name: String, type_oid: u32, type_modifier: i32 }`, `TypeMessage { oid: u32, namespace: String, name: String }`, `Insert { relation_oid: u32, new: Tuple }`, `Update { relation_oid: u32, old: Option<OldTuple>, new: Tuple }`, `Delete { relation_oid: u32, old: OldTuple }`, `Truncate { cascade: bool, restart_identity: bool, relation_oids: Vec<u32> }`
  - `pub enum ReplicaIdentity { Default, Nothing, Full, Index }`, `pub enum OldTuple { Key(Tuple), Full(Tuple) }`, `pub struct Tuple(pub Vec<TupleValue>)`, `pub enum TupleValue { Null, UnchangedToast, Text(String) }`
  - `pub enum DecodeError` as below; all message types derive `Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq`

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing LSN tests and implement `Lsn`**

`crates/decoder/src/lsn.rs`:

```rust
use std::fmt;
use std::str::FromStr;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Default)]
pub struct Lsn(pub u64);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("invalid LSN {0:?}")]
pub struct ParseLsnError(String);

impl fmt::Display for Lsn {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:X}/{:X}", self.0 >> 32, self.0 & 0xFFFF_FFFF)
    }
}

impl FromStr for Lsn {
    type Err = ParseLsnError;

    fn from_str(text: &str) -> Result<Self, Self::Err> {
        let invalid = || ParseLsnError(text.to_owned());
        let (high, low) = text.split_once('/').ok_or_else(invalid)?;
        let half = |part: &str| {
            if part.is_empty() || part.len() > 8 || !part.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
                return Err(invalid());
            }
            u32::from_str_radix(part, 16).map_err(|_| invalid())
        };
        Ok(Self((u64::from(half(high)?) << 32) | u64::from(half(low)?)))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn displays_in_postgres_notation() {
        assert_eq!(Lsn(0x1A2B_3C4D).to_string(), "0/1A2B3C4D");
        assert_eq!(Lsn(0x1_0000_00FF).to_string(), "1/FF");
        assert_eq!(Lsn(0).to_string(), "0/0");
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_padded_unpadded_and_lowercase() {
        assert_eq!("0/1A2B3C4D".parse::<Lsn>(), Ok(Lsn(0x1A2B_3C4D)));
        assert_eq!("0/016B3748".parse::<Lsn>(), Ok(Lsn(0x016B_3748)));
        assert_eq!("1/ff".parse::<Lsn>(), Ok(Lsn(0x1_0000_00FF)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_malformed_text() {
        for bad in ["", "1", "/1", "1/", "g/1", "+1/1", "123456789/1", "1/2/3", " 0/1"] {
            assert!(bad.parse::<Lsn>().is_err(), "{bad:?} must be rejected");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn orders_by_position() {
        assert!(Lsn(1) < Lsn(0x1_0000_0000));
    }
}
```

```rust
use crate::lsn::Lsn;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum DecodeError {
    #[error("empty message")]
    Empty,
    #[error("unknown message tag {0:#04x}")]
    UnknownTag(u8),
    #[error("message truncated while reading {what}")]
    Truncated { what: &'static str },
    #[error("{what} is not terminated")]
    UnterminatedString { what: &'static str },
    #[error("{what} is not valid UTF-8")]
    InvalidUtf8 { what: &'static str },
    #[error("{what} is negative: {value}")]
    NegativeCount { what: &'static str, value: i64 },
    #[error("unexpected tuple marker {0:#04x}")]
    UnexpectedTupleMarker(u8),
    #[error("unexpected column kind {0:#04x}")]
    UnexpectedColumnKind(u8),
    #[error("unknown replica identity {0:#04x}")]
    UnknownReplicaIdentity(u8),
    #[error("{0} trailing bytes after the message")]
    TrailingBytes(usize),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum LogicalMessage {
    Begin(Begin),
    Commit(Commit),
    Origin(Origin),
    Relation(Relation),
    Type(TypeMessage),
    Insert(Insert),
    Update(Update),
    Delete(Delete),
    Truncate(Truncate),
}

impl LogicalMessage {
    pub fn tag(&self) -> u8 {
        match self {
            Self::Begin(_) => b'B',
            Self::Commit(_) => b'C',
            Self::Origin(_) => b'O',
            Self::Relation(_) => b'R',
            Self::Type(_) => b'Y',
            Self::Insert(_) => b'I',
            Self::Update(_) => b'U',
            Self::Delete(_) => b'D',
            Self::Truncate(_) => b'T',
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Begin {
    pub final_lsn: Lsn,
    pub commit_ts: i64,
    pub xid: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Commit {
    pub flags: u8,
    pub commit_lsn: Lsn,
    pub end_lsn: Lsn,
    pub commit_ts: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Origin {
    pub commit_lsn: Lsn,
    pub name: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ReplicaIdentity {
    Default,
    Nothing,
    Full,
    Index,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RelationColumn {
    pub is_key: bool,
    pub name: String,
    pub type_oid: u32,
    pub type_modifier: i32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Relation {
    pub oid: u32,
    pub namespace: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub replica_identity: ReplicaIdentity,
    pub columns: Vec<RelationColumn>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TypeMessage {
    pub oid: u32,
    pub namespace: String,
    pub name: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TupleValue {
    Null,
    UnchangedToast,
    Text(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Tuple(pub Vec<TupleValue>);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum OldTuple {
    Key(Tuple),
    Full(Tuple),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Insert {
    pub relation_oid: u32,
    pub new: Tuple,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Update {
    pub relation_oid: u32,
    pub old: Option<OldTuple>,
    pub new: Tuple,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Delete {
    pub relation_oid: u32,
    pub old: OldTuple,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Truncate {
    pub cascade: bool,
    pub restart_identity: bool,
    pub relation_oids: Vec<u32>,
}

pub fn decode(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<LogicalMessage, DecodeError> {
    let (&tag, body) = bytes.split_first().ok_or(DecodeError::Empty)?;
    let mut reader = Reader { bytes: body };
    let message = match tag {
        b'B' => LogicalMessage::Begin(Begin {
            final_lsn: Lsn(reader.u64("final LSN")?),
            commit_ts: reader.i64("commit timestamp")?,
            xid: reader.u32("xid")?,
        }),
        b'C' => LogicalMessage::Commit(Commit {
            flags: reader.u8("commit flags")?,
            commit_lsn: Lsn(reader.u64("commit LSN")?),
            end_lsn: Lsn(reader.u64("end LSN")?),
            commit_ts: reader.i64("commit timestamp")?,
        }),
        b'O' => LogicalMessage::Origin(Origin {
            commit_lsn: Lsn(reader.u64("origin commit LSN")?),
            name: reader.cstring("origin name")?,
        }),
        b'R' => LogicalMessage::Relation(relation(&mut reader)?),
        b'Y' => LogicalMessage::Type(TypeMessage {
            oid: reader.u32("type oid")?,
            namespace: reader.cstring("type namespace")?,
            name: reader.cstring("type name")?,
        }),
        b'I' => LogicalMessage::Insert(insert(&mut reader)?),
        b'U' => LogicalMessage::Update(update(&mut reader)?),
        b'D' => LogicalMessage::Delete(delete(&mut reader)?),
        b'T' => LogicalMessage::Truncate(truncate(&mut reader)?),
        other => return Err(DecodeError::UnknownTag(other)),
    };
    reader.finish()?;
    Ok(message)
}

fn relation(reader: &mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<Relation, DecodeError> {
    let oid = reader.u32("relation oid")?;
    let namespace = reader.cstring("relation namespace")?;
    let name = reader.cstring("relation name")?;
    let replica_identity = match reader.u8("replica identity")? {
        b'd' => ReplicaIdentity::Default,
        b'n' => ReplicaIdentity::Nothing,
        b'f' => ReplicaIdentity::Full,
        b'i' => ReplicaIdentity::Index,
        other => return Err(DecodeError::UnknownReplicaIdentity(other)),
    };
    let count = reader.count16("relation column count")?;
    let mut columns = Vec::with_capacity(count.min(reader.remaining()));
    for _ in 0..count {
        columns.push(RelationColumn {
            is_key: reader.u8("column flags")? & 1 == 1,
            name: reader.cstring("column name")?,
            type_oid: reader.u32("column type oid")?,
            type_modifier: reader.i32("column type modifier")?,
        });
    }
    Ok(Relation { oid, namespace, name, replica_identity, columns })
}

fn insert(reader: &mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<Insert, DecodeError> {
    let relation_oid = reader.u32("relation oid")?;
    match reader.u8("tuple marker")? {
        b'N' => Ok(Insert { relation_oid, new: tuple(reader)? }),
        other => Err(DecodeError::UnexpectedTupleMarker(other)),
    }
}

fn update(reader: &mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<Update, DecodeError> {
    let relation_oid = reader.u32("relation oid")?;
    let mut marker = reader.u8("tuple marker")?;
    let old = match marker {
        b'K' | b'O' => {
            let old = old_tuple(marker, reader)?;
            marker = reader.u8("tuple marker")?;
            Some(old)
        }
        _ => None,
    };
    if marker != b'N' {
        return Err(DecodeError::UnexpectedTupleMarker(marker));
    }
    Ok(Update { relation_oid, old, new: tuple(reader)? })
}

fn delete(reader: &mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<Delete, DecodeError> {
    let relation_oid = reader.u32("relation oid")?;
    let marker = reader.u8("tuple marker")?;
    Ok(Delete { relation_oid, old: old_tuple(marker, reader)? })
}

fn old_tuple(marker: u8, reader: &mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<OldTuple, DecodeError> {
    match marker {
        b'K' => Ok(OldTuple::Key(tuple(reader)?)),
        b'O' => Ok(OldTuple::Full(tuple(reader)?)),
        other => Err(DecodeError::UnexpectedTupleMarker(other)),
    }
}

fn truncate(reader: &mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<Truncate, DecodeError> {
    let count = reader.count32("truncated relation count")?;
    let options = reader.u8("truncate options")?;
    let mut relation_oids = Vec::with_capacity(count.min(reader.remaining() / 4));
    for _ in 0..count {
        relation_oids.push(reader.u32("truncated relation oid")?);
    }
    Ok(Truncate { cascade: options & 1 == 1, restart_identity: options & 2 == 2, relation_oids })
}

fn tuple(reader: &mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<Tuple, DecodeError> {
    let count = reader.count16("tuple column count")?;
    let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(count.min(reader.remaining()));
    for _ in 0..count {
        let value = match reader.u8("column kind")? {
            b'n' => TupleValue::Null,
            b'u' => TupleValue::UnchangedToast,
            b't' => {
                let len = reader.count32("text length")?;
                let bytes = reader.take(len, "text value")?;
                let text = std::str::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| DecodeError::InvalidUtf8 { what: "text value" })?;
                TupleValue::Text(text.to_owned())
            }
            other => return Err(DecodeError::UnexpectedColumnKind(other)),
        };
        values.push(value);
    }
    Ok(Tuple(values))
}

struct Reader<'a> {
    bytes: &'a [u8],
}

impl<'a> Reader<'a> {
    fn remaining(&self) -> usize {
        self.bytes.len()
    }

    fn take(&mut self, len: usize, what: &'static str) -> Result<&'a [u8], DecodeError> {
        let (head, rest) = self.bytes.split_at_checked(len).ok_or(DecodeError::Truncated { what })?;
        self.bytes = rest;
        Ok(head)
    }

    fn array<const N: usize>(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<[u8; N], DecodeError> {
        let (head, rest) = self.bytes.split_first_chunk::<N>().ok_or(DecodeError::Truncated { what })?;
        self.bytes = rest;
        Ok(*head)
    }

    fn u8(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<u8, DecodeError> {
        Ok(u8::from_be_bytes(self.array(what)?))
    }

    fn i32(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<i32, DecodeError> {
        Ok(i32::from_be_bytes(self.array(what)?))
    }

    fn u32(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<u32, DecodeError> {
        Ok(u32::from_be_bytes(self.array(what)?))
    }

    fn i64(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<i64, DecodeError> {
        Ok(i64::from_be_bytes(self.array(what)?))
    }

    fn u64(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<u64, DecodeError> {
        Ok(u64::from_be_bytes(self.array(what)?))
    }

    fn count16(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<usize, DecodeError> {
        let value = i16::from_be_bytes(self.array(what)?);
        usize::try_from(value).map_err(|_| DecodeError::NegativeCount { what, value: i64::from(value) })
    }

    fn count32(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<usize, DecodeError> {
        let value = self.i32(what)?;
        usize::try_from(value).map_err(|_| DecodeError::NegativeCount { what, value: i64::from(value) })
    }

    fn cstring(&mut self, what: &'static str) -> Result<String, DecodeError> {
        let end = self
            .bytes
            .iter()
            .position(|&byte| byte == 0)
            .ok_or(DecodeError::UnterminatedString { what })?;
        let text = self.take(end, what)?;
        self.take(1, what)?;
        std::str::from_utf8(text)
            .map(str::to_owned)
            .map_err(|_| DecodeError::InvalidUtf8 { what })
    }

    fn finish(self) -> Result<(), DecodeError> {
        if self.bytes.is_empty() { Ok(()) } else { Err(DecodeError::TrailingBytes(self.bytes.len())) }
    }
}
```
