```rust
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use pg_cdc_decoder::message::{LogicalMessage, OldTuple, Relation, ReplicaIdentity, Tuple, TupleValue, Update, decode};
use pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, read_fixture, write_fixture};

type TestResult<T = ()> = Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error>>;

const VERSIONS: [u8; 5] = [14, 15, 16, 17, 18];

const SETUP: &[&str] = &[
    "CREATE TYPE fx_mood AS ENUM ('happy', 'sad')",
    "CREATE TABLE fx_items (id int PRIMARY KEY, name text, mood fx_mood)",
    "CREATE TABLE fx_docs (id int PRIMARY KEY, body text)",
    "ALTER TABLE fx_docs ALTER COLUMN body SET STORAGE EXTERNAL",
    "CREATE TABLE fx_full (id int PRIMARY KEY, body text)",
    "ALTER TABLE fx_full ALTER COLUMN body SET STORAGE EXTERNAL",
    "ALTER TABLE fx_full REPLICA IDENTITY FULL",
    "CREATE PUBLICATION fx_pub FOR TABLE fx_items, fx_docs, fx_full",
    "SELECT pg_replication_origin_create('fx_origin')",
];

const SCENARIOS: &[(&str, &[&str])] = &[
    ("insert", &["INSERT INTO fx_items VALUES (1, 'a', 'happy')"]),
    ("update", &["UPDATE fx_items SET name = 'b' WHERE id = 1", "UPDATE fx_items SET id = 2 WHERE id = 1"]),
    ("delete", &["DELETE FROM fx_items WHERE id = 2"]),
    (
        "toast",
        &[
            "INSERT INTO fx_docs VALUES (1, repeat('x', 3000))",
            "UPDATE fx_docs SET id = 2 WHERE id = 1",
            "INSERT INTO fx_full VALUES (1, repeat('y', 3000))",
            "UPDATE fx_full SET id = 2 WHERE id = 1",
        ],
    ),
    ("truncate", &["TRUNCATE fx_items, fx_docs RESTART IDENTITY"]),
    (
        "origin",
        &["SELECT pg_replication_origin_session_setup('fx_origin'); INSERT INTO fx_items VALUES (9, 'o', NULL)"],
    ),
];

fn fixture_path(version: u8, scenario: &str) -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
        .join("tests")
        .join("fixtures")
        .join(format!("pg{version}"))
        .join(format!("{scenario}.bin"))
}

fn load(version: u8, scenario: &str) -> TestResult<Vec<LogicalMessage>> {
    let mut messages = Vec::new();
    for bytes in read_fixture(&fixture_path(version, scenario))? {
        messages.push(decode(&bytes)?);
    }
    Ok(messages)
}

fn tags(messages: &[LogicalMessage]) -> String {
    messages.iter().map(|message| char::from(message.tag())).collect()
}

fn text(value: &str) -> TupleValue {
    TupleValue::Text(value.to_owned())
}

fn updates_in(messages: &[LogicalMessage]) -> Vec<&Update> {
    messages
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|message| match message {
            LogicalMessage::Update(update) => Some(update),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect()
}

fn relations_in(messages: &[LogicalMessage]) -> Vec<&Relation> {
    messages
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|message| match message {
            LogicalMessage::Relation(relation) => Some(relation),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect()
}

#[tokio::test]
#[ignore = "regenerates golden fixtures; run `mise run fixtures`"]
async fn capture_golden_fixtures() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    for statement in SETUP {
        pg.execute(statement).await?;
    }
    pg.create_slot("fx_slot").await?;
    for (scenario, statements) in SCENARIOS {
        for statement in *statements {
            pg.execute(statement).await?;
        }
        let messages = pg.capture_pgoutput("fx_slot", "fx_pub").await?;
        write_fixture(&fixture_path(pg.version(), scenario), &messages)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn golden_insert_decodes_on_every_version() -> TestResult {
    for version in VERSIONS {
        let messages = load(version, "insert")?;
        assert_eq!(tags(&messages), "BYRIC", "pg{version}");
        let [
            LogicalMessage::Begin(begin),
            LogicalMessage::Type(mood),
            LogicalMessage::Relation(relation),
            LogicalMessage::Insert(insert),
            LogicalMessage::Commit(commit),
        ] = messages.as_slice()
        else {
            panic!("pg{version}: unexpected message shape");
        };
        assert_eq!((mood.namespace.as_str(), mood.name.as_str()), ("public", "fx_mood"));
        assert_eq!((relation.namespace.as_str(), relation.name.as_str()), ("public", "fx_items"));
        assert_eq!(relation.replica_identity, ReplicaIdentity::Default);
        let columns: Vec<(&str, bool, u32)> =
            relation.columns.iter().map(|column| (column.name.as_str(), column.is_key, column.type_oid)).collect();
        assert_eq!(columns, vec![("id", true, 23), ("name", false, 25), ("mood", false, mood.oid)]);
        assert_eq!(insert.relation_oid, relation.oid);
        assert_eq!(insert.new, Tuple(vec![text("1"), text("a"), text("happy")]));
        assert_eq!(begin.final_lsn, commit.commit_lsn);
        assert!(commit.end_lsn > commit.commit_lsn);
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn golden_update_and_delete_decode_on_every_version() -> TestResult {
    for version in VERSIONS {
        let update = load(version, "update")?;
        assert_eq!(tags(&update), "BYRUCBUC", "pg{version}");
        let updates = updates_in(&update);
        assert_eq!(updates.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(updates[0].old, None);
        assert_eq!(updates[0].new, Tuple(vec![text("1"), text("b"), text("happy")]));
        assert_eq!(updates[1].old, Some(OldTuple::Key(Tuple(vec![text("1"), TupleValue::Null, TupleValue::Null]))));
        assert_eq!(updates[1].new, Tuple(vec![text("2"), text("b"), text("happy")]));

        let delete = load(version, "delete")?;
        assert_eq!(tags(&delete), "BYRDC", "pg{version}");
        let Some(LogicalMessage::Delete(delete)) = delete.get(3) else {
            panic!("pg{version}: no delete");
        };
        assert_eq!(delete.old, OldTuple::Key(Tuple(vec![text("2"), TupleValue::Null, TupleValue::Null])));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn golden_toast_decodes_on_every_version() -> TestResult {
    let docs_body = "x".repeat(3000);
    let full_body = "y".repeat(3000);
    for version in VERSIONS {
        let messages = load(version, "toast")?;
        assert_eq!(tags(&messages), "BRICBUCBRICBUC", "pg{version}");
        let relations = relations_in(&messages);
        assert_eq!(relations[0].replica_identity, ReplicaIdentity::Default);
        assert_eq!(relations[1].replica_identity, ReplicaIdentity::Full);
        assert!(relations[1].columns.iter().all(|column| column.is_key));
        let updates = updates_in(&messages);
        assert_eq!(updates[0].old, Some(OldTuple::Key(Tuple(vec![text("1"), TupleValue::Null]))));
        assert_eq!(updates[0].new, Tuple(vec![text("2"), TupleValue::UnchangedToast]));
        assert_eq!(updates[1].old, Some(OldTuple::Full(Tuple(vec![text("1"), text(&full_body)]))));
        assert_eq!(updates[1].new, Tuple(vec![text("2"), TupleValue::UnchangedToast]));
        let Some(LogicalMessage::Insert(first)) = messages.get(2) else {
            panic!("pg{version}: no insert");
        };
        assert_eq!(first.new, Tuple(vec![text("1"), text(&docs_body)]));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn golden_truncate_and_origin_decode_on_every_version() -> TestResult {
    for version in VERSIONS {
        let messages = load(version, "truncate")?;
        let truncate_tags = tags(&messages);
        assert!(truncate_tags.starts_with('B') && truncate_tags.ends_with("TC"), "pg{version}: {truncate_tags}");
        assert_eq!(truncate_tags.matches('R').count(), 2, "pg{version}: {truncate_tags}");
        let relation_oids: BTreeSet<u32> = relations_in(&messages).iter().map(|relation| relation.oid).collect();
        let Some(LogicalMessage::Truncate(truncate)) = messages.iter().rev().nth(1) else {
            panic!("pg{version}: no truncate");
        };
        assert!(!truncate.cascade);
        assert!(truncate.restart_identity);
        assert_eq!(truncate.relation_oids.iter().copied().collect::<BTreeSet<u32>>(), relation_oids);
        assert_eq!(relation_oids.len(), 2);

        let origin = load(version, "origin")?;
        assert_eq!(tags(&origin), "BOYRIC", "pg{version}");
        let Some(LogicalMessage::Origin(origin)) = origin.get(1) else {
            panic!("pg{version}: no origin");
        };
        assert_eq!(origin.name, "fx_origin");
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

### Task 5: Decoder: value mapping
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 4
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** normal

Adds `pg_cdc_decoder::value`: the total mapping from a Postgres text value and its type to the JSON `to_jsonb` produces (spec §6.4, D2), including the array text parser, the float and JSON number normalization (Rulings R5–R7), timestamp rewriting, commit timestamp formatting for envelopes, property tests and the `map_value` fuzz target. The type of a non-built-in column is resolved later through the catalog (Task 9); this module only needs a `TypeKind`.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/decoder/src/value/mod.rs`, `crates/decoder/src/value/number.rs`, `crates/decoder/src/value/datetime.rs`, `crates/decoder/src/value/array.rs`
- Create: `fuzz/fuzz_targets/map_value.rs`
- Modify: `crates/decoder/src/lib.rs` (add `pub mod value;`), `crates/decoder/Cargo.toml` (add `serde_json.workspace = true`), `fuzz/Cargo.toml` (add the `map_value` bin)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing beyond the crate from Task 4.
- Produces:
  - `pub enum TypeKind { Bool, Integer, Float, Numeric, Json, Timestamp, Timestamptz, Text, Array { element: Box<TypeKind>, delimiter: char } }` (`Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq`)
  - `pub fn builtin_kind(oid: u32) -> Option<TypeKind>`: `bool` 16 → Bool; `int8` 20, `int2` 21, `int4` 23, `oid` 26 → Integer; `float4` 700, `float8` 701 → Float; `numeric` 1700 → Numeric; `json` 114, `jsonb` 3802 → Json; `timestamp` 1114 → Timestamp; `timestamptz` 1184 → Timestamptz; `bytea` 17, `"char"` 18, `name` 19, `text` 25, `bpchar` 1042, `varchar` 1043, `date` 1082 → Text; their array types 1000, 1005, 1007, 1016, 1028, 1021, 1022, 1231, 199, 3807, 1115, 1185, 1001, 1002, 1003, 1009, 1014, 1015, 1182 → `Array { element, delimiter: ',' }`; any other OID → `None`
  - `pub struct Mapped { pub value: serde_json::Value, pub fallback: bool }`; `pub fn map_value(kind: &TypeKind, text: &str) -> Mapped` (never fails; `fallback` is true when a typed parse failed and the text form was used)
  - `pub fn format_commit_ts(micros_since_2000: i64) -> String` → `2026-10-14T12:00:00.123456+00:00` form, fraction trimmed of trailing zeros and omitted when zero

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`crates/decoder/src/value/mod.rs` starts with the module declarations and ends with this test module; Step 2 fills in the code between them.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use proptest::prelude::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn mapped(kind: &TypeKind, text: &str) -> Value {
        let result = map_value(kind, text);
        assert!(!result.fallback, "{text:?} as {kind:?} fell back");
        result.value
    }

    fn array_of(element: TypeKind) -> TypeKind {
        TypeKind::Array { element: Box::new(element), delimiter: ',' }
    }

    #[test]
    fn maps_booleans_and_integers() {
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Bool, "t"), json!(true));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Bool, "f"), json!(false));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Integer, "-9223372036854775808").to_string(), "-9223372036854775808");
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Integer, "4294967295").to_string(), "4294967295");
    }

    #[test]
    fn unparsable_typed_values_fall_back_to_their_text() {
        for (kind, text) in [
            (TypeKind::Bool, "yes"),
            (TypeKind::Integer, "12a"),
            (TypeKind::Integer, "007"),
            (TypeKind::Float, "1.2.3"),
            (TypeKind::Numeric, "1e"),
            (TypeKind::Json, "{"),
            (TypeKind::Timestamp, "noon"),
            (TypeKind::Timestamptz, "2026-10-14 12:00:00"),
        ] {
            let result = map_value(&kind, text);
            assert!(result.fallback, "{text:?} as {kind:?}");
            assert_eq!(result.value, Value::String(text.to_owned()));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn floats_render_as_to_jsonb_plain_decimals() {
        let hundred_zeros = format!("1{}", "0".repeat(100));
        let tiny = format!("0.{}5", "0".repeat(323));
        for (text, expected) in [
            ("1e+100", hundred_zeros.as_str()),
            ("1.5e-07", "0.00000015"),
            ("-1.5e-07", "-0.00000015"),
            ("1.2345678901234568e+20", "123456789012345680000"),
            ("3.4028234663852886e+38", "340282346638528860000000000000000000000"),
            ("0.1", "0.1"),
            ("-0", "0"),
            ("1e-05", "0.00001"),
            ("5e-324", tiny.as_str()),
            ("42", "42"),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Float, text).to_string(), expected, "{text}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_finite_floats_and_numerics_are_strings() {
        for kind in [TypeKind::Float, TypeKind::Numeric] {
            for text in ["NaN", "Infinity", "-Infinity"] {
                assert_eq!(mapped(&kind, text), json!(text));
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn numerics_keep_exact_digits() {
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Numeric, "1.50").to_string(), "1.50");
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Numeric, "-12.3456").to_string(), "-12.3456");
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Numeric, "0.000000000001").to_string(), "0.000000000001");
        assert_eq!(
            mapped(&TypeKind::Numeric, "1000000000000000000000000000000").to_string(),
            "1000000000000000000000000000000"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn json_is_embedded_with_to_jsonb_numbers() {
        let value = mapped(&TypeKind::Json, r#"{"b": 1, "a": 1e2, "a": 3, "c": [-0, 1.0E1, "x"]}"#);
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_string(&value).unwrap(), r#"{"b":1,"a":3,"c":[0,10,"x"]}"#);
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Json, "null"), Value::Null);
        assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Json, r#"{"x": 100}"#), json!({"x": 100}));
    }

    #[test]
    fn deeply_nested_json_falls_back_to_text() {
        let text = format!("{}{}", "[".repeat(200), "]".repeat(200));
        let result = map_value(&TypeKind::Json, &text);
        assert!(result.fallback);
        assert_eq!(result.value, Value::String(text));
    }

    #[test]
    fn timestamps_render_in_xsd_form() {
        for (text, expected) in [
            ("2026-10-14 12:00:00.5", "2026-10-14T12:00:00.5"),
            ("0044-03-15 12:00:00 BC", "0044-03-15T12:00:00 BC"),
            ("-infinity", "-infinity"),
            ("infinity", "infinity"),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Timestamp, text), json!(expected), "{text}");
        }
        for (text, expected) in [
            ("2026-10-14 12:00:00.123456+00", "2026-10-14T12:00:00.123456+00:00"),
            ("2026-10-14 12:00:00+00", "2026-10-14T12:00:00+00:00"),
            ("0044-03-15 12:00:00+00 BC", "0044-03-15T12:00:00+00:00 BC"),
            ("10000-01-01 00:00:00+00", "10000-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"),
            ("2026-10-14 12:00:00+05:30", "2026-10-14T12:00:00+05:30"),
            ("1900-01-01 00:00:00-00:01:15", "1900-01-01T00:00:00-00:01:15"),
            ("infinity", "infinity"),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Timestamptz, text), json!(expected), "{text}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn text_kinds_are_strings_verbatim() {
        for text in ["", "\\x0102", "ab  ", "2026-10-14", "0044-03-15 BC", "(1,\"x y\")", "emoji 🐘"] {
            assert_eq!(mapped(&TypeKind::Text, text), json!(text));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn arrays_nest_by_dimension_and_map_elements() {
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Integer), "{1,NULL,3}"), json!([1, null, 3]));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Integer), "[0:1]={1,2}"), json!([1, 2]));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Integer), "{{1,2},{3,4}}"), json!([[1, 2], [3, 4]]));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Integer), "{}"), json!([]));
        assert_eq!(
            mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Text), r#"{{a,"b c"},{"NULL",NULL}}"#),
            json!([["a", "b c"], ["NULL", null]])
        );
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Text), r#"{"q\"uote","back\\slash",""}"#), json!(["q\"uote", "back\\slash", ""]));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Text), r#"{"\\x01","\\x"}"#), json!(["\\x01", "\\x"]));
        assert_eq!(
            mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Timestamptz), r#"{"2026-10-14 12:00:00+00"}"#),
            json!(["2026-10-14T12:00:00+00:00"])
        );
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Json), r#"{"{\"a\": 1}"}"#), json!([{"a": 1}]));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Float), "{NaN,1.5}"), json!(["NaN", 1.5]));
        assert_eq!(mapped(&array_of(TypeKind::Bool), "{t,f}"), json!([true, false]));
        let boxes = TypeKind::Array { element: Box::new(TypeKind::Text), delimiter: ';' };
        assert_eq!(mapped(&boxes, "{(1,1),(0,0);(2,2),(1,1)}"), json!(["(1,1),(0,0)", "(2,2),(1,1)"]));
    }

    #[test]
    fn malformed_arrays_fall_back_to_text() {
        for text in ["{1,2", "{1,2}x", "1,2", r#"{"a}"#, "{,}", "{{{{{{{1}}}}}}}", "[1:2={1,2}", "{a\"b}"] {
            let result = map_value(&array_of(TypeKind::Text), text);
            assert!(result.fallback, "{text:?}");
            assert_eq!(result.value, Value::String(text.to_owned()));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn array_element_fallback_marks_the_value() {
        let result = map_value(&array_of(TypeKind::Integer), "{1,x}");
        assert!(result.fallback);
        assert_eq!(result.value, json!([1, "x"]));
    }

    #[test]
    fn builtin_kinds_cover_the_mapped_types() {
        assert_eq!(builtin_kind(26), Some(TypeKind::Integer));
        assert_eq!(builtin_kind(1184), Some(TypeKind::Timestamptz));
        assert_eq!(builtin_kind(1082), Some(TypeKind::Text));
        assert_eq!(builtin_kind(1009), Some(array_of(TypeKind::Text)));
        assert_eq!(builtin_kind(3807), Some(array_of(TypeKind::Json)));
        assert_eq!(builtin_kind(2950), None);
        assert_eq!(builtin_kind(16_389), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn commit_timestamps_use_to_jsonb_form() {
        assert_eq!(format_commit_ts(845_294_400_123_456), "2026-10-14T12:00:00.123456+00:00");
        assert_eq!(format_commit_ts(845_294_400_500_000), "2026-10-14T12:00:00.5+00:00");
        assert_eq!(format_commit_ts(845_285_183_069_961), "2026-10-14T09:26:23.069961+00:00");
        assert_eq!(format_commit_ts(762_480_000_000_010), "2024-02-29T00:00:00.00001+00:00");
        assert_eq!(format_commit_ts(0), "2000-01-01T00:00:00+00:00");
        assert_eq!(format_commit_ts(-1), "1999-12-31T23:59:59.999999+00:00");
    }

    fn render_pg_array(elements: &[Option<String>]) -> String {
        let rendered: Vec<String> = elements
            .iter()
            .map(|element| match element {
                None => "NULL".to_owned(),
                Some(text) => {
                    let needs_quotes = text.is_empty()
                        || text.eq_ignore_ascii_case("NULL")
                        || text.chars().any(|c| matches!(c, '{' | '}' | ',' | '"' | '\\') || c.is_ascii_whitespace());
                    if needs_quotes {
                        format!("\"{}\"", text.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('"', "\\\""))
                    } else {
                        text.clone()
                    }
                }
            })
            .collect();
        format!("{{{}}}", rendered.join(","))
    }

    fn scalar_kind() -> impl Strategy<Value = TypeKind> {
        prop_oneof![
            Just(TypeKind::Bool),
            Just(TypeKind::Integer),
            Just(TypeKind::Float),
            Just(TypeKind::Numeric),
            Just(TypeKind::Json),
            Just(TypeKind::Timestamp),
            Just(TypeKind::Timestamptz),
            Just(TypeKind::Text),
        ]
    }

    proptest! {
        #[test]
        fn d2_map_value_is_total(kind in scalar_kind(), text in ".*", as_array in any::<bool>(), semicolon in any::<bool>()) {
            let scalar = kind.clone();
            let kind = if as_array {
                TypeKind::Array { element: Box::new(kind), delimiter: if semicolon { ';' } else { ',' } }
            } else {
                kind
            };
            let result = map_value(&kind, &text);
            if !as_array && result.fallback {
                prop_assert_eq!(result.value, Value::String(text.clone()));
            }
            if !as_array && scalar == TypeKind::Text {
                prop_assert!(!result.fallback);
                prop_assert_eq!(map_value(&TypeKind::Text, &text).value, Value::String(text));
            }
        }

        #[test]
        fn float_normalization_preserves_the_value(x in any::<f64>().prop_filter("finite", |x| x.is_finite())) {
            let mapped = map_value(&TypeKind::Float, &format!("{x:e}"));
            prop_assert!(!mapped.fallback);
            let parsed: f64 = mapped.value.to_string().parse().unwrap();
            prop_assert_eq!(parsed.to_bits(), (x + 0.0).to_bits());
        }

        #[test]
        fn text_arrays_round_trip(elements in proptest::collection::vec(proptest::option::of(".*"), 0..8)) {
            let expected = Value::Array(
                elements.iter().map(|element| element.clone().map_or(Value::Null, Value::String)).collect(),
            );
            let result = map_value(&array_of(TypeKind::Text), &render_pg_array(&elements));
            prop_assert!(!result.fallback);
            prop_assert_eq!(result.value, expected);
        }
    }
}
```

`float_normalization_preserves_the_value` compares bit patterns after `x + 0.0`, which turns `-0.0` into `0.0`: the mapping renders `-0` as `0` on purpose (Ruling R6).

`crates/decoder/src/value/mod.rs` (above the test module):

```rust
mod array;
mod datetime;
mod number;

use serde_json::{Number, Value};

pub use datetime::format_commit_ts;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TypeKind {
    Bool,
    Integer,
    Float,
    Numeric,
    Json,
    Timestamp,
    Timestamptz,
    Text,
    Array { element: Box<TypeKind>, delimiter: char },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Mapped {
    pub value: Value,
    pub fallback: bool,
}

pub fn builtin_kind(oid: u32) -> Option<TypeKind> {
    let element = match oid {
        1000 => 16,
        1005 => 21,
        1007 => 23,
        1016 => 20,
        1028 => 26,
        1021 => 700,
        1022 => 701,
        1231 => 1700,
        199 => 114,
        3807 => 3802,
        1115 => 1114,
        1185 => 1184,
        1001 => 17,
        1002 => 18,
        1003 => 19,
        1009 => 25,
        1014 => 1042,
        1015 => 1043,
        1182 => 1082,
        _ => return builtin_scalar(oid),
    };
    builtin_scalar(element).map(|element| TypeKind::Array { element: Box::new(element), delimiter: ',' })
}

fn builtin_scalar(oid: u32) -> Option<TypeKind> {
    match oid {
        16 => Some(TypeKind::Bool),
        20 | 21 | 23 | 26 => Some(TypeKind::Integer),
        700 | 701 => Some(TypeKind::Float),
        1700 => Some(TypeKind::Numeric),
        114 | 3802 => Some(TypeKind::Json),
        1114 => Some(TypeKind::Timestamp),
        1184 => Some(TypeKind::Timestamptz),
        17 | 18 | 19 | 25 | 1042 | 1043 | 1082 => Some(TypeKind::Text),
        _ => None,
    }
}

pub fn map_value(kind: &TypeKind, text: &str) -> Mapped {
    let mut fallback = false;
    let value = map_into(kind, text, &mut fallback);
    Mapped { value, fallback }
}

fn map_into(kind: &TypeKind, text: &str, fallback: &mut bool) -> Value {
    let typed = match kind {
        TypeKind::Array { element, delimiter } => {
            array::parse(text, *delimiter).map(|node| array_value(node, element, fallback))
        }
        scalar => map_scalar(scalar, text),
    };
    typed.unwrap_or_else(|| {
        *fallback = true;
        Value::String(text.to_owned())
    })
}

fn array_value(node: array::Node, element: &TypeKind, fallback: &mut bool) -> Value {
    match node {
        array::Node::Null => Value::Null,
        array::Node::Element(text) => map_into(element, &text, fallback),
        array::Node::Array(items) => {
            Value::Array(items.into_iter().map(|item| array_value(item, element, fallback)).collect())
        }
    }
}

fn map_scalar(kind: &TypeKind, text: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    match kind {
        TypeKind::Bool => match text {
            "t" => Some(Value::Bool(true)),
            "f" => Some(Value::Bool(false)),
            _ => None,
        },
        TypeKind::Integer => integer(text),
        TypeKind::Float | TypeKind::Numeric => non_finite(text).or_else(|| number::to_json_number(text)),
        TypeKind::Json => json(text),
        TypeKind::Timestamp => datetime::timestamp(text).map(Value::String),
        TypeKind::Timestamptz => datetime::timestamptz(text).map(Value::String),
        TypeKind::Text | TypeKind::Array { .. } => Some(Value::String(text.to_owned())),
    }
}

fn non_finite(text: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    matches!(text, "NaN" | "Infinity" | "-Infinity").then(|| Value::String(text.to_owned()))
}

fn integer(text: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    let digits = text.strip_prefix('-').unwrap_or(text);
    if digits.is_empty() || !digits.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit()) {
        return None;
    }
    serde_json::from_str::<Number>(text).ok().map(Value::Number)
}

fn json(text: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    normalize_numbers(serde_json::from_str(text).ok()?)
}

fn normalize_numbers(value: Value) -> Option<Value> {
    Some(match value {
        Value::Number(number) => number::to_json_number(&number.to_string())?,
        Value::Array(items) => Value::Array(items.into_iter().map(normalize_numbers).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
        Value::Object(map) => Value::Object(
            map.into_iter()
                .map(|(key, value)| normalize_numbers(value).map(|value| (key, value)))
                .collect::<Option<_>>()?,
        ),
        other => other,
    })
}
```

With `arbitrary_precision`, `Number::to_string` returns the digits exactly as written in the JSON text, which `to_json_number` then rewrites.

`crates/decoder/src/value/number.rs`:

```rust
use serde_json::{Number, Value};

const MAX_INTEGER_DIGITS: i64 = 131_072;
const MAX_SCALE: i64 = 16_383;

pub(super) fn to_json_number(text: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    let plain = plain_decimal(text)?;
    serde_json::from_str::<Number>(&plain).ok().map(Value::Number)
}

fn plain_decimal(text: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let (negative, unsigned) = match text.strip_prefix('-') {
        Some(rest) => (true, rest),
        None => (false, text),
    };
    let (mantissa, exponent) = match unsigned.split_once(['e', 'E']) {
        Some((mantissa, exponent)) => (mantissa, exponent.parse::<i64>().ok()?),
        None => (unsigned, 0),
    };
    let (int_part, frac_part) = mantissa.split_once('.').unwrap_or((mantissa, ""));
    let all_digits = |part: &str| part.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit());
    if int_part.is_empty() || !all_digits(int_part) || !all_digits(frac_part) || mantissa.ends_with('.') {
        return None;
    }
    let digits = format!("{int_part}{frac_part}");
    let digit_count = i64::try_from(digits.len()).ok()?;
    let point = i64::try_from(int_part.len()).ok()?.checked_add(exponent)?;
    let scale = i64::try_from(frac_part.len()).ok()?.checked_sub(exponent)?.max(0);
    if point > MAX_INTEGER_DIGITS || scale > MAX_SCALE {
        return None;
    }
    let (integer, fraction) = if point <= 0 {
        let zeros = "0".repeat(usize::try_from(-point).ok()?);
        ("0".to_owned(), format!("{zeros}{digits}"))
    } else if point >= digit_count {
        let zeros = "0".repeat(usize::try_from(point - digit_count).ok()?);
        (format!("{digits}{zeros}"), String::new())
    } else {
        let (integer, fraction) = digits.split_at_checked(usize::try_from(point).ok()?)?;
        (integer.to_owned(), fraction.to_owned())
    };
    let trimmed = integer.trim_start_matches('0');
    let integer = if trimmed.is_empty() { "0" } else { trimmed };
    let sign = if negative && digits.bytes().any(|byte| byte != b'0') { "-" } else { "" };
    let dot = if fraction.is_empty() { "" } else { "." };
    Some(format!("{sign}{integer}{dot}{fraction}"))
}
```

The fraction always has exactly `scale` digits: when the point falls inside or before the digits, the digits after it number `frac_len - exponent`; when it falls after them, the exponent is at least `frac_len`, so the scale is 0.

`crates/decoder/src/value/datetime.rs`:

```rust
const MICROS_PER_DAY: i64 = 86_400_000_000;
const DAYS_FROM_1970_TO_2000: i64 = 10_957;

pub(super) fn timestamp(text: &str) -> Option<String> {
    if is_infinite(text) {
        return Some(text.to_owned());
    }
    let (date, time) = text.split_once(' ')?;
    if date.is_empty() || time.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    Some(format!("{date}T{time}"))
}

pub(super) fn timestamptz(text: &str) -> Option<String> {
    if is_infinite(text) {
        return Some(text.to_owned());
    }
    let (body, era) = match text.strip_suffix(" BC") {
        Some(body) => (body, " BC"),
        None => (text, ""),
    };
    let (date, time) = body.split_once(' ')?;
    let offset_at = time.rfind(['+', '-'])?;
    let (clock, offset) = time.split_at_checked(offset_at)?;
    if date.is_empty() || clock.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let offset = match offset.len() {
        3 => format!("{offset}:00"),
        6 | 9 => offset.to_owned(),
        _ => return None,
    };
    Some(format!("{date}T{clock}{offset}{era}"))
}

fn is_infinite(text: &str) -> bool {
    text == "infinity" || text == "-infinity"
}

pub fn format_commit_ts(micros_since_2000: i64) -> String {
    let days = micros_since_2000.div_euclid(MICROS_PER_DAY) + DAYS_FROM_1970_TO_2000;
    let micros_of_day = micros_since_2000.rem_euclid(MICROS_PER_DAY);
    let (year, month, day) = civil_from_days(days);
    let seconds = micros_of_day / 1_000_000;
    let fraction = micros_of_day % 1_000_000;
    let (hour, minute, second) = (seconds / 3600, seconds / 60 % 60, seconds % 60);
    let mut out = format!("{year:04}-{month:02}-{day:02}T{hour:02}:{minute:02}:{second:02}");
    if fraction != 0 {
        let digits = format!("{fraction:06}");
        out.push('.');
        out.push_str(digits.trim_end_matches('0'));
    }
    out.push_str("+00:00");
    out
}

fn civil_from_days(days_since_1970: i64) -> (i64, i64, i64) {
    let shifted = days_since_1970 + 719_468;
    let era = shifted.div_euclid(146_097);
    let day_of_era = shifted.rem_euclid(146_097);
    let year_of_era = (day_of_era - day_of_era / 1460 + day_of_era / 36_524 - day_of_era / 146_096) / 365;
    let day_of_year = day_of_era - (365 * year_of_era + year_of_era / 4 - year_of_era / 100);
    let month_index = (5 * day_of_year + 2) / 153;
    let day = day_of_year - (153 * month_index + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let month = if month_index < 10 { month_index + 3 } else { month_index - 9 };
    let year = year_of_era + era * 400 + i64::from(month <= 2);
    (year, month, day)
}
```

`civil_from_days` is Howard Hinnant's days-to-civil algorithm, with March as the first month of its internal year.

`crates/decoder/src/value/array.rs`:

```rust
use std::iter::Peekable;
use std::str::Chars;

const MAX_DIMENSIONS: usize = 6;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(super) enum Node {
    Null,
    Element(String),
    Array(Vec<Node>),
}

pub(super) fn parse(text: &str, delimiter: char) -> Option<Node> {
    let body = strip_dimensions(text)?;
    let mut chars = body.chars().peekable();
    let node = parse_array(&mut chars, delimiter, 1)?;
    chars.next().is_none().then_some(node)
}

fn strip_dimensions(text: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    if !text.starts_with('[') {
        return Some(text);
    }
    let (dimensions, rest) = text.split_once('=')?;
    let well_formed = dimensions.ends_with(']')
        && dimensions.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit() || matches!(byte, b'[' | b']' | b':' | b'-'));
    well_formed.then_some(rest)
}

fn parse_array(chars: &mut Peekable<Chars<'_>>, delimiter: char, depth: usize) -> Option<Node> {
    if depth > MAX_DIMENSIONS || chars.next()? != '{' {
        return None;
    }
    let mut items = Vec::new();
    if chars.peek() == Some(&'}') {
        chars.next();
        return Some(Node::Array(items));
    }
    loop {
        let item = match chars.peek()? {
            '{' => parse_array(chars, delimiter, depth + 1)?,
            '"' => {
                chars.next();
                Node::Element(parse_quoted(chars)?)
            }
            _ => parse_unquoted(chars, delimiter)?,
        };
        items.push(item);
        match chars.next()? {
            '}' => return Some(Node::Array(items)),
            next if next == delimiter => {}
            _ => return None,
        }
    }
}

fn parse_quoted(chars: &mut Peekable<Chars<'_>>) -> Option<String> {
    let mut out = String::new();
    loop {
        match chars.next()? {
            '"' => return Some(out),
            '\\' => out.push(chars.next()?),
            other => out.push(other),
        }
    }
}

fn parse_unquoted(chars: &mut Peekable<Chars<'_>>, delimiter: char) -> Option<Node> {
    let mut out = String::new();
    while let Some(&next) = chars.peek() {
        if next == delimiter || next == '}' {
            break;
        }
        if matches!(next, '{' | '"') {
            return None;
        }
        chars.next();
        if next == '\\' {
            out.push(chars.next()?);
        } else {
            out.push(next);
        }
    }
    if out.is_empty() {
        None
    } else if out.eq_ignore_ascii_case("NULL") {
        Some(Node::Null)
    } else {
        Some(Node::Element(out))
    }
}
```

```rust
#![no_main]

use libfuzzer_sys::fuzz_target;
use pg_cdc_decoder::value::{TypeKind, map_value};

fuzz_target!(|input: (u8, &str)| {
    let (selector, text) = input;
    let scalar = match selector % 8 {
        0 => TypeKind::Bool,
        1 => TypeKind::Integer,
        2 => TypeKind::Float,
        3 => TypeKind::Numeric,
        4 => TypeKind::Json,
        5 => TypeKind::Timestamp,
        6 => TypeKind::Timestamptz,
        _ => TypeKind::Text,
    };
    let kind = if selector & 0x80 == 0 {
        scalar
    } else {
        let delimiter = if selector & 0x40 == 0 { ',' } else { ';' };
        TypeKind::Array { element: Box::new(scalar), delimiter }
    };
    let _ = map_value(&kind, text);
});
```

### Task 6: Configuration loading
**Depends on:** Task 1
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** normal

Implements `pg_cdc::config`: the milestone-1 subset of spec §15 (`pipeline`, `source`, `sink`), loaded from one YAML file with `PGCDC__<SECTION>__<KEY>` environment overrides, passwords from the environment only, durations with units, and strict validation (Ruling R12).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/config.rs` (empty file from Task 1), `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces:
  - `pub const ENV_PREFIX: &str = "PGCDC__"`
  - `pub struct Config { pub pipeline: String, pub source: SourceConfig, pub sink: SinkConfig }`
  - `pub struct SourceConfig { pub host: String, pub port: u16, pub database: String, pub user: String, pub credentials: Credentials, pub tls: TlsSection, pub slot: String, pub publication: String, pub status_interval: Duration }` (defaults: port 5432, `tls.mode` verify-full, `status_interval` 10 s)
  - `pub enum Credentials { Password { password: Option<SecretString> }, AwsRdsIam { region: Option<String> } }` (YAML `type: password` / `type: aws_rds_iam`)
  - `pub struct TlsSection { pub mode: TlsMode, pub ca_file: Option<PathBuf> }`; `pub enum TlsMode { Disable, Require, VerifyCa, VerifyFull }` (YAML `disable`, `require`, `verify-ca`, `verify-full`; `Default` is `VerifyFull`; `Copy`)
  - `pub enum SinkConfig { Stdout }` (YAML `type: stdout`)
  - `pub fn load(path: &Path, env: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, String)>) -> Result<Config, ConfigError>`; `pub fn parse(yaml: &str, env: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, String)>) -> Result<Config, ConfigError>`; `pub fn parse_duration(text: &str) -> Option<Duration>`
  - `pub enum ConfigError { Read { path, source }, Yaml(String), Invalid(serde_json::Error), PasswordInFile, MissingPassword, BadEnvKey(String), InvalidValue { field: &'static str, value: String, reason: &'static str } }`

At the end of `crates/pg-cdc/src/config.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use secrecy::ExposeSecret;

    const MINIMAL: &str = "
pipeline: orders
source:
  host: db.internal
  database: app
  user: pgcdc
  credentials:
    type: password
  slot: pgcdc_orders
  publication: pgcdc_orders
sink:
  type: stdout
";

    fn env(pairs: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        pairs.iter().map(|(key, value)| ((*key).to_owned(), (*value).to_owned())).collect()
    }

    fn with_password() -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        env(&[("PGCDC__SOURCE__CREDENTIALS__PASSWORD", "s3cr3t pass")])
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_minimal_password_config_with_defaults() {
        let config = parse(MINIMAL, with_password()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(config.pipeline, "orders");
        assert_eq!(config.source.host, "db.internal");
        assert_eq!(config.source.port, 5432);
        assert_eq!(config.source.tls.mode, TlsMode::VerifyFull);
        assert_eq!(config.source.tls.ca_file, None);
        assert_eq!(config.source.status_interval, Duration::from_secs(10));
        assert_eq!(config.sink, SinkConfig::Stdout);
        let Credentials::Password { password: Some(password) } = &config.source.credentials else {
            panic!("expected a password");
        };
        assert_eq!(password.expose_secret(), "s3cr3t pass");
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_aws_rds_iam_with_tls_and_region() {
        let yaml = MINIMAL.replace("type: password", "type: aws_rds_iam\n    region: us-east-1").replace(
            "  slot:",
            "  tls:\n    mode: verify-ca\n    ca_file: /etc/pg-cdc/rds.pem\n  slot:",
        );
        let config = parse(&yaml, env(&[])).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(&config.source.credentials, Credentials::AwsRdsIam { region: Some(region) } if region == "us-east-1"));
        assert_eq!(config.source.tls.mode, TlsMode::VerifyCa);
        assert_eq!(config.source.tls.ca_file, Some(PathBuf::from("/etc/pg-cdc/rds.pem")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn environment_overrides_any_key() {
        let mut vars = with_password();
        vars.extend(env(&[
            ("PGCDC__SOURCE__PORT", "6543"),
            ("PGCDC__SOURCE__TLS__MODE", "require"),
            ("PGCDC__SOURCE__STATUS_INTERVAL", "250ms"),
            ("PGCDC__PIPELINE", "billing"),
            ("HOME", "/root"),
            ("PGCDCX__SOURCE__PORT", "1"),
        ]));
        let config = parse(MINIMAL, vars).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(config.source.port, 6543);
        assert_eq!(config.source.tls.mode, TlsMode::Require);
        assert_eq!(config.source.status_interval, Duration::from_millis(250));
        assert_eq!(config.pipeline, "billing");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_password_in_file() {
        let yaml = MINIMAL.replace("type: password", "type: password\n    password: hunter2");
        assert!(matches!(parse(&yaml, with_password()), Err(ConfigError::PasswordInFile)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn password_credentials_need_the_environment_password() {
        assert!(matches!(parse(MINIMAL, env(&[])), Err(ConfigError::MissingPassword)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unknown_keys_and_types() {
        let unknown_key = MINIMAL.replace("  slot:", "  sslmode: require\n  slot:");
        assert!(matches!(parse(&unknown_key, with_password()), Err(ConfigError::Invalid(_))));
        let unknown_sink = MINIMAL.replace("type: stdout", "type: kafka");
        assert!(matches!(parse(&unknown_sink, with_password()), Err(ConfigError::Invalid(_))));
        assert!(matches!(parse("pipeline: [", with_password()), Err(ConfigError::Yaml(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unsafe_names() {
        for slot in ["Orders", "orders-x", "a b", "o'r", "", &"s".repeat(64)] {
            let yaml = MINIMAL.replace("slot: pgcdc_orders", &format!("slot: \"{slot}\""));
            assert!(
                matches!(parse(&yaml, with_password()), Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue { field: "source.slot", .. })),
                "slot {slot:?}"
            );
        }
        let publication = MINIMAL.replace("publication: pgcdc_orders", "publication: \"pub;drop\"");
        assert!(matches!(
            parse(&publication, with_password()),
            Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue { field: "source.publication", .. })
        ));
        let pipeline = MINIMAL.replace("pipeline: orders", "pipeline: \"orders/../x\"");
        assert!(matches!(parse(&pipeline, with_password()), Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue { field: "pipeline", .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_bad_env_keys_and_values() {
        let mut empty_segment = with_password();
        empty_segment.push(("PGCDC__SOURCE____PORT".to_owned(), "1".to_owned()));
        assert!(matches!(parse(MINIMAL, empty_segment), Err(ConfigError::BadEnvKey(_))));
        let mut scalar_parent = with_password();
        scalar_parent.push(("PGCDC__PIPELINE__NAME".to_owned(), "x".to_owned()));
        assert!(matches!(parse(MINIMAL, scalar_parent), Err(ConfigError::BadEnvKey(_))));
        let mut bad_port = with_password();
        bad_port.push(("PGCDC__SOURCE__PORT".to_owned(), "70000".to_owned()));
        assert!(matches!(parse(MINIMAL, bad_port), Err(ConfigError::Invalid(_))));
        let mut zero_interval = with_password();
        zero_interval.push(("PGCDC__SOURCE__STATUS_INTERVAL".to_owned(), "0s".to_owned()));
        assert!(matches!(
            parse(MINIMAL, zero_interval),
            Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue { field: "source.status_interval", .. })
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_durations_with_units() {
        assert_eq!(parse_duration("250ms"), Some(Duration::from_millis(250)));
        assert_eq!(parse_duration("30s"), Some(Duration::from_secs(30)));
        assert_eq!(parse_duration("5m"), Some(Duration::from_secs(300)));
        assert_eq!(parse_duration("2h"), Some(Duration::from_secs(7200)));
        for bad in ["", "10", "s", "1.5s", "5d", "-1s", "1 s", "99999999999999999999s"] {
            assert_eq!(parse_duration(bad), None, "{bad:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn password_is_redacted_in_debug() {
        let config = parse(MINIMAL, with_password()).unwrap();
        let debug = format!("{config:?}");
        assert!(!debug.contains("s3cr3t"), "{debug}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn load_reads_the_file() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let path = dir.path().join("pg-cdc.yaml");
        std::fs::write(&path, MINIMAL).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(load(&path, with_password()).unwrap().pipeline, "orders");
        assert!(matches!(load(&dir.path().join("missing.yaml"), with_password()), Err(ConfigError::Read { .. })));
    }
}
```

Above the test module in `crates/pg-cdc/src/config.rs`:

```rust
use std::fmt;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::Duration;

use secrecy::SecretString;
use serde::de::{self, Deserializer, Visitor};
use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::{Map, Value};

pub const ENV_PREFIX: &str = "PGCDC__";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Config {
    pub pipeline: String,
    pub source: SourceConfig,
    pub sink: SinkConfig,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct SourceConfig {
    pub host: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_port", deserialize_with = "de_port")]
    pub port: u16,
    pub database: String,
    pub user: String,
    pub credentials: Credentials,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub tls: TlsSection,
    pub slot: String,
    pub publication: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_status_interval", deserialize_with = "de_duration")]
    pub status_interval: Duration,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub enum Credentials {
    Password { password: Option<SecretString> },
    AwsRdsIam { region: Option<String> },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct TlsSection {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub mode: TlsMode,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub ca_file: Option<PathBuf>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum TlsMode {
    Disable,
    Require,
    VerifyCa,
    #[default]
    VerifyFull,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub enum SinkConfig {
    Stdout,
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum ConfigError {
    #[error("cannot read {path}: {source}")]
    Read { path: PathBuf, source: std::io::Error },
    #[error("invalid YAML: {0}")]
    Yaml(String),
    #[error("invalid configuration: {0}")]
    Invalid(#[from] serde_json::Error),
    #[error("source.credentials.password must come from PGCDC__SOURCE__CREDENTIALS__PASSWORD, not the config file")]
    PasswordInFile,
    #[error("password credentials need PGCDC__SOURCE__CREDENTIALS__PASSWORD")]
    MissingPassword,
    #[error("environment variable {0} does not name a configuration key")]
    BadEnvKey(String),
    #[error("{field} {value:?} is invalid: {reason}")]
    InvalidValue { field: &'static str, value: String, reason: &'static str },
}

pub fn load(path: &Path, env: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, String)>) -> Result<Config, ConfigError> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|source| ConfigError::Read { path: path.to_owned(), source })?;
    parse(&text, env)
}

pub fn parse(yaml: &str, env: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, String)>) -> Result<Config, ConfigError> {
    let mut tree: Value = serde_saphyr::from_str(yaml).map_err(|error| ConfigError::Yaml(error.to_string()))?;
    if tree.pointer("/source/credentials/password").is_some() {
        return Err(ConfigError::PasswordInFile);
    }
    for (name, value) in env {
        let Some(path) = name.strip_prefix(ENV_PREFIX) else {
            continue;
        };
        let keys: Vec<String> = path.split("__").map(str::to_ascii_lowercase).collect();
        if keys.iter().any(String::is_empty) || set_path(&mut tree, &keys, Value::String(value)).is_none() {
            return Err(ConfigError::BadEnvKey(name));
        }
    }
    let config: Config = serde_json::from_value(tree)?;
    config.validate()?;
    Ok(config)
}

fn set_path(tree: &mut Value, keys: &[String], value: Value) -> Option<()> {
    let (last, parents) = keys.split_last()?;
    let mut node = tree;
    for key in parents {
        if node.is_null() {
            *node = Value::Object(Map::new());
        }
        node = node.as_object_mut()?.entry(key.clone()).or_insert(Value::Null);
    }
    if node.is_null() {
        *node = Value::Object(Map::new());
    }
    node.as_object_mut()?.insert(last.clone(), value);
    Some(())
}

pub fn parse_duration(text: &str) -> Option<Duration> {
    let split = text.find(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit())?;
    let (digits, unit) = text.split_at_checked(split)?;
    let amount: u64 = digits.parse().ok()?;
    match unit {
        "ms" => Some(Duration::from_millis(amount)),
        "s" => Some(Duration::from_secs(amount)),
        "m" => amount.checked_mul(60).map(Duration::from_secs),
        "h" => amount.checked_mul(3600).map(Duration::from_secs),
        _ => None,
    }
}

impl Config {
    fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {
        let slot_char = |byte: u8| byte.is_ascii_lowercase() || byte.is_ascii_digit() || byte == b'_';
        let pipeline_char = |byte: u8| byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || byte == b'_' || byte == b'-';
        check_name("pipeline", &self.pipeline, pipeline_char, "must be 1 to 63 letters, digits, '_' or '-'")?;
        let slot_reason = "must be 1 to 63 lowercase letters, digits or underscores";
        check_name("source.slot", &self.source.slot, slot_char, slot_reason)?;
        check_name("source.publication", &self.source.publication, slot_char, slot_reason)?;
        for (field, value) in [
            ("source.host", &self.source.host),
            ("source.database", &self.source.database),
            ("source.user", &self.source.user),
        ] {
            if value.is_empty() {
                return Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue { field, value: value.clone(), reason: "must not be empty" });
            }
        }
        if self.source.status_interval.is_zero() {
            return Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue {
                field: "source.status_interval",
                value: "0".to_owned(),
                reason: "must be greater than zero",
            });
        }
        if matches!(self.source.credentials, Credentials::Password { password: None }) {
            return Err(ConfigError::MissingPassword);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn check_name(
    field: &'static str,
    value: &str,
    allowed: impl Fn(u8) -> bool,
    reason: &'static str,
) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {
    if value.is_empty() || value.len() > 63 || !value.bytes().all(allowed) {
        return Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue { field, value: value.to_owned(), reason });
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn default_port() -> u16 {
    5432
}

fn default_status_interval() -> Duration {
    Duration::from_secs(10)
}

fn de_duration<'de, D: Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Duration, D::Error> {
    let text = String::deserialize(deserializer)?;
    parse_duration(&text)
        .ok_or_else(|| de::Error::custom(format!("invalid duration {text:?}; use an integer with ms, s, m or h")))
}

fn de_port<'de, D: Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<u16, D::Error> {
    struct PortVisitor;

    impl Visitor<'_> for PortVisitor {
        type Value = u16;

        fn expecting(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
            formatter.write_str("a TCP port number")
        }

        fn visit_u64<E: de::Error>(self, value: u64) -> Result<u16, E> {
            u16::try_from(value).map_err(|_| E::custom(format!("port {value} is out of range")))
        }

        fn visit_i64<E: de::Error>(self, value: i64) -> Result<u16, E> {
            u16::try_from(value).map_err(|_| E::custom(format!("port {value} is out of range")))
        }

        fn visit_str<E: de::Error>(self, value: &str) -> Result<u16, E> {
            value.parse().map_err(|_| E::custom(format!("port {value:?} is not a number from 0 to 65535")))
        }
    }

    deserializer.deserialize_any(PortVisitor)
}
```

`PortVisitor` is a private local type, so `missing_debug_implementations` (public types only) does not apply. `serde_saphyr::from_str::<T>(&str)` is the serde-saphyr 2.0.0 entry point.

### Task 7: Connection providers, TLS and control connection
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 2, Task 6
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** normal

Implements `pg_cdc::connection` (spec §6.1, §11.7): the `ConnectionProvider` trait, the `password` and `aws_rds_iam` providers, rustls client configurations for the four TLS modes (Ruling R18), and the control connection over tokio-postgres with the pinned session settings. The provider is called before every connection attempt, so an IAM token is fresh each time.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/mod.rs` (empty from Task 1), `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/password.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/rds_iam.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/tls.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/control.rs`
- Test: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/connection.rs`; unit tests in `crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/mod.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc::config::TlsMode` (Task 6); `pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, PostgresOptions}` (Task 2, tests).
- Produces:
  - `pub const SESSION_OPTIONS: &str = "-c DateStyle=ISO,MDY -c TimeZone=UTC -c IntervalStyle=postgres -c extra_float_digits=1 -c bytea_output=hex"`
  - `pub struct TlsConfig { pub mode: TlsMode, pub ca_file: Option<PathBuf> }`
  - `pub struct ConnectParams { pub host: String, pub port: u16, pub database: String, pub user: String, pub password: Option<SecretString>, pub tls: TlsConfig }` (`Debug` redacts the password, `Clone`)
  - `#[async_trait] pub trait ConnectionProvider: Send + Sync { async fn params(&self) -> Result<ConnectParams, ConnectError>; }`
  - `pub struct PasswordProvider` with `new(params: ConnectParams)`; `pub struct RdsIamProvider` with `new(base: ConnectParams, sdk: aws_config::SdkConfig)` and `async fn from_environment(base: ConnectParams, region: Option<String>) -> Self`
  - `pub struct ControlConnection` with `client(&self) -> &tokio_postgres::Client` (aborts its connection task on drop); `pub async fn connect_control(params: &ConnectParams) -> Result<ControlConnection, ConnectError>`
  - `pub enum ConnectError { Token(String), CaFile { path, source }, EmptyCaFile(PathBuf), Tls(rustls::Error), Verifier(rustls::client::VerifierBuilderError), Postgres(tokio_postgres::Error) }`

```rust
use aws_config::{BehaviorVersion, Region};
use aws_sdk_rds::config::Credentials;
use pg_cdc::config::TlsMode;
use pg_cdc::connection::{
    ConnectParams, ConnectionProvider, PasswordProvider, RdsIamProvider, TlsConfig, connect_control,
};
use pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, PostgresOptions};
use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};

type TestResult = Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

fn params(pg: &Postgres, mode: TlsMode) -> ConnectParams {
    ConnectParams {
        host: pg.host().to_owned(),
        port: pg.port(),
        database: pg.database().to_owned(),
        user: pg.user().to_owned(),
        password: Some(SecretString::from(pg.password().to_owned())),
        tls: TlsConfig { mode, ca_file: pg.ca_file().map(std::path::Path::to_path_buf) },
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn password_provider_returns_its_params_every_time() -> TestResult {
    let base = ConnectParams {
        host: "db.internal".to_owned(),
        port: 6432,
        database: "app".to_owned(),
        user: "pgcdc".to_owned(),
        password: Some(SecretString::from("secret".to_owned())),
        tls: TlsConfig { mode: TlsMode::Require, ca_file: None },
    };
    let provider = PasswordProvider::new(base);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        let params = provider.params().await?;
        assert_eq!((params.host.as_str(), params.port, params.user.as_str()), ("db.internal", 6432, "pgcdc"));
        assert_eq!(params.password.as_ref().map(ExposeSecret::expose_secret), Some("secret"));
        assert_eq!(params.tls.mode, TlsMode::Require);
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn rds_iam_provider_signs_a_fresh_connect_token() -> TestResult {
    let sdk = aws_config::defaults(BehaviorVersion::latest())
        .region(Region::new("us-east-1"))
        .credentials_provider(Credentials::new("AKIDEXAMPLE", "wJalrXUtnFEMI", None, None, "test"))
        .load()
        .await;
    let base = ConnectParams {
        host: "db.cluster-xyz.us-east-1.rds.amazonaws.com".to_owned(),
        port: 5432,
        database: "app".to_owned(),
        user: "pgcdc".to_owned(),
        password: None,
        tls: TlsConfig { mode: TlsMode::VerifyFull, ca_file: None },
    };
    let provider = RdsIamProvider::new(base, sdk);
    let params = provider.params().await?;
    let token = params.password.as_ref().ok_or("no token")?.expose_secret().to_owned();
    assert!(token.starts_with("db.cluster-xyz.us-east-1.rds.amazonaws.com:5432/?Action=connect&DBUser=pgcdc&"), "{token}");
    for part in ["X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256", "X-Amz-Credential=AKIDEXAMPLE", "us-east-1", "X-Amz-Expires=900", "X-Amz-Signature="] {
        assert!(token.contains(part), "{part} missing from {token}");
    }
    assert!(!token.contains("wJalrXUtnFEMI"));
    assert_eq!(
        (params.host.as_str(), params.port, params.database.as_str()),
        ("db.cluster-xyz.us-east-1.rds.amazonaws.com", 5432, "app")
    );
    assert!(!format!("{params:?}").contains("X-Amz-Signature"));
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn control_connection_pins_session_settings() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    for setting in [
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET timezone = 'America/New_York'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET datestyle = 'SQL, DMY'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET intervalstyle = 'iso_8601'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET bytea_output = 'escape'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET extra_float_digits = 0",
    ] {
        pg.execute(setting).await?;
    }
    let control = connect_control(&params(&pg, TlsMode::Disable)).await?;
    for (setting, expected) in [
        ("TimeZone", "UTC"),
        ("DateStyle", "ISO, MDY"),
        ("IntervalStyle", "postgres"),
        ("bytea_output", "hex"),
        ("extra_float_digits", "1"),
        ("application_name", "pg-cdc"),
    ] {
        let value: String = control.client().query_one(&format!("SHOW {setting}"), &[]).await?.try_get(0)?;
        assert_eq!(value, expected, "{setting}");
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn connects_with_special_character_password() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute(r#"CREATE ROLE "pg cdc" LOGIN PASSWORD 'p@ss w:rd/''"&=%'"#).await?;
    let mut params = params(&pg, TlsMode::Disable);
    params.user = "pg cdc".to_owned();
    params.password = Some(SecretString::from(r#"p@ss w:rd/'"&=%"#.to_owned()));
    let control = connect_control(&params).await?;
    let user: String = control.client().query_one("SELECT current_user::text", &[]).await?.try_get(0)?;
    assert_eq!(user, "pg cdc");
    Ok(())
}

async fn ssl_in_use(params: &ConnectParams) -> Result<bool, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    let control = connect_control(params).await?;
    let row = control.client().query_one("SELECT ssl FROM pg_stat_ssl WHERE pid = pg_backend_pid()", &[]).await?;
    Ok(row.try_get(0)?)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn tls_modes_encrypt_and_verify_as_configured() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start_with(PostgresOptions { tls: true }).await?;

    let mut require = params(&pg, TlsMode::Require);
    require.tls.ca_file = None;
    assert!(ssl_in_use(&require).await?);

    let mut verify_full = params(&pg, TlsMode::VerifyFull);
    verify_full.host = "localhost".to_owned();
    assert!(ssl_in_use(&verify_full).await?);

    let mut wrong_host = params(&pg, TlsMode::VerifyFull);
    wrong_host.host = "127.0.0.1".to_owned();
    assert!(connect_control(&wrong_host).await.is_err(), "verify-full must check the host name");

    let verify_ca = params(&pg, TlsMode::VerifyCa);
    assert_eq!(verify_ca.host, "127.0.0.1");
    assert!(ssl_in_use(&verify_ca).await?);

    let mut unknown_ca = params(&pg, TlsMode::VerifyFull);
    unknown_ca.host = "localhost".to_owned();
    unknown_ca.tls.ca_file = None;
    assert!(connect_control(&unknown_ca).await.is_err(), "the public roots must not trust the test CA");

    let disabled = params(&pg, TlsMode::Disable);
    assert!(!ssl_in_use(&disabled).await?);
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn missing_ca_file_is_a_clear_error() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    let mut params = params(&pg, TlsMode::VerifyFull);
    params.tls.ca_file = Some("/nonexistent/ca.pem".into());
    let error = connect_control(&params).await.err().ok_or("expected an error")?;
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("/nonexistent/ca.pem"), "{error}");
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
mod control;
mod password;
mod rds_iam;
mod tls;

use std::path::PathBuf;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use secrecy::SecretString;

use crate::config::TlsMode;

pub use control::{ControlConnection, connect_control};
pub use password::PasswordProvider;
pub use rds_iam::RdsIamProvider;

pub const SESSION_OPTIONS: &str =
    "-c DateStyle=ISO,MDY -c TimeZone=UTC -c IntervalStyle=postgres -c extra_float_digits=1 -c bytea_output=hex";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TlsConfig {
    pub mode: TlsMode,
    pub ca_file: Option<PathBuf>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ConnectParams {
    pub host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub database: String,
    pub user: String,
    pub password: Option<SecretString>,
    pub tls: TlsConfig,
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait ConnectionProvider: Send + Sync {
    async fn params(&self) -> Result<ConnectParams, ConnectError>;
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum ConnectError {
    #[error("cannot generate an RDS IAM token: {0}")]
    Token(String),
    #[error("cannot read CA file {path}: {source}")]
    CaFile { path: PathBuf, source: rustls_pki_types::pem::Error },
    #[error("CA file {0} holds no certificates")]
    EmptyCaFile(PathBuf),
    #[error("TLS setup failed: {0}")]
    Tls(#[from] rustls::Error),
    #[error("TLS verifier setup failed: {0}")]
    Verifier(#[from] rustls::client::VerifierBuilderError),
    #[error("control connection failed: {0}")]
    Postgres(#[from] tokio_postgres::Error),
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/password.rs`:

```rust
use async_trait::async_trait;

use super::{ConnectError, ConnectParams, ConnectionProvider};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PasswordProvider {
    params: ConnectParams,
}

impl PasswordProvider {
    pub fn new(params: ConnectParams) -> Self {
        Self { params }
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl ConnectionProvider for PasswordProvider {
    async fn params(&self) -> Result<ConnectParams, ConnectError> {
        Ok(self.params.clone())
    }
}
```

```rust
use async_trait::async_trait;
use aws_config::{BehaviorVersion, Region, SdkConfig};
use aws_sdk_rds::auth_token::{AuthTokenGenerator, Config};
use aws_smithy_http_client::tls::{Provider, rustls_provider::CryptoMode};
use secrecy::SecretString;

use super::{ConnectError, ConnectParams, ConnectionProvider};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct RdsIamProvider {
    base: ConnectParams,
    sdk: SdkConfig,
}

impl RdsIamProvider {
    pub fn new(base: ConnectParams, sdk: SdkConfig) -> Self {
        Self { base, sdk }
    }

    pub async fn from_environment(base: ConnectParams, region: Option<String>) -> Self {
        let http_client = aws_smithy_http_client::Builder::new()
            .tls_provider(Provider::Rustls(CryptoMode::Ring))
            .build_https();
        let mut loader = aws_config::defaults(BehaviorVersion::latest()).http_client(http_client);
        if let Some(region) = region {
            loader = loader.region(Region::new(region));
        }
        Self::new(base, loader.load().await)
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl ConnectionProvider for RdsIamProvider {
    async fn params(&self) -> Result<ConnectParams, ConnectError> {
        let mut builder = Config::builder()
            .hostname(&self.base.host)
            .port(u64::from(self.base.port))
            .username(&self.base.user);
        if let Some(region) = self.sdk.region() {
            builder = builder.region(region.clone());
        }
        let config = builder.build().map_err(|error| ConnectError::Token(error.to_string()))?;
        let token = AuthTokenGenerator::new(config)
            .auth_token(&self.sdk)
            .await
            .map_err(|error| ConnectError::Token(error.to_string()))?;
        Ok(ConnectParams { password: Some(SecretString::from(token.as_str().to_owned())), ..self.base.clone() })
    }
}
```

`AuthTokenGenerator::auth_token` signs offline with the SDK config's credentials and time source; it makes no network call.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement TLS and the control connection**

`crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/tls.rs`:

```rust
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;

use rustls::client::WebPkiServerVerifier;
use rustls::client::danger::{HandshakeSignatureValid, ServerCertVerified, ServerCertVerifier};
use rustls::crypto::{CryptoProvider, verify_tls12_signature, verify_tls13_signature};
use rustls::{CertificateError, ClientConfig, DigitallySignedStruct, RootCertStore, SignatureScheme};
use rustls_pki_types::pem::PemObject;
use rustls_pki_types::{CertificateDer, ServerName, UnixTime};

use super::{ConnectError, TlsConfig};
use crate::config::TlsMode;

pub(super) fn client_config(tls: &TlsConfig) -> Result<Option<ClientConfig>, ConnectError> {
    let provider = Arc::new(rustls::crypto::ring::default_provider());
    let builder = ClientConfig::builder_with_provider(Arc::clone(&provider)).with_safe_default_protocol_versions()?;
    let config = match tls.mode {
        TlsMode::Disable => return Ok(None),
        TlsMode::Require => builder
            .dangerous()
            .with_custom_certificate_verifier(Arc::new(AcceptAnyCertificate { provider }))
            .with_no_client_auth(),
        TlsMode::VerifyCa => {
            let roots = Arc::new(root_store(tls.ca_file.as_deref())?);
            let inner = WebPkiServerVerifier::builder_with_provider(roots, provider).build()?;
            builder
                .dangerous()
                .with_custom_certificate_verifier(Arc::new(IgnoreHostName { inner }))
                .with_no_client_auth()
        }
        TlsMode::VerifyFull => builder.with_root_certificates(root_store(tls.ca_file.as_deref())?).with_no_client_auth(),
    };
    Ok(Some(config))
}

fn root_store(ca_file: Option<&Path>) -> Result<RootCertStore, ConnectError> {
    let Some(path) = ca_file else {
        return Ok(webpki_roots::TLS_SERVER_ROOTS.iter().cloned().collect());
    };
    let ca_error = |source| ConnectError::CaFile { path: path.to_owned(), source };
    let mut store = RootCertStore::empty();
    for certificate in CertificateDer::pem_file_iter(path).map_err(ca_error)? {
        store.add(certificate.map_err(ca_error)?)?;
    }
    if store.is_empty() {
        return Err(ConnectError::EmptyCaFile(path.to_owned()));
    }
    Ok(store)
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct AcceptAnyCertificate {
    provider: Arc<CryptoProvider>,
}

impl ServerCertVerifier for AcceptAnyCertificate {
    fn verify_server_cert(
        &self,
        _end_entity: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        _intermediates: &[CertificateDer<'_>],
        _server_name: &ServerName<'_>,
        _ocsp_response: &[u8],
        _now: UnixTime,
    ) -> Result<ServerCertVerified, rustls::Error> {
        Ok(ServerCertVerified::assertion())
    }

    fn verify_tls12_signature(
        &self,
        message: &[u8],
        cert: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        dss: &DigitallySignedStruct,
    ) -> Result<HandshakeSignatureValid, rustls::Error> {
        verify_tls12_signature(message, cert, dss, &self.provider.signature_verification_algorithms)
    }

    fn verify_tls13_signature(
        &self,
        message: &[u8],
        cert: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        dss: &DigitallySignedStruct,
    ) -> Result<HandshakeSignatureValid, rustls::Error> {
        verify_tls13_signature(message, cert, dss, &self.provider.signature_verification_algorithms)
    }

    fn supported_verify_schemes(&self) -> Vec<SignatureScheme> {
        self.provider.signature_verification_algorithms.supported_schemes()
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct IgnoreHostName {
    inner: Arc<WebPkiServerVerifier>,
}

impl ServerCertVerifier for IgnoreHostName {
    fn verify_server_cert(
        &self,
        end_entity: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        intermediates: &[CertificateDer<'_>],
        server_name: &ServerName<'_>,
        ocsp_response: &[u8],
        now: UnixTime,
    ) -> Result<ServerCertVerified, rustls::Error> {
        match self.inner.verify_server_cert(end_entity, intermediates, server_name, ocsp_response, now) {
            Err(rustls::Error::InvalidCertificate(
                CertificateError::NotValidForName | CertificateError::NotValidForNameContext { .. },
            )) => Ok(ServerCertVerified::assertion()),
            other => other,
        }
    }

    fn verify_tls12_signature(
        &self,
        message: &[u8],
        cert: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        dss: &DigitallySignedStruct,
    ) -> Result<HandshakeSignatureValid, rustls::Error> {
        self.inner.verify_tls12_signature(message, cert, dss)
    }

    fn verify_tls13_signature(
        &self,
        message: &[u8],
        cert: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        dss: &DigitallySignedStruct,
    ) -> Result<HandshakeSignatureValid, rustls::Error> {
        self.inner.verify_tls13_signature(message, cert, dss)
    }

    fn supported_verify_schemes(&self) -> Vec<SignatureScheme> {
        self.inner.supported_verify_schemes()
    }
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/connection/control.rs`:

```rust
use std::fmt;

use secrecy::ExposeSecret;
use tokio::io::{AsyncRead, AsyncWrite};
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;
use tokio_postgres::config::SslMode;
use tokio_postgres::{Client, Connection, NoTls};
use tokio_postgres_rustls::MakeRustlsConnect;

use super::{ConnectError, ConnectParams, SESSION_OPTIONS, tls};

pub struct ControlConnection {
    client: Client,
    task: JoinHandle<()>,
}

impl ControlConnection {
    pub fn client(&self) -> &Client {
        &self.client
    }
}

impl fmt::Debug for ControlConnection {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("ControlConnection").finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

impl Drop for ControlConnection {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.task.abort();
    }
}

pub async fn connect_control(params: &ConnectParams) -> Result<ControlConnection, ConnectError> {
    let mut config = tokio_postgres::Config::new();
    config
        .host(&params.host)
        .port(params.port)
        .dbname(&params.database)
        .user(&params.user)
        .options(SESSION_OPTIONS)
        .application_name("pg-cdc");
    if let Some(password) = &params.password {
        config.password(password.expose_secret());
    }
    match tls::client_config(&params.tls)? {
        None => {
            config.ssl_mode(SslMode::Disable);
            let (client, connection) = config.connect(NoTls).await?;
            Ok(spawn(client, connection))
        }
        Some(tls_config) => {
            config.ssl_mode(SslMode::Require);
            let (client, connection) = config.connect(MakeRustlsConnect::new(tls_config)).await?;
            Ok(spawn(client, connection))
        }
    }
}

fn spawn<S, T>(client: Client, connection: Connection<S, T>) -> ControlConnection
where
    S: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send + 'static,
    T: AsyncRead + AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send + 'static,
{
    let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
        if let Err(error) = connection.await {
            tracing::warn!(%error, "control connection closed");
        }
    });
    ControlConnection { client, task }
}
```

tokio-postgres re-resolves the host name on every connect, so a failover to a new address is picked up by the next attempt.

Expected: 7 tests pass (1 unit, 6 integration).

Run: `cargo tree -e normal -i aws-lc-sys; cargo tree -e normal -i openssl-sys`
Expected: both report that no package matches.

---

### Task 8: Event model: ids, envelope and builder
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 4, Task 5, Task 6
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Implements `pg_cdc::event` (spec §6.5, §7.1–§7.4): event ids derived only from the WAL (O1), the version 1 envelope and its exact JSON form, and the builder that turns decoded row changes plus relation metadata into envelopes, including `before` images per replica identity, unchanged-TOAST filling, keys and truncate events. The builder is pure; it does no I/O.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/mod.rs` (empty from Task 1), `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/id.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/envelope.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/builder.rs`
- Test: unit tests in `id.rs` and `builder.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn`; `pg_cdc_decoder::message::{Insert, Update, Delete, OldTuple, Tuple, TupleValue}` (Task 4); `pg_cdc_decoder::value::{TypeKind, map_value, format_commit_ts}` (Task 5); the `serde`, `serde_json` and `thiserror` entries Task 6 adds to `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`.
- Produces (re-exported from `pg_cdc::event`):
  - `pub struct EventId` (`Copy`, `Ord`, `Hash`) with `new(commit_lsn: Lsn, ordinal: u32)`, `commit_lsn(self) -> Lsn`, `ordinal(self) -> u32`; `Display` and `Serialize` as `{commit_lsn:016x}-{ordinal:08x}`
  - `pub enum Op { Insert, Update, Delete, Truncate }` (serializes lowercase); `pub type Row = serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value>`; `pub const ENVELOPE_VERSION: u8 = 1`
  - `pub struct Envelope { pub id: EventId, pub version: u8, pub op: Op, pub schema: String, pub table: String, pub key: Option<Row>, pub before: Option<Row>, pub after: Option<Row>, pub unchanged_toast: Vec<String>, pub source: Source }` with `encode(&self) -> Result<Vec<u8>, serde_json::Error>`; field order in JSON is exactly that order, `unchanged_toast` omitted when empty
  - `pub struct Source { pub pipeline: String, pub commit_lsn: String, pub commit_ts: String }`
  - `pub struct ColumnInfo { pub name: String, pub type_oid: u32, pub kind: TypeKind, pub is_identity: bool }`; `pub struct RelationInfo { pub oid: u32, pub schema: String, pub table: String, pub columns: Vec<ColumnInfo>, pub key_columns: Vec<String> }`
  - `pub struct Fallback { pub column: String, pub type_oid: u32 }`; `pub struct Built { pub envelope: Envelope, pub fallbacks: Vec<Fallback> }`
  - `pub struct Builder` with `new(pipeline: impl Into<String>)`, `begin(&mut self, commit_lsn: Lsn, commit_ts_micros: i64) -> Result<(), BuildError>`, `commit(&mut self) -> Result<(), BuildError>`, `insert(&mut self, &RelationInfo, &Insert) -> Result<Built, BuildError>`, `update(&mut self, &RelationInfo, &Update)`, `delete(&mut self, &RelationInfo, &Delete)`, `truncate(&mut self, &RelationInfo)`
  - `pub enum BuildError { NoOpenTransaction, TransactionAlreadyOpen, TupleShape { relation: u32, expected: usize, actual: usize }, OrdinalOverflow }`
