Tests at the end of `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/id.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use proptest::prelude::*;

    #[test]
    fn formats_as_padded_lowercase_hex() {
        assert_eq!(EventId::new(Lsn(0x1A2B_3C4D), 3).to_string(), "000000001a2b3c4d-00000003");
        assert_eq!(EventId::new(Lsn(u64::MAX), u32::MAX).to_string(), "ffffffffffffffff-ffffffff");
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_string(&EventId::new(Lsn(1), 0)).unwrap(), "\"0000000000000001-00000000\"");
    }

    proptest! {
        #[test]
        fn string_order_matches_stream_order(a in any::<(u64, u32)>(), b in any::<(u64, u32)>()) {
            let left = EventId::new(Lsn(a.0), a.1);
            let right = EventId::new(Lsn(b.0), b.1);
            prop_assert_eq!(left.cmp(&right), left.to_string().cmp(&right.to_string()));
        }
    }
}
```

Tests at the end of `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/builder.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use pg_cdc_decoder::message::{Delete, Insert, OldTuple, Tuple, TupleValue, Update};
    use pg_cdc_decoder::value::TypeKind;
    use serde_json::json;

    const COMMIT_LSN: Lsn = Lsn(0x1A2B_3C4D);
    const COMMIT_TS: i64 = 845_294_400_123_456;

    fn column(name: &str, kind: TypeKind, type_oid: u32, is_identity: bool) -> ColumnInfo {
        ColumnInfo { name: name.to_owned(), type_oid, kind, is_identity }
    }

    fn orders(columns: Vec<ColumnInfo>) -> RelationInfo {
        RelationInfo {
            oid: 16_384,
            schema: "public".to_owned(),
            table: "orders".to_owned(),
            columns,
            key_columns: vec!["id".to_owned()],
        }
    }

    fn two_columns() -> RelationInfo {
        orders(vec![column("id", TypeKind::Integer, 23, true), column("status", TypeKind::Text, 25, false)])
    }

    fn three_columns() -> RelationInfo {
        orders(vec![
            column("id", TypeKind::Integer, 23, true),
            column("status", TypeKind::Text, 25, false),
            column("note", TypeKind::Text, 25, false),
        ])
    }

    fn t(value: &str) -> TupleValue {
        TupleValue::Text(value.to_owned())
    }

    fn open() -> Builder {
        let mut builder = Builder::new("orders");
        builder.begin(COMMIT_LSN, COMMIT_TS).unwrap();
        builder
    }

    fn encoded(built: &Built) -> String {
        String::from_utf8(built.envelope.encode().unwrap()).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn insert_encodes_the_golden_envelope() {
        let mut builder = open();
        let insert = Insert { relation_oid: 16_384, new: Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("paid"), t("n")]) };
        let built = builder.insert(&three_columns(), &insert).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            encoded(&built),
            r#"{"id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000000","version":1,"op":"insert","schema":"public","table":"orders","key":{"id":42},"before":null,"after":{"id":42,"status":"paid","note":"n"},"source":{"pipeline":"orders","commit_lsn":"0/1A2B3C4D","commit_ts":"2026-10-14T12:00:00.123456+00:00"}}"#
        );
        assert_eq!(built.fallbacks, Vec::new());
    }

    #[test]
    fn update_with_full_identity_matches_the_spec_example() {
        let relation = two_columns();
        let mut builder = open();
        for id in ["1", "2", "3"] {
            builder.insert(&relation, &Insert { relation_oid: 16_384, new: Tuple(vec![t(id), t("x")]) }).unwrap();
        }
        let update = Update {
            relation_oid: 16_384,
            old: Some(OldTuple::Full(Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("pending")]))),
            new: Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("paid")]),
        };
        let built = builder.update(&relation, &update).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            encoded(&built),
            r#"{"id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000003","version":1,"op":"update","schema":"public","table":"orders","key":{"id":42},"before":{"id":42,"status":"pending"},"after":{"id":42,"status":"paid"},"source":{"pipeline":"orders","commit_lsn":"0/1A2B3C4D","commit_ts":"2026-10-14T12:00:00.123456+00:00"}}"#
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn update_without_old_tuple_has_null_before() {
        let update = Update { relation_oid: 16_384, old: None, new: Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("paid")]) };
        let envelope = open().update(&two_columns(), &update).unwrap().envelope;
        assert_eq!(envelope.before, None);
        assert_eq!(envelope.after, Some(row(json!({"id": 42, "status": "paid"}))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn update_with_key_tuple_keeps_only_identity_columns_in_before() {
        let update = Update {
            relation_oid: 16_384,
            old: Some(OldTuple::Key(Tuple(vec![t("41"), TupleValue::Null]))),
            new: Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("paid")]),
        };
        let envelope = open().update(&two_columns(), &update).unwrap().envelope;
        assert_eq!(envelope.before, Some(row(json!({"id": 41}))));
        assert_eq!(envelope.key, Some(row(json!({"id": 42}))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn unchanged_toast_is_filled_from_a_full_old_tuple() {
        let update = Update {
            relation_oid: 16_384,
            old: Some(OldTuple::Full(Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("pending"), t("long text")]))),
            new: Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("paid"), TupleValue::UnchangedToast]),
        };
        let envelope = open().update(&three_columns(), &update).unwrap().envelope;
        assert_eq!(envelope.after, Some(row(json!({"id": 42, "status": "paid", "note": "long text"}))));
        assert_eq!(envelope.unchanged_toast, Vec::<String>::new());
        let keys: Vec<&String> = envelope.after.as_ref().unwrap().keys().collect();
        assert_eq!(keys, ["id", "status", "note"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unchanged_toast_without_old_tuple_is_listed_and_omitted() {
        let update = Update {
            relation_oid: 16_384,
            old: None,
            new: Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("paid"), TupleValue::UnchangedToast]),
        };
        let built = open().update(&three_columns(), &update).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(built.envelope.after, Some(row(json!({"id": 42, "status": "paid"}))));
        assert_eq!(built.envelope.unchanged_toast, vec!["note".to_owned()]);
        assert!(encoded(&built).contains(r#""after":{"id":42,"status":"paid"},"unchanged_toast":["note"],"source":"#));
    }

    #[test]
    fn deletes_carry_the_old_image_and_no_after() {
        let key_delete = Delete { relation_oid: 16_384, old: OldTuple::Key(Tuple(vec![t("42"), TupleValue::Null])) };
        let envelope = open().delete(&two_columns(), &key_delete).unwrap().envelope;
        assert_eq!(envelope.op, Op::Delete);
        assert_eq!(envelope.before, Some(row(json!({"id": 42}))));
        assert_eq!(envelope.after, None);
        assert_eq!(envelope.key, Some(row(json!({"id": 42}))));

        let full_delete = Delete { relation_oid: 16_384, old: OldTuple::Full(Tuple(vec![t("42"), t("paid")])) };
        let envelope = open().delete(&two_columns(), &full_delete).unwrap().envelope;
        assert_eq!(envelope.before, Some(row(json!({"id": 42, "status": "paid"}))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn truncate_events_have_null_images_and_one_ordinal_each() {
        let mut builder = open();
        let mut other = two_columns();
        other.oid = 16_390;
        other.table = "items".to_owned();
        let first = builder.truncate(&two_columns()).unwrap();
        let second = builder.truncate(&other).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(first.envelope.id.ordinal(), 0);
        assert_eq!(second.envelope.id.ordinal(), 1);
        assert_eq!(second.envelope.table, "items");
        for built in [&first, &second] {
            assert_eq!(built.envelope.op, Op::Truncate);
            assert!(encoded(built).contains(r#""key":null,"before":null,"after":null"#));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn relation_without_key_has_null_key() {
        let mut relation = two_columns();
        relation.key_columns.clear();
        let insert = Insert { relation_oid: 16_384, new: Tuple(vec![t("1"), t("a")]) };
        assert_eq!(open().insert(&relation, &insert).unwrap().envelope.key, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn key_is_null_when_a_key_value_is_absent() {
        let mut relation = two_columns();
        relation.key_columns = vec!["id".to_owned(), "status".to_owned()];
        let update = Update { relation_oid: 16_384, old: None, new: Tuple(vec![t("1"), TupleValue::UnchangedToast]) };
        assert_eq!(open().update(&relation, &update).unwrap().envelope.key, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn o1_event_ids_are_deterministic_and_strictly_increasing() {
        let relation = two_columns();
        let run = || {
            let mut builder = Builder::new("orders");
            let mut ids = Vec::new();
            for (lsn, rows) in [(Lsn(0x100), 3), (Lsn(0x200), 2)] {
                builder.begin(lsn, COMMIT_TS).unwrap();
                for row in 0..rows {
                    let insert = Insert { relation_oid: 16_384, new: Tuple(vec![t(&row.to_string()), t("x")]) };
                    ids.push(builder.insert(&relation, &insert).unwrap().envelope.id);
                }
                builder.commit().unwrap();
            }
            ids
        };
        let first = run();
        assert_eq!(first, run());
        assert!(first.windows(2).all(|pair| pair[0] < pair[1] && pair[0].to_string() < pair[1].to_string()));
        assert_eq!(first[3], EventId::new(Lsn(0x200), 0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn d3_tuple_with_wrong_column_count_is_a_fault() {
        let insert = Insert { relation_oid: 16_384, new: Tuple(vec![t("1")]) };
        assert_eq!(
            open().insert(&two_columns(), &insert),
            Err(BuildError::TupleShape { relation: 16_384, expected: 2, actual: 1 })
        );
        let update = Update {
            relation_oid: 16_384,
            old: Some(OldTuple::Full(Tuple(vec![t("1")]))),
            new: Tuple(vec![t("1"), t("a")]),
        };
        assert!(matches!(open().update(&two_columns(), &update), Err(BuildError::TupleShape { .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn row_changes_need_an_open_transaction() {
        let mut builder = Builder::new("orders");
        let insert = Insert { relation_oid: 16_384, new: Tuple(vec![t("1"), t("a")]) };
        assert_eq!(builder.insert(&two_columns(), &insert), Err(BuildError::NoOpenTransaction));
        assert_eq!(builder.commit(), Err(BuildError::NoOpenTransaction));
        builder.begin(COMMIT_LSN, COMMIT_TS).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(builder.begin(COMMIT_LSN, COMMIT_TS), Err(BuildError::TransactionAlreadyOpen));
    }

    #[test]
    fn value_fallbacks_are_reported() {
        let insert = Insert { relation_oid: 16_384, new: Tuple(vec![t("abc"), t("a")]) };
        let built = open().insert(&two_columns(), &insert).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(built.fallbacks, vec![Fallback { column: "id".to_owned(), type_oid: 23 }]);
        assert_eq!(built.envelope.after, Some(row(json!({"id": "abc", "status": "a"}))));
    }

    fn row(value: serde_json::Value) -> Row {
        match value {
            serde_json::Value::Object(map) => map,
            other => panic!("not an object: {other}"),
        }
    }
}
```

`Built` and `BuildError` derive `PartialEq` so these assertions compile.

`crates/pg-cdc/src/event/id.rs` (above its tests):

```rust
use std::fmt;

use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use serde::{Serialize, Serializer};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub struct EventId {
    commit_lsn: Lsn,
    ordinal: u32,
}

impl EventId {
    pub fn new(commit_lsn: Lsn, ordinal: u32) -> Self {
        Self { commit_lsn, ordinal }
    }

    pub fn commit_lsn(self) -> Lsn {
        self.commit_lsn
    }

    pub fn ordinal(self) -> u32 {
        self.ordinal
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for EventId {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:016x}-{:08x}", self.commit_lsn.0, self.ordinal)
    }
}

impl Serialize for EventId {
    fn serialize<S: Serializer>(&self, serializer: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
        serializer.collect_str(self)
    }
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/event/envelope.rs`:

```rust
use serde::Serialize;
use serde_json::{Map, Value};

use super::EventId;

pub const ENVELOPE_VERSION: u8 = 1;

pub type Row = Map<String, Value>;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum Op {
    Insert,
    Update,
    Delete,
    Truncate,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct Source {
    pub pipeline: String,
    pub commit_lsn: String,
    pub commit_ts: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct Envelope {
    pub id: EventId,
    pub version: u8,
    pub op: Op,
    pub schema: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub key: Option<Row>,
    pub before: Option<Row>,
    pub after: Option<Row>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    pub unchanged_toast: Vec<String>,
    pub source: Source,
}

impl Envelope {
    pub fn encode(&self) -> Result<Vec<u8>, serde_json::Error> {
        serde_json::to_vec(self)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the builder**

`crates/pg-cdc/src/event/builder.rs` (above its tests):

```rust
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_decoder::message::{Delete, Insert, OldTuple, Tuple, TupleValue, Update};
use pg_cdc_decoder::value::{TypeKind, format_commit_ts, map_value};
use serde_json::Value;

use super::{ENVELOPE_VERSION, Envelope, EventId, Op, Row, Source};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ColumnInfo {
    pub name: String,
    pub type_oid: u32,
    pub kind: TypeKind,
    pub is_identity: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RelationInfo {
    pub oid: u32,
    pub schema: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub columns: Vec<ColumnInfo>,
    pub key_columns: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Fallback {
    pub column: String,
    pub type_oid: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Built {
    pub envelope: Envelope,
    pub fallbacks: Vec<Fallback>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum BuildError {
    #[error("row change outside a transaction")]
    NoOpenTransaction,
    #[error("a transaction is already open")]
    TransactionAlreadyOpen,
    #[error("relation {relation} has {expected} columns but the tuple has {actual}")]
    TupleShape { relation: u32, expected: usize, actual: usize },
    #[error("the transaction has more than 2^32 events")]
    OrdinalOverflow,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Builder {
    pipeline: String,
    txn: Option<Txn>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct Txn {
    commit_lsn: Lsn,
    commit_ts: String,
    next_ordinal: u64,
}

struct Change {
    op: Op,
    key: Option<Row>,
    before: Option<Row>,
    after: Option<Row>,
    unchanged_toast: Vec<String>,
}

impl Builder {
    pub fn new(pipeline: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self { pipeline: pipeline.into(), txn: None }
    }

    pub fn begin(&mut self, commit_lsn: Lsn, commit_ts_micros: i64) -> Result<(), BuildError> {
        if self.txn.is_some() {
            return Err(BuildError::TransactionAlreadyOpen);
        }
        self.txn = Some(Txn { commit_lsn, commit_ts: format_commit_ts(commit_ts_micros), next_ordinal: 0 });
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn commit(&mut self) -> Result<(), BuildError> {
        self.txn.take().map(|_| ()).ok_or(BuildError::NoOpenTransaction)
    }

    pub fn insert(&mut self, relation: &RelationInfo, insert: &Insert) -> Result<Built, BuildError> {
        check_shape(relation, &insert.new)?;
        let mut fallbacks = Vec::new();
        let (after, unchanged_toast) = map_row(relation, &insert.new.0, |_| true, &mut fallbacks);
        let change = Change { op: Op::Insert, key: key(relation, &after), before: None, after: Some(after), unchanged_toast };
        self.emit(relation, change, fallbacks)
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, relation: &RelationInfo, update: &Update) -> Result<Built, BuildError> {
        check_shape(relation, &update.new)?;
        let mut fallbacks = Vec::new();
        let before = match &update.old {
            Some(old) => Some(old_image(relation, old, &mut fallbacks)?),
            None => None,
        };
        let new_values: Vec<TupleValue> = match &update.old {
            Some(OldTuple::Full(old)) => update
                .new
                .0
                .iter()
                .zip(&old.0)
                .map(|(new, old)| match (new, old) {
                    (TupleValue::UnchangedToast, TupleValue::Text(_) | TupleValue::Null) => old.clone(),
                    _ => new.clone(),
                })
                .collect(),
            _ => update.new.0.clone(),
        };
        let (after, unchanged_toast) = map_row(relation, &new_values, |_| true, &mut fallbacks);
        let change = Change { op: Op::Update, key: key(relation, &after), before, after: Some(after), unchanged_toast };
        self.emit(relation, change, fallbacks)
    }

    pub fn delete(&mut self, relation: &RelationInfo, delete: &Delete) -> Result<Built, BuildError> {
        let mut fallbacks = Vec::new();
        let before = old_image(relation, &delete.old, &mut fallbacks)?;
        let change = Change {
            op: Op::Delete,
            key: key(relation, &before),
            before: Some(before),
            after: None,
            unchanged_toast: Vec::new(),
        };
        self.emit(relation, change, fallbacks)
    }

    pub fn truncate(&mut self, relation: &RelationInfo) -> Result<Built, BuildError> {
        let change = Change { op: Op::Truncate, key: None, before: None, after: None, unchanged_toast: Vec::new() };
        self.emit(relation, change, Vec::new())
    }

    fn emit(&mut self, relation: &RelationInfo, change: Change, fallbacks: Vec<Fallback>) -> Result<Built, BuildError> {
        let txn = self.txn.as_mut().ok_or(BuildError::NoOpenTransaction)?;
        let ordinal = u32::try_from(txn.next_ordinal).map_err(|_| BuildError::OrdinalOverflow)?;
        txn.next_ordinal += 1;
        let envelope = Envelope {
            id: EventId::new(txn.commit_lsn, ordinal),
            version: ENVELOPE_VERSION,
            op: change.op,
            schema: relation.schema.clone(),
            table: relation.table.clone(),
            key: change.key,
            before: change.before,
            after: change.after,
            unchanged_toast: change.unchanged_toast,
            source: Source {
                pipeline: self.pipeline.clone(),
                commit_lsn: txn.commit_lsn.to_string(),
                commit_ts: txn.commit_ts.clone(),
            },
        };
        Ok(Built { envelope, fallbacks })
    }
}

fn check_shape(relation: &RelationInfo, tuple: &Tuple) -> Result<(), BuildError> {
    if tuple.0.len() == relation.columns.len() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(BuildError::TupleShape { relation: relation.oid, expected: relation.columns.len(), actual: tuple.0.len() })
    }
}

fn old_image(relation: &RelationInfo, old: &OldTuple, fallbacks: &mut Vec<Fallback>) -> Result<Row, BuildError> {
    let (tuple, key_only) = match old {
        OldTuple::Key(tuple) => (tuple, true),
        OldTuple::Full(tuple) => (tuple, false),
    };
    check_shape(relation, tuple)?;
    let (row, _) = map_row(relation, &tuple.0, |column| !key_only || column.is_identity, fallbacks);
    Ok(row)
}

fn map_row(
    relation: &RelationInfo,
    values: &[TupleValue],
    keep: impl Fn(&ColumnInfo) -> bool,
    fallbacks: &mut Vec<Fallback>,
) -> (Row, Vec<String>) {
    let mut row = Row::new();
    let mut unchanged = Vec::new();
    for (column, value) in relation.columns.iter().zip(values) {
        if !keep(column) {
            continue;
        }
        match value {
            TupleValue::Null => {
                row.insert(column.name.clone(), Value::Null);
            }
            TupleValue::UnchangedToast => unchanged.push(column.name.clone()),
            TupleValue::Text(text) => {
                let mapped = map_value(&column.kind, text);
                if mapped.fallback {
                    fallbacks.push(Fallback { column: column.name.clone(), type_oid: column.type_oid });
                }
                row.insert(column.name.clone(), mapped.value);
            }
        }
    }
    (row, unchanged)
}

fn key(relation: &RelationInfo, image: &Row) -> Option<Row> {
    if relation.key_columns.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    relation
        .key_columns
        .iter()
        .map(|name| image.get(name).map(|value| (name.clone(), value.clone())))
        .collect()
}
```

A K tuple from Postgres carries every column with non-identity ones as null, so `old_image` keeps only the identity columns for it (spec §6.5). The key comes from `after`, or from `before` for deletes (spec §7.3), and is `None` when any key value is absent (Ruling R14).

### Task 9: Catalog lookups and to_jsonb reference tests
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 2, Task 4, Task 5, Task 6
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** normal

Implements `pg_cdc::catalog` over the control connection (spec §6.3, §6.5, §9.2): type resolution for non-built-in types (enums, domains, arrays, composites, ranges and custom types), primary-key and replica-identity-index columns by relation OID, the slot's `confirmed_flush_lsn`, and the published tables without `REPLICA IDENTITY FULL`. It also adds the `to_jsonb` reference test over every built-in type and arrays of them, which uses Postgres itself as the oracle (spec §6.4, §18.2, D2).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/catalog.rs` (empty from Task 1), `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`
- Test: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/catalog.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/to_jsonb_reference.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc_decoder::{Lsn, value::{TypeKind, builtin_kind, map_value}, message::{decode, LogicalMessage, TupleValue}}` (Tasks 4, 5); `pg_cdc_testkit::Postgres` (tests); the `thiserror` and `serde_json` entries Task 6 adds to `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`.
- Produces:
  - `pub struct TypeResolver` (`Default`) with `new() -> Self` and `async fn resolve(&mut self, client: &tokio_postgres::Client, oid: u32) -> Result<TypeKind, CatalogError>`: built-in kinds without a query, then a per-process cache, then `pg_type`; domains resolve to their base type, arrays (`typcategory = 'A'`, `typelem <> 0`) to `TypeKind::Array` with the element type's `typdelim`, everything else (enum, composite, range, multirange, base and unknown OIDs) to `TypeKind::Text`; resolution depth is capped at 16
  - `pub async fn key_columns(client: &Client, relation_oid: u32) -> Result<Vec<String>, CatalogError>`: primary key columns in index order, else the replica identity index columns, else empty
  - `pub async fn slot_confirmed_flush(client: &Client, slot: &str) -> Result<Option<Lsn>, CatalogError>`: `None` when the slot does not exist
  - `pub async fn tables_without_full_identity(client: &Client, publication: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, CatalogError>`: `(schema, table)` sorted
  - `pub enum CatalogError { Query(tokio_postgres::Error), InvalidLsn { slot: String, value: String } }`

```rust
use pg_cdc::catalog::{TypeResolver, key_columns, slot_confirmed_flush, tables_without_full_identity};
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_decoder::value::TypeKind;
use pg_cdc_testkit::Postgres;
use tokio_postgres::Client;

type TestResult = Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

async fn oid_of(client: &Client, expression: &str) -> Result<u32, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> {
    Ok(client.query_one(&format!("SELECT ({expression})::oid"), &[]).await?.try_get(0)?)
}

fn array(element: TypeKind, delimiter: char) -> TypeKind {
    TypeKind::Array { element: Box::new(element), delimiter }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn resolves_builtin_and_user_defined_types() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute(
        "CREATE TYPE mood AS ENUM ('happy', 'sad');
         CREATE DOMAIN posint AS int CHECK (VALUE > 0);
         CREATE DOMAIN posints AS int[];
         CREATE TYPE pair AS (a int, b text);",
    )
    .await?;
    let client = pg.client().await?;
    let mut resolver = TypeResolver::new();
    for (expression, expected) in [
        ("'mood'::regtype", TypeKind::Text),
        ("'mood[]'::regtype", array(TypeKind::Text, ',')),
        ("'posint'::regtype", TypeKind::Integer),
        ("'posint[]'::regtype", array(TypeKind::Integer, ',')),
        ("'posints'::regtype", array(TypeKind::Integer, ',')),
        ("'pair'::regtype", TypeKind::Text),
        ("'pair[]'::regtype", array(TypeKind::Text, ',')),
        ("'int4range'::regtype", TypeKind::Text),
        ("'box[]'::regtype", array(TypeKind::Text, ';')),
        ("'uuid[]'::regtype", array(TypeKind::Text, ',')),
        ("'timestamptz'::regtype", TypeKind::Timestamptz),
        ("'jsonb[]'::regtype", array(TypeKind::Json, ',')),
    ] {
        let oid = oid_of(&client, expression).await?;
        assert_eq!(resolver.resolve(&client, oid).await?, expected, "{expression}");
        assert_eq!(resolver.resolve(&client, oid).await?, expected, "{expression} from cache");
    }
    assert_eq!(resolver.resolve(&client, 4_000_000_000).await?, TypeKind::Text);
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn key_columns_follow_the_primary_key_then_the_replica_identity_index() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE pk (b int, a int, c text, PRIMARY KEY (b, a));
         CREATE TABLE ri (a int, c text NOT NULL);
         CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ri_c ON ri (c);
         ALTER TABLE ri REPLICA IDENTITY USING INDEX ri_c;
         CREATE TABLE nokey (a int);",
    )
    .await?;
    let client = pg.client().await?;
    assert_eq!(key_columns(&client, oid_of(&client, "'pk'::regclass").await?).await?, ["b", "a"]);
    assert_eq!(key_columns(&client, oid_of(&client, "'ri'::regclass").await?).await?, ["c"]);
    assert_eq!(key_columns(&client, oid_of(&client, "'nokey'::regclass").await?).await?, Vec::<String>::new());
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn key_columns_handle_quoted_identifiers() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute(
        r#"CREATE SCHEMA "Weird Schema";
           CREATE TABLE "Weird Schema"."Order Items" ("Order ID" int PRIMARY KEY, "naïve" text);"#,
    )
    .await?;
    let client = pg.client().await?;
    let oid = oid_of(&client, r#"'"Weird Schema"."Order Items"'::regclass"#).await?;
    assert_eq!(key_columns(&client, oid).await?, ["Order ID"]);
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn reads_slot_position_and_identity_warnings() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE full_t (id int PRIMARY KEY);
         ALTER TABLE full_t REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
         CREATE TABLE default_t (id int PRIMARY KEY);
         CREATE TABLE nothing_t (id int);
         ALTER TABLE nothing_t REPLICA IDENTITY NOTHING;",
    )
    .await?;
    pg.create_publication("cat_pub", &["full_t", "default_t", "nothing_t"]).await?;
    let created = pg.create_slot("cat_slot").await?;
    let client = pg.client().await?;
    assert_eq!(slot_confirmed_flush(&client, "cat_slot").await?, Some(Lsn(created)));
    assert_eq!(slot_confirmed_flush(&client, "missing_slot").await?, None);
    assert_eq!(
        tables_without_full_identity(&client, "cat_pub").await?,
        vec![("public".to_owned(), "default_t".to_owned()), ("public".to_owned(), "nothing_t".to_owned())]
    );
    Ok(())
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the catalog module**

`crates/pg-cdc/src/catalog.rs`:

```rust
use std::collections::HashMap;

use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_decoder::value::{TypeKind, builtin_kind};
use tokio_postgres::Client;

const MAX_TYPE_DEPTH: usize = 16;

const TYPE_SQL: &str = "SELECT t.typtype, t.typcategory, t.typelem, t.typbasetype, coalesce(e.typdelim, t.typdelim) \
     FROM pg_type t LEFT JOIN pg_type e ON e.oid = t.typelem WHERE t.oid = $1";

const KEY_SQL: &str = "SELECT a.attname::text FROM pg_index i \
     CROSS JOIN LATERAL unnest(i.indkey) WITH ORDINALITY AS k(attnum, ord) \
     JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = i.indrelid AND a.attnum = k.attnum \
     WHERE i.indexrelid = (SELECT x.indexrelid FROM pg_index x \
     WHERE x.indrelid = $1 AND (x.indisprimary OR x.indisreplident) \
     ORDER BY x.indisprimary DESC LIMIT 1) \
     ORDER BY k.ord";

const SLOT_SQL: &str = "SELECT confirmed_flush_lsn::text FROM pg_replication_slots WHERE slot_name = $1";

const IDENTITY_SQL: &str = "SELECT pt.schemaname::text, pt.tablename::text FROM pg_publication_tables pt \
     JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.nspname = pt.schemaname \
     JOIN pg_class c ON c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relname = pt.tablename \
     WHERE pt.pubname = $1 AND c.relreplident <> 'f' ORDER BY 1, 2";

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum CatalogError {
    #[error("catalog query failed: {0}")]
    Query(#[from] tokio_postgres::Error),
    #[error("slot {slot} reports an invalid confirmed_flush_lsn {value:?}")]
    InvalidLsn { slot: String, value: String },
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct TypeResolver {
    cache: HashMap<u32, TypeKind>,
}

impl TypeResolver {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub async fn resolve(&mut self, client: &Client, oid: u32) -> Result<TypeKind, CatalogError> {
        if let Some(kind) = builtin_kind(oid) {
            return Ok(kind);
        }
        if let Some(kind) = self.cache.get(&oid) {
            return Ok(kind.clone());
        }
        let kind = lookup(client, oid).await?;
        self.cache.insert(oid, kind.clone());
        Ok(kind)
    }
}

async fn lookup(client: &Client, oid: u32) -> Result<TypeKind, CatalogError> {
    let mut delimiters = Vec::new();
    let mut current = oid;
    for _ in 0..MAX_TYPE_DEPTH {
        if let Some(kind) = builtin_kind(current) {
            return Ok(wrap(kind, &delimiters));
        }
        let Some(row) = client.query_opt(TYPE_SQL, &[&current]).await? else {
            return Ok(wrap(TypeKind::Text, &delimiters));
        };
        let typtype: i8 = row.try_get(0)?;
        let category: i8 = row.try_get(1)?;
        let element: u32 = row.try_get(2)?;
        let base: u32 = row.try_get(3)?;
        let delimiter: i8 = row.try_get(4)?;
        match (typtype.cast_unsigned(), category.cast_unsigned()) {
            (b'd', _) => current = base,
            (_, b'A') if element != 0 => {
                delimiters.push(char::from(delimiter.cast_unsigned()));
                current = element;
            }
            _ => return Ok(wrap(TypeKind::Text, &delimiters)),
        }
    }
    Ok(TypeKind::Text)
}

fn wrap(kind: TypeKind, delimiters: &[char]) -> TypeKind {
    delimiters
        .iter()
        .rev()
        .fold(kind, |element, &delimiter| TypeKind::Array { element: Box::new(element), delimiter })
}

pub async fn key_columns(client: &Client, relation_oid: u32) -> Result<Vec<String>, CatalogError> {
    let rows = client.query(KEY_SQL, &[&relation_oid]).await?;
    rows.iter().map(|row| row.try_get(0).map_err(CatalogError::from)).collect()
}

pub async fn slot_confirmed_flush(client: &Client, slot: &str) -> Result<Option<Lsn>, CatalogError> {
    let Some(row) = client.query_opt(SLOT_SQL, &[&slot]).await? else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let Some(text) = row.try_get::<_, Option<String>>(0)? else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    text.parse()
        .map(Some)
        .map_err(|_| CatalogError::InvalidLsn { slot: slot.to_owned(), value: text })
}

pub async fn tables_without_full_identity(
    client: &Client,
    publication: &str,
) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, CatalogError> {
    let rows = client.query(IDENTITY_SQL, &[&publication]).await?;
    rows.iter()
        .map(|row| Ok((row.try_get(0)?, row.try_get(1)?)))
        .collect()
}
```

Catalog queries take OIDs and bind every value as a parameter, so quoted or non-ASCII identifiers need no escaping. `KEY_SQL` picks the primary key index when there is one and the replica identity index otherwise, in one round trip. `"char"` columns such as `typtype` decode as `i8` in tokio-postgres.

Run: `cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test catalog`
Expected: 4 tests pass.

`crates/pg-cdc/tests/to_jsonb_reference.rs`:

```rust
use pg_cdc::catalog::TypeResolver;
use pg_cdc_decoder::message::{LogicalMessage, TupleValue, decode};
use pg_cdc_decoder::value::map_value;
use pg_cdc_testkit::Postgres;
use serde_json::Value;

type TestResult = Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

const TO_JSONB: &str = "to_jsonb({c})";
const AS_TEXT: &str = "to_jsonb({c}::text)";
const AS_TEXT_ARRAY: &str = "to_jsonb({c}::text[])";
const AS_INT8: &str = "to_jsonb({c}::int8)";

struct Case {
    sql_type: &'static str,
    expected: &'static str,
    literals: &'static [&'static str],
}

const fn case(sql_type: &'static str, literals: &'static [&'static str]) -> Case {
    Case { sql_type, expected: TO_JSONB, literals }
}

const CASES: &[Case] = &[
    case("bool", &["true", "false"]),
    case("int2", &["-32768", "32767"]),
    case("int4", &["-2147483648", "0"]),
    case("int8", &["-9223372036854775808", "9223372036854775807"]),
    Case { sql_type: "oid", expected: AS_INT8, literals: &["0", "4294967295"] },
    case("float4", &["'NaN'", "'Infinity'", "'-Infinity'", "'0.1'", "'-0'", "'3.4028235e38'", "'1.5e-7'"]),
    case("float8", &["'NaN'", "'-Infinity'", "'1e100'", "'5e-324'", "'0.1'", "'-0'", "'123456789012345680000'"]),
    case("numeric", &["'NaN'", "'Infinity'", "'-Infinity'", "'1.50'", "'-12.3456'", "'0.000000000001'", "'1e30'"]),
    case("text", &["''", r#"'quote " and backslash \'"#, "'emoji 🐘'", r"E'tab\tnewline\n'"]),
    case("varchar(10)", &["'abc'"]),
    case("bpchar(5)", &["'ab'"]),
    case("name", &["'pg_catalog'"]),
    case("\"char\"", &["'a'"]),
    case("json", &[r#"'{"b": 1, "a": 1e2, "a": 3}'"#, r#"'[1.0, -0, "x"]'"#, "'null'"]),
    case("jsonb", &[r#"'{"x": 1.0e2, "nested": {"k": [true, null]}}'"#]),
    case("date", &["'2026-10-14'", "'infinity'", "'0044-03-15 BC'", "'10000-01-01'"]),
    case("timestamp", &["'2026-10-14 12:00:00.5'", "'-infinity'", "'0044-03-15 12:00 BC'"]),
    case(
        "timestamptz",
        &["'2026-10-14 12:00:00.123456+00'", "'infinity'", "'2026-10-14 08:00:00-04'", "'0044-03-15 12:00+00 BC'"],
    ),
    case("time", &["'12:34:56.789'"]),
    case("timetz", &["'12:34:56+05:30'"]),
    case("interval", &["'1 day 02:03:04'", "'-1 year'"]),
    case("bytea", &[r"'\x0102ff'", "''"]),
    case("uuid", &["'a0eebc99-9c0b-4ef8-bb6d-6bb9bd380a11'"]),
    case("inet", &["'192.168.0.1/24'"]),
    case("cidr", &["'10.0.0.0/8'"]),
    case("macaddr", &["'08:00:2b:01:02:03'"]),
    case("macaddr8", &["'08:00:2b:01:02:03:04:05'"]),
    case("money", &["'12.34'"]),
    case("bit(4)", &["B'1010'"]),
    case("varbit", &["B'101'"]),
    case("xml", &["'<a>1</a>'"]),
    case("tsvector", &["'a fat cat'"]),
    case("tsquery", &["'fat & cat'"]),
    case("point", &["'(1,2)'"]),
    case("line", &["'{1,2,3}'"]),
    case("lseg", &["'[(0,0),(1,1)]'"]),
    case("box", &["'(1,1),(0,0)'"]),
    case("path", &["'[(0,0),(1,1)]'"]),
    case("polygon", &["'((0,0),(1,1),(1,0))'"]),
    case("circle", &["'<(0,0),1>'"]),
    case("int4range", &["'[1,5)'", "'empty'"]),
    case("numrange", &["'(1.5,2.5]'"]),
    case("tstzrange", &["'[2026-10-14 00:00+00,)'"]),
    case("daterange", &["'[2026-01-01,2026-02-01)'"]),
    case("int4multirange", &["'{[1,3),[5,7)}'"]),
    case("pg_lsn", &["'0/1A2B3C4D'"]),
    case("jsonpath", &["'$.a[*] ? (@ > 1)'"]),
    case("int4[]", &["'{1,NULL,3}'", "'[0:1]={1,2}'", "'{}'", "'{{1,2},{3,4}}'"]),
    case("text[]", &[r#"'{a,"b c","NULL",NULL,"q\"uote","back\\slash",""}'"#]),
    case("bool[]", &["'{t,f}'"]),
    case("float8[]", &["'{NaN,1.5,1e100}'"]),
    case("numeric[]", &["'{1.50,NaN}'"]),
    case("bytea[]", &[r#"'{"\\x01",""}'"#]),
    case("date[]", &["'{2026-10-14,infinity}'"]),
    case("timestamptz[]", &[r#"'{"2026-10-14 12:00:00+00"}'"#]),
    case("jsonb[]", &[r#"'{"{\"a\": 1}"}'"#]),
    case("uuid[]", &["'{a0eebc99-9c0b-4ef8-bb6d-6bb9bd380a11}'"]),
    case("box[]", &["'{(1,1),(0,0);(2,2),(1,1)}'"]),
    case("mood", &["'happy'"]),
    case("mood[]", &["'{happy,sad}'"]),
    case("posint", &["5"]),
    case("posint[]", &["'{1,2}'"]),
    Case { sql_type: "pair", expected: AS_TEXT, literals: &["ROW(1, 'x y')"] },
    Case { sql_type: "pair[]", expected: AS_TEXT_ARRAY, literals: &["ARRAY[ROW(1, 'x y')::pair]"] },
];

const SETUP: &str = "CREATE TYPE mood AS ENUM ('happy', 'sad');
     CREATE DOMAIN posint AS int CHECK (VALUE > 0);
     CREATE TYPE pair AS (a int, b text);";

fn rows() -> usize {
    CASES.iter().map(|case| case.literals.len()).max().unwrap_or(0)
}

fn create_table() -> String {
    let columns: Vec<String> = CASES.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, case)| format!("c{i} {}", case.sql_type)).collect();
    format!("CREATE TABLE ref_values (id int PRIMARY KEY, {})", columns.join(", "))
}

fn inserts() -> String {
    let statements: Vec<String> = (0..rows())
        .map(|row| {
            let values: Vec<&str> =
                CASES.iter().map(|case| case.literals.get(row).copied().unwrap_or("NULL")).collect();
            format!("INSERT INTO ref_values VALUES ({row}, {});", values.join(", "))
        })
        .collect();
    statements.join("\n")
}

fn expected_query() -> String {
    let columns: Vec<String> = CASES
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .map(|(i, case)| format!("{}::text", case.expected.replace("{c}", &format!("c{i}"))))
        .collect();
    format!("SELECT {} FROM ref_values ORDER BY id", columns.join(", "))
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn d2_to_jsonb_reference_matches_every_builtin_type() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    pg.execute(SETUP).await?;
    pg.execute(&create_table()).await?;
    pg.create_publication("ref_pub", &["ref_values"]).await?;
    pg.create_slot("ref_slot").await?;
    pg.execute(&inserts()).await?;

    let client = pg.client().await?;
    let mut expected_rows = Vec::new();
    for row in client.query(&expected_query(), &[]).await? {
        let mut values = Vec::new();
        for index in 0..CASES.len() {
            let text: Option<String> = row.try_get(index)?;
            values.push(match text {
                Some(text) => serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&text)?,
                None => Value::Null,
            });
        }
        expected_rows.push(values);
    }

    let mut resolver = TypeResolver::new();
    let mut relation = None;
    let mut actual_rows = Vec::new();
    for bytes in pg.capture_pgoutput("ref_slot", "ref_pub").await? {
        match decode(&bytes)? {
            LogicalMessage::Relation(message) => relation = Some(message),
            LogicalMessage::Insert(insert) => {
                let relation = relation.as_ref().ok_or("insert before relation")?;
                let mut values = Vec::new();
                for (column, value) in relation.columns.iter().zip(insert.new.0).skip(1) {
                    values.push(match value {
                        TupleValue::Text(text) => {
                            let kind = resolver.resolve(&client, column.type_oid).await?;
                            let mapped = map_value(&kind, &text);
                            assert!(!mapped.fallback, "{} {text:?} fell back", column.name);
                            mapped.value
                        }
                        TupleValue::Null => Value::Null,
                        TupleValue::UnchangedToast => return Err("unexpected unchanged TOAST".into()),
                    });
                }
                actual_rows.push(values);
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    assert_eq!(actual_rows.len(), expected_rows.len());
    for (row, (actual, expected)) in actual_rows.iter().zip(&expected_rows).enumerate() {
        for (index, case) in CASES.iter().enumerate() {
            assert_eq!(actual[index], expected[index], "row {row}, {} ({})", case.sql_type, case.expected);
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

The capture runs in a session with the pinned settings (testkit), the same settings the expected-value query uses, so both sides render values identically. Composite values are compared against their text form (spec §6.4 v1 rule) and `oid` against a number (Ruling R4); every other column must equal `to_jsonb`.

Run: `cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test to_jsonb_reference` and `PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION=14 cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test to_jsonb_reference`
Expected: PASS on both versions. A mismatch names the row, type and expected expression; fix the mapping in `pg-cdc-decoder` (Task 5's module) rather than the expectation, unless the spec's §6.4 table says otherwise, and record that as a Ruling in the task report.

### Task 10: Stdout sink and acknowledgement progress
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 4
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** high

Implements `pg_cdc::sink` (the `stdout` sink of spec §11.1: JSON lines) and `pg_cdc::ack` (the milestone-1 acknowledgement rules of spec §9.1–§9.2, Ruling R9). Only a successful flush creates a `Delivered` receipt, and only a receipt settles events (A1). The confirmed LSN advances to a Commit's end LSN only when every event of that transaction is settled, never decreases (A2), and moves to a keepalive's `wal_end` only when no transaction is open (A3).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/sink.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/ack.rs` (empty from Task 1), `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`
- Test: unit tests in both files

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `pub struct Delivered` (no public constructor, not `Clone`) with `into_events(self) -> u64`, which consumes the receipt
  - `pub struct StdoutSink<W>` with `new(writer: W) -> Self`, `StdoutSink::<tokio::io::Stdout>::stdout() -> Self`, `async fn write(&mut self, body: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<()>` (appends `\n`, counts one pending event), `async fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<Delivered>` (flushes, returns a receipt for every event written since the last flush), `async fn reset_pending(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()>` (flushes buffered bytes and zeroes the pending count; called by Task 11 at the start of every session), `pending(&self) -> u64`; methods require `W: AsyncWrite + Unpin`
  - `pub struct AckProgress` with `new(start: Lsn)`, `confirmed(&self) -> Lsn`, `is_idle(&self) -> bool`, `begin(&mut self) -> Result<(), AckError>`, `sent(&mut self) -> Result<(), AckError>`, `settle(&mut self, receipt: Delivered) -> Result<(), AckError>`, `commit(&mut self, end_lsn: Lsn) -> Result<Lsn, AckError>`, `keepalive(&mut self, wal_end: Lsn) -> Lsn`
  - `pub enum AckError { AlreadyOpen, NotOpen, OverSettled { settled: u64, unsettled: u64 }, Unsettled { end_lsn: Lsn, unsettled: u64 } }`

Tests at the end of `crates/pg-cdc/src/sink.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::time::Duration;
    use tokio::io::AsyncReadExt;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn writes_json_lines_and_receipts_count_flushed_events() {
        let mut sink = StdoutSink::new(Vec::new());
        sink.write(br#"{"a":1}"#).await.unwrap();
        sink.write(br#"{"b":2}"#).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(sink.pending(), 2);
        let receipt = sink.flush().await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(receipt.into_events(), 2);
        assert_eq!(sink.pending(), 0);
        assert_eq!(sink.flush().await.unwrap().into_events(), 0);
        assert_eq!(sink.out.get_ref().as_slice(), b"{\"a\":1}\n{\"b\":2}\n");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn nothing_reaches_the_writer_before_flush() {
        let (writer, mut reader) = tokio::io::duplex(1024);
        let mut sink = StdoutSink::new(writer);
        sink.write(b"{}").await.unwrap();
        let mut buffer = [0u8; 8];
        let early = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_millis(100), reader.read(&mut buffer)).await;
        assert!(early.is_err(), "bytes reached the reader before flush");
        sink.flush().await.unwrap();
        let read = reader.read(&mut buffer).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(&buffer[..read], b"{}\n");
    }
}
```

Tests at the end of `crates/pg-cdc/src/ack.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::sink::StdoutSink;
    use proptest::prelude::*;

    async fn receipt(events: usize) -> Delivered {
        let mut sink = StdoutSink::new(Vec::new());
        for _ in 0..events {
            sink.write(b"{}").await.unwrap();
        }
        sink.flush().await.unwrap()
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a1_only_a_delivery_receipt_settles_events() {
        let mut ack = AckProgress::new(Lsn(100));
        ack.begin().unwrap();
        ack.sent().unwrap();
        ack.sent().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ack.commit(Lsn(200)), Err(AckError::Unsettled { end_lsn: Lsn(200), unsettled: 2 }));
        assert_eq!(ack.confirmed(), Lsn(100));
        ack.settle(receipt(2).await).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ack.commit(Lsn(200)), Ok(Lsn(200)));
        assert!(ack.is_idle());
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn settling_more_than_was_sent_is_an_error() {
        let mut ack = AckProgress::new(Lsn(0));
        ack.begin().unwrap();
        ack.sent().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ack.settle(receipt(2).await), Err(AckError::OverSettled { settled: 2, unsettled: 1 }));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a2_confirmed_lsn_never_decreases() {
        let mut ack = AckProgress::new(Lsn(500));
        ack.begin().unwrap();
        ack.settle(receipt(0).await).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ack.commit(Lsn(400)), Ok(Lsn(500)));
        assert_eq!(ack.keepalive(Lsn(450)), Lsn(500));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a3_keepalive_ignored_while_transaction_open() {
        let mut ack = AckProgress::new(Lsn(100));
        assert_eq!(ack.keepalive(Lsn(150)), Lsn(150));
        ack.begin().unwrap();
        assert!(!ack.is_idle());
        assert_eq!(ack.keepalive(Lsn(900)), Lsn(150));
        assert_eq!(ack.commit(Lsn(200)), Ok(Lsn(200)));
        assert_eq!(ack.keepalive(Lsn(900)), Lsn(900));
    }

    #[test]
    fn transaction_boundaries_must_pair() {
        let mut ack = AckProgress::new(Lsn(0));
        assert_eq!(ack.sent(), Err(AckError::NotOpen));
        assert_eq!(ack.commit(Lsn(1)), Err(AckError::NotOpen));
        ack.begin().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ack.begin(), Err(AckError::AlreadyOpen));
    }

    proptest! {
        #[test]
        fn a2_random_transactions_keep_the_confirmed_lsn_safe(
            transactions in proptest::collection::vec(
                (1u64..1000, proptest::collection::vec(any::<bool>(), 0..6), 0u64..5000),
                1..20,
            )
        ) {
            let runtime = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread().build().unwrap();
            runtime.block_on(async {
                let mut sink = StdoutSink::new(Vec::new());
                let mut ack = AckProgress::new(Lsn(1));
                let mut end = 1u64;
                for (gap, flush_after, keepalive) in transactions {
                    end += gap;
                    let before = ack.confirmed();
                    ack.begin().unwrap();
                    for flush_now in &flush_after {
                        sink.write(b"{}").await.unwrap();
                        ack.sent().unwrap();
                        if *flush_now {
                            ack.settle(sink.flush().await.unwrap()).unwrap();
                        }
                        assert_eq!(ack.keepalive(Lsn(end + keepalive)), before);
                    }
                    let unflushed = sink.pending();
                    if unflushed > 0 {
                        assert_eq!(ack.commit(Lsn(end)), Err(AckError::Unsettled { end_lsn: Lsn(end), unsettled: unflushed }));
                        assert_eq!(ack.confirmed(), before);
                    }
                    ack.settle(sink.flush().await.unwrap()).unwrap();
                    let confirmed = ack.commit(Lsn(end)).unwrap();
                    assert_eq!(confirmed, before.max(Lsn(end)));
                    let idle = ack.keepalive(Lsn(end.saturating_sub(keepalive)));
                    assert!(idle >= confirmed);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the sink and the progress tracker**

`crates/pg-cdc/src/sink.rs` (above its tests):

```rust
use tokio::io::{AsyncWrite, AsyncWriteExt, BufWriter, Stdout};

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Delivered {
    events: u64,
}

impl Delivered {
    pub fn into_events(self) -> u64 {
        self.events
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct StdoutSink<W> {
    out: BufWriter<W>,
    pending: u64,
}

impl StdoutSink<Stdout> {
    pub fn stdout() -> Self {
        Self::new(tokio::io::stdout())
    }
}

impl<W: AsyncWrite + Unpin> StdoutSink<W> {
    pub fn new(writer: W) -> Self {
        Self { out: BufWriter::new(writer), pending: 0 }
    }

    pub async fn write(&mut self, body: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        self.out.write_all(body).await?;
        self.out.write_all(b"\n").await?;
        self.pending += 1;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<Delivered> {
        self.out.flush().await?;
        Ok(Delivered { events: std::mem::take(&mut self.pending) })
    }

    pub async fn reset_pending(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        self.flush().await.map(drop)
    }

    pub fn pending(&self) -> u64 {
        self.pending
    }
}
```

`Delivered`'s only field is private and the only code that builds one is `flush`, which runs after the writer reports every byte written; this is the type-level guarantee of invariant A1.

`crates/pg-cdc/src/ack.rs` (above its tests):

```rust
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;

use crate::sink::Delivered;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum AckError {
    #[error("a transaction is already open")]
    AlreadyOpen,
    #[error("no transaction is open")]
    NotOpen,
    #[error("a receipt settles {settled} events but only {unsettled} are unsettled")]
    OverSettled { settled: u64, unsettled: u64 },
    #[error("commit at {end_lsn} with {unsettled} unsettled events")]
    Unsettled { end_lsn: Lsn, unsettled: u64 },
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct AckProgress {
    confirmed: Lsn,
    open: Option<u64>,
}

impl AckProgress {
    pub fn new(start: Lsn) -> Self {
        Self { confirmed: start, open: None }
    }

    pub fn confirmed(&self) -> Lsn {
        self.confirmed
    }

    pub fn is_idle(&self) -> bool {
        self.open.is_none()
    }

    pub fn begin(&mut self) -> Result<(), AckError> {
        if self.open.is_some() {
            return Err(AckError::AlreadyOpen);
        }
        self.open = Some(0);
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn sent(&mut self) -> Result<(), AckError> {
        let unsettled = self.open.as_mut().ok_or(AckError::NotOpen)?;
        *unsettled += 1;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn settle(&mut self, receipt: Delivered) -> Result<(), AckError> {
        let unsettled = self.open.as_mut().ok_or(AckError::NotOpen)?;
        let settled = receipt.into_events();
        *unsettled = unsettled
            .checked_sub(settled)
            .ok_or(AckError::OverSettled { settled, unsettled: *unsettled })?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn commit(&mut self, end_lsn: Lsn) -> Result<Lsn, AckError> {
        let unsettled = self.open.ok_or(AckError::NotOpen)?;
        if unsettled != 0 {
            return Err(AckError::Unsettled { end_lsn, unsettled });
        }
        self.open = None;
        self.confirmed = self.confirmed.max(end_lsn);
        Ok(self.confirmed)
    }

    pub fn keepalive(&mut self, wal_end: Lsn) -> Lsn {
        if self.open.is_none() {
            self.confirmed = self.confirmed.max(wal_end);
        }
        self.confirmed
    }
}
```

### Task 11: Streaming session
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 2, Task 3, Task 6, Task 7, Task 8, Task 9, Task 10
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Implements `pg_cdc::session`: one replication session end to end. It asks the provider for parameters, opens the control connection, reads the slot's `confirmed_flush_lsn` as the starting confirmed LSN (§9.2), warns for published tables without `REPLICA IDENTITY FULL` (§6.5), opens the replication connection with the pinned settings (§6.1), and resets the sink's pending count so the new session starts with nothing unsettled (R22), and then processes transport events in order: Begin opens a transaction, each XLogData is decoded (D3 faults carry their `wal_start`), Relation messages rebuild the per-connection relation cache with catalog types and keys (§6.3, D4), row changes become envelopes written to the sink, Commit flushes the sink and only then advances the acknowledged LSN (A1, A2), and keepalives advance it only when idle (A3). The transport's own timer sends the status updates (A4).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/session.rs` (empty from Task 1), `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`
- Test: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/main.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/support.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/session.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc_replication::{ReplicationClient, ReplicationConfig, ReplicationEvent, TlsConfig, Lsn, PgWireError}` (Task 3); `pg_cdc::config::TlsMode` (Task 6); `pg_cdc::connection::{ConnectionProvider, ConnectParams, ConnectError, TlsConfig, SESSION_OPTIONS, connect_control, ControlConnection}` (Task 7); `pg_cdc::event::{Builder, Built, BuildError, ColumnInfo, RelationInfo}` (Task 8); `pg_cdc::catalog::{TypeResolver, CatalogError, key_columns, slot_confirmed_flush, tables_without_full_identity}` (Task 9); `pg_cdc::sink::StdoutSink` including `reset_pending`, `pg_cdc::ack::{AckProgress, AckError}` (Task 10); `pg_cdc_decoder::{Lsn, message::{decode, DecodeError, LogicalMessage, Relation}}` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `pub struct SessionConfig { pub pipeline: String, pub slot: String, pub publication: String, pub status_interval: Duration }`
  - `pub async fn run_session<W: AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send>(config: &SessionConfig, provider: &dyn ConnectionProvider, types: &mut TypeResolver, sink: &mut StdoutSink<W>, shutdown: &CancellationToken) -> Result<(), SessionError>`: returns `Ok(())` only after `shutdown` is cancelled
  - `pub enum SessionError { Connect(ConnectError), Catalog(CatalogError), SlotMissing(String), Replication(PgWireError), StreamEnded, Decode { wal_start: Lsn, source: DecodeError }, Build { wal_start: Lsn, source: BuildError }, UnknownRelation { wal_start: Lsn, oid: u32 }, Unexpected { wal_start: Lsn, what: &'static str }, Ack(AckError), Sink(std::io::Error), Encode(serde_json::Error) }` with `fault_lsn(&self) -> Option<Lsn>` (`Some` for `Decode`, `Build`, `UnknownRelation`, `Unexpected`) and `is_fatal(&self) -> bool` (true for `Ack`, `Sink`, `Encode`)
  - Test harness in `tests/stream/support.rs` (used by Task 12): `TestResult<T = ()>`, `SLOT`, `PUBLICATION`, `PIPELINE`, `WAIT`, `prepare(&Postgres, statements: &[&str], tables: &[&str]) -> TestResult`, `params(&Postgres) -> ConnectParams`, `spawn_session(ConnectParams, status_interval: Duration, buffer: usize) -> Running`, `spawn_after_abandoned_writes(ConnectParams, status_interval: Duration, buffer: usize, abandoned: usize) -> Running`, `Running::{next_event(&mut self) -> TestResult<Value>, no_event_within(&mut self, Duration) -> TestResult<bool>, stop(self) -> TestResult}`, `commit_lsn(&Value) -> TestResult<u64>`

```rust
use std::time::Duration;

use pg_cdc::catalog::TypeResolver;
use pg_cdc::config::TlsMode;
use pg_cdc::connection::{ConnectParams, PasswordProvider, TlsConfig};
use pg_cdc::session::{SessionConfig, SessionError, run_session};
use pg_cdc::sink::StdoutSink;
use pg_cdc_testkit::Postgres;
use secrecy::SecretString;
use serde_json::Value;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, BufReader, DuplexStream, Lines};
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;

pub(crate) type TestResult<T = ()> = Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

pub(crate) const SLOT: &str = "it_slot";
pub(crate) const PUBLICATION: &str = "it_pub";
pub(crate) const PIPELINE: &str = "it";
pub(crate) const WAIT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

pub(crate) async fn prepare(pg: &Postgres, statements: &[&str], tables: &[&str]) -> TestResult {
    for statement in statements {
        pg.execute(statement).await?;
    }
    pg.create_publication(PUBLICATION, tables).await?;
    pg.create_slot(SLOT).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn params(pg: &Postgres) -> ConnectParams {
    ConnectParams {
        host: pg.host().to_owned(),
        port: pg.port(),
        database: pg.database().to_owned(),
        user: pg.user().to_owned(),
        password: Some(SecretString::from(pg.password().to_owned())),
        tls: TlsConfig { mode: TlsMode::Disable, ca_file: None },
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Running {
    shutdown: CancellationToken,
    task: JoinHandle<Result<(), SessionError>>,
    lines: Lines<BufReader<DuplexStream>>,
}

pub(crate) fn spawn_session(params: ConnectParams, status_interval: Duration, buffer: usize) -> Running {
    spawn_after_abandoned_writes(params, status_interval, buffer, 0)
}

pub(crate) fn spawn_after_abandoned_writes(
    params: ConnectParams,
    status_interval: Duration,
    buffer: usize,
    abandoned: usize,
) -> Running {
    let (writer, reader) = tokio::io::duplex(buffer);
    let shutdown = CancellationToken::new();
    let token = shutdown.clone();
    let config = SessionConfig {
        pipeline: PIPELINE.to_owned(),
        slot: SLOT.to_owned(),
        publication: PUBLICATION.to_owned(),
        status_interval,
    };
    let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
        let provider = PasswordProvider::new(params);
        let mut types = TypeResolver::new();
        let mut sink = StdoutSink::new(writer);
        for n in 0..abandoned {
            let line = format!("{{\"abandoned\":{n}}}");
            sink.write(line.as_bytes()).await.map_err(SessionError::Sink)?;
        }
        run_session(&config, &provider, &mut types, &mut sink, &token).await
    });
    Running { shutdown, task, lines: BufReader::new(reader).lines() }
}

impl Running {
    pub(crate) async fn next_event(&mut self) -> TestResult<Value> {
        let line = tokio::time::timeout(WAIT, self.lines.next_line()).await??.ok_or("session output closed")?;
        Ok(serde_json::from_str(&line)?)
    }

    pub(crate) async fn no_event_within(&mut self, window: Duration) -> TestResult<bool> {
        Ok(tokio::time::timeout(window, self.lines.next_line()).await.is_err())
    }

    pub(crate) async fn stop(self) -> TestResult {
        self.shutdown.cancel();
        self.task.await??;
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub(crate) fn commit_lsn(event: &Value) -> TestResult<u64> {
    let text = event.pointer("/source/commit_lsn").and_then(Value::as_str).ok_or("missing commit_lsn")?;
    Ok(pg_cdc_testkit::parse_lsn(text)?)
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/session.rs`:

```rust
use std::time::Duration;

use pg_cdc::config::TlsMode;
use pg_cdc::connection::TlsConfig;
use pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, PostgresOptions};
use secrecy::SecretString;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

use crate::support::{self, SLOT, TestResult, WAIT};

const FAST: Duration = Duration::from_millis(200);
const BUFFER: usize = 1 << 16;

#[tokio::test]
async fn streams_insert_update_delete_end_to_end() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY, status text)", "ALTER TABLE orders REPLICA IDENTITY FULL"],
        &["orders"],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'pending')").await?;
    pg.execute("UPDATE orders SET status = 'paid' WHERE id = 1").await?;
    pg.execute("DELETE FROM orders WHERE id = 1").await?;
    let insert = session.next_event().await?;
    let update = session.next_event().await?;
    let delete = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(insert["op"], "insert");
    assert_eq!(insert["key"], json!({"id": 1}));
    assert_eq!(insert["before"], Value::Null);
    assert_eq!(insert["after"], json!({"id": 1, "status": "pending"}));
    assert_eq!(update["op"], "update");
    assert_eq!(update["before"], json!({"id": 1, "status": "pending"}));
    assert_eq!(update["after"], json!({"id": 1, "status": "paid"}));
    assert_eq!(delete["op"], "delete");
    assert_eq!(delete["key"], json!({"id": 1}));
    assert_eq!(delete["before"], json!({"id": 1, "status": "paid"}));
    assert_eq!(delete["after"], Value::Null);
    let mut ids = Vec::new();
    for event in [&insert, &update, &delete] {
        assert_eq!(event["version"], 1);
        assert_eq!(event["schema"], "public");
        assert_eq!(event["table"], "orders");
        assert_eq!(event["source"]["pipeline"], support::PIPELINE);
        assert!(event.get("unchanged_toast").is_none());
        let id = event["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned();
        let commit = format!("{:016x}", support::commit_lsn(event)?);
        assert!(id.starts_with(&commit), "{id} does not start with the commit LSN {commit}");
        ids.push(id);
    }
    assert!(ids.windows(2).all(|pair| pair[0] < pair[1]), "{ids:?}");
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a2_slot_not_acknowledged_before_delivery() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(&pg, &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY, status text)"], &["orders"]).await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, 64);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'blocked')").await?;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
    let blocked = pg.slot_state(SLOT).await?.ok_or("slot missing")?.confirmed_flush_lsn.ok_or("no position")?;
    let event = session.next_event().await?;
    let commit = support::commit_lsn(&event)?;
    assert!(blocked <= commit, "slot acknowledged {blocked:X} past the undelivered commit at {commit:X}");
    pg.wait_for_slot(SLOT, WAIT, |state| state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn > commit)).await?;
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a3_idle_publication_advances_slot() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(&pg, &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY)", "CREATE TABLE noise (id int)"], &["orders"])
        .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO noise VALUES (1)").await?;
    let target = pg.current_wal_lsn().await?;
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO noise VALUES (2)").await?;
    pg.wait_for_slot(SLOT, WAIT, |state| state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn >= target)).await?;
    assert!(session.no_event_within(Duration::from_millis(500)).await?);
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn o1_replayed_transaction_keeps_its_ids() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(&pg, &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY)"], &["orders"]).await?;
    let mut first = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), Duration::from_secs(60), BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1), (2)").await?;
    let a = first.next_event().await?;
    let b = first.next_event().await?;
    let commit = support::commit_lsn(&a)?;
    first.stop().await?;
    let state = pg.wait_for_slot(SLOT, WAIT, |state| !state.active).await?;
    assert!(state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn < commit), "the first session acknowledged too early");
    let mut second = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    let replayed_a = second.next_event().await?;
    let replayed_b = second.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!((&replayed_a["id"], &replayed_b["id"]), (&a["id"], &b["id"]));
    assert_eq!(replayed_a, a);
    second.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn pinned_session_settings_override_database_defaults() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    for setting in [
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET timezone = 'America/New_York'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET datestyle = 'SQL, DMY'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET intervalstyle = 'iso_8601'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET bytea_output = 'escape'",
        "ALTER DATABASE postgres SET extra_float_digits = 0",
    ] {
        pg.execute(setting).await?;
    }
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &["CREATE TABLE probe (id int PRIMARY KEY, at timestamptz, day date, span interval, raw bytea, ratio float8)"],
        &["probe"],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute(
        r"INSERT INTO probe VALUES (1, '2026-10-14 12:00:00+00', '2026-10-14', '1 day 02:00:00', '\x0102', 1.1::float8 + 2.2::float8)",
    )
    .await?;
    let event = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(event["after"]["at"], "2026-10-14T12:00:00+00:00");
    assert_eq!(event["after"]["day"], "2026-10-14");
    assert_eq!(event["after"]["span"], "1 day 02:00:00");
    assert_eq!(event["after"]["raw"], "\\x0102");
    assert_eq!(event["after"]["ratio"].to_string(), "3.3000000000000003");
    assert_eq!(event["source"]["commit_ts"].as_str().map(|ts| ts.ends_with("+00:00")), Some(true));
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn tls_require_streams_over_an_encrypted_replication_connection() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start_with(PostgresOptions { tls: true }).await?;
    support::prepare(&pg, &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY)"], &["orders"]).await?;
    let mut params = support::params(&pg);
    params.tls = TlsConfig { mode: TlsMode::Require, ca_file: None };
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(params, FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (7)").await?;
    assert_eq!(session.next_event().await?["after"]["id"], 7);
    let encrypted: i64 = pg
        .client()
        .await?
        .query_one(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM pg_stat_ssl s JOIN pg_stat_replication r ON r.pid = s.pid WHERE s.ssl",
            &[],
        )
        .await?
        .try_get(0)?;
    assert_eq!(encrypted, 1);
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_new_session_starts_from_a_sink_with_no_pending_events() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(&pg, &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY)"], &["orders"]).await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_after_abandoned_writes(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER, 3);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1), (2)").await?;
    for n in 0..3 {
        assert_eq!(session.next_event().await?, json!({"abandoned": n}));
    }
    let first = session.next_event().await?;
    let second = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!((&first["after"]["id"], &second["after"]["id"]), (&json!(1), &json!(2)));
    let commit = support::commit_lsn(&first)?;
    pg.wait_for_slot(SLOT, WAIT, |state| state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn > commit)).await?;
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn special_character_password_streams() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &[
            "CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY)",
            r#"CREATE ROLE "pg cdc" LOGIN REPLICATION PASSWORD 'p@ss w:rd/''"&=%'"#,
        ],
        &["orders"],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut params = support::params(&pg);

    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (3)").await?;
    assert_eq!(session.next_event().await?["after"]["id"], 3);
    session.stop().await
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/session.rs`:

```rust
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::time::Duration;

use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_decoder::message::{DecodeError, LogicalMessage, Relation, decode};
use pg_cdc_replication::{
    Lsn as WireLsn, PgWireError, ReplicationClient, ReplicationConfig, ReplicationEvent, TlsConfig as WireTls,
};
use secrecy::ExposeSecret;
use tokio::io::AsyncWrite;
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;

use crate::ack::{AckError, AckProgress};
use crate::catalog::{self, CatalogError, TypeResolver};
use crate::config::TlsMode;
use crate::connection::{
    ConnectError, ConnectParams, ConnectionProvider, ControlConnection, SESSION_OPTIONS, TlsConfig, connect_control,
};
use crate::event::{BuildError, Builder, Built, ColumnInfo, RelationInfo};
use crate::sink::StdoutSink;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SessionConfig {
    pub pipeline: String,
    pub slot: String,
    pub publication: String,
    pub status_interval: Duration,
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum SessionError {
    #[error(transparent)]
    Connect(#[from] ConnectError),
    #[error(transparent)]
    Catalog(#[from] CatalogError),
    #[error("replication slot {0} does not exist")]
    SlotMissing(String),
    #[error("replication stream failed: {0}")]
    Replication(#[from] PgWireError),
    #[error("replication stream ended")]
    StreamEnded,
    #[error("malformed message at {wal_start}: {source}")]
    Decode { wal_start: Lsn, source: DecodeError },
    #[error("cannot build an event at {wal_start}: {source}")]
    Build { wal_start: Lsn, source: BuildError },
    #[error("relation {oid} at {wal_start} has no Relation message on this connection")]
    UnknownRelation { wal_start: Lsn, oid: u32 },
    #[error("unexpected {what} message at {wal_start}")]
    Unexpected { wal_start: Lsn, what: &'static str },
    #[error("acknowledgement invariant violated: {0}")]
    Ack(#[from] AckError),
    #[error("sink write failed: {0}")]
    Sink(std::io::Error),
    #[error("cannot encode an event: {0}")]
    Encode(#[from] serde_json::Error),
}

impl SessionError {
    pub fn fault_lsn(&self) -> Option<Lsn> {
        match self {
            Self::Decode { wal_start, .. }
            | Self::Build { wal_start, .. }
            | Self::UnknownRelation { wal_start, .. }
            | Self::Unexpected { wal_start, .. } => Some(*wal_start),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn is_fatal(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Ack(_) | Self::Sink(_) | Self::Encode(_))
    }
}

pub async fn run_session<W: AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send>(
    config: &SessionConfig,
    provider: &dyn ConnectionProvider,
    types: &mut TypeResolver,
    sink: &mut StdoutSink<W>,
    shutdown: &CancellationToken,
) -> Result<(), SessionError> {
    sink.reset_pending().await.map_err(SessionError::Sink)?;
    let params = provider.params().await?;
    let control = connect_control(&params).await?;
    let start = catalog::slot_confirmed_flush(control.client(), &config.slot)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| SessionError::SlotMissing(config.slot.clone()))?;
    for (schema, table) in catalog::tables_without_full_identity(control.client(), &config.publication).await? {
        tracing::warn!(%schema, %table, "published table does not use REPLICA IDENTITY FULL");
    }
    let mut client = ReplicationClient::connect(replication_config(&params, config)).await?;
    client.update_applied_lsn(WireLsn(start.0));
    let mut stream = Stream {
        control: &control,
        types,
        sink,
        ack: AckProgress::new(start),
        builder: Builder::new(config.pipeline.clone()),
        relations: HashMap::new(),
    };
    loop {
        let received = tokio::select! {
            biased;
            () = shutdown.cancelled() => break,
            received = client.recv() => received,
        };
        let event = received?.ok_or(SessionError::StreamEnded)?;
        let confirmed = tokio::select! {
            biased;
            () = shutdown.cancelled() => break,
            handled = stream.handle(event) => handled?,
        };
        if let Some(lsn) = confirmed {
            client.update_applied_lsn(WireLsn(lsn.0));
        }
    }
    client.stop();
    Ok(())
}

fn replication_config(params: &ConnectParams, config: &SessionConfig) -> ReplicationConfig {
    let password = params.password.as_ref().map(|secret| secret.expose_secret().to_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    ReplicationConfig::new(
        params.host.clone(),
        params.user.clone(),
        password,
        params.database.clone(),
        config.slot.clone(),
        config.publication.clone(),
    )
    .with_port(params.port)
    .with_tls(wire_tls(&params.tls))
    .with_options(SESSION_OPTIONS)
    .with_status_interval(config.status_interval)
    .with_wakeup_interval(config.status_interval)
}

fn wire_tls(tls: &TlsConfig) -> WireTls {
    match tls.mode {
        TlsMode::Disable => WireTls::disabled(),
        TlsMode::Require => WireTls::require(),
        TlsMode::VerifyCa => WireTls::verify_ca(tls.ca_file.clone()),
        TlsMode::VerifyFull => WireTls::verify_full(tls.ca_file.clone()),
    }
}

struct Stream<'a, W> {
    control: &'a ControlConnection,
    types: &'a mut TypeResolver,
    sink: &'a mut StdoutSink<W>,
    ack: AckProgress,
    builder: Builder,
    relations: HashMap<u32, RelationInfo>,
}

impl<W: AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send> Stream<'_, W> {
    async fn handle(&mut self, event: ReplicationEvent) -> Result<Option<Lsn>, SessionError> {
        match event {
            ReplicationEvent::Begin { final_lsn, xid, commit_time_micros } => {
                let wal_start = Lsn(final_lsn.0);
                self.builder
                    .begin(wal_start, commit_time_micros)
                    .map_err(|source| SessionError::Build { wal_start, source })?;
                self.ack.begin()?;
                tracing::debug!(xid, commit_lsn = %wal_start, "transaction");
                Ok(None)
            }
            ReplicationEvent::XLogData { wal_start, data, .. } => {
                self.change(Lsn(wal_start.0), &data).await?;
                Ok(None)
            }
            ReplicationEvent::Commit { end_lsn, .. } => {
                let end_lsn = Lsn(end_lsn.0);
                self.builder.commit().map_err(|source| SessionError::Build { wal_start: end_lsn, source })?;
                let receipt = self.sink.flush().await.map_err(SessionError::Sink)?;
                self.ack.settle(receipt)?;
                Ok(Some(self.ack.commit(end_lsn)?))
            }
            ReplicationEvent::KeepAlive { wal_end, .. } => Ok(Some(self.ack.keepalive(Lsn(wal_end.0)))),
            ReplicationEvent::Message { lsn, .. } => {
                Err(SessionError::Unexpected { wal_start: Lsn(lsn.0), what: "logical decoding message" })
            }
            ReplicationEvent::StoppedAt { reached } => {
                Err(SessionError::Unexpected { wal_start: Lsn(reached.0), what: "stop" })
            }
        }
    }

    async fn change(&mut self, wal_start: Lsn, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), SessionError> {
        let message = decode(data).map_err(|source| SessionError::Decode { wal_start, source })?;
        let build_error = |source| SessionError::Build { wal_start, source };
        let built = match message {
            LogicalMessage::Relation(relation) => return self.relation(relation).await,
            LogicalMessage::Type(_) | LogicalMessage::Origin(_) => return Ok(()),
            LogicalMessage::Begin(_) | LogicalMessage::Commit(_) => {
                return Err(SessionError::Unexpected { wal_start, what: "transaction boundary" });
            }
            LogicalMessage::Insert(insert) => {
                let relation = known(&self.relations, wal_start, insert.relation_oid)?;
                self.builder.insert(relation, &insert).map_err(build_error)?
            }
            LogicalMessage::Update(update) => {
                let relation = known(&self.relations, wal_start, update.relation_oid)?;
                self.builder.update(relation, &update).map_err(build_error)?
            }
            LogicalMessage::Delete(delete) => {
                let relation = known(&self.relations, wal_start, delete.relation_oid)?;
                self.builder.delete(relation, &delete).map_err(build_error)?
            }
            LogicalMessage::Truncate(truncate) => {
                for oid in truncate.relation_oids {
                    let event = self.builder.truncate(known(&self.relations, wal_start, oid)?).map_err(build_error)?;
                    self.emit(event).await?;
                }
                return Ok(());
            }
        };
        self.emit(built).await
    }

    async fn relation(&mut self, relation: Relation) -> Result<(), SessionError> {
        let client = self.control.client();
        let mut columns = Vec::with_capacity(relation.columns.len());
        for column in relation.columns {
            let kind = self.types.resolve(client, column.type_oid).await?;
            columns.push(ColumnInfo { name: column.name, type_oid: column.type_oid, kind, is_identity: column.is_key });
        }
        let key_columns = catalog::key_columns(client, relation.oid).await?;
        let schema = if relation.namespace.is_empty() { "pg_catalog".to_owned() } else { relation.namespace };
        let info = RelationInfo { oid: relation.oid, schema, table: relation.name, columns, key_columns };
        self.relations.insert(relation.oid, info);
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn emit(&mut self, built: Built) -> Result<(), SessionError> {
        for fallback in &built.fallbacks {
            tracing::error!(
                id = %built.envelope.id,
                column = %fallback.column,
                type_oid = fallback.type_oid,
                "value fell back to its text form"
            );
        }
        let body = built.envelope.encode()?;
        self.sink.write(&body).await.map_err(SessionError::Sink)?;
        self.ack.sent()?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn known(relations: &HashMap<u32, RelationInfo>, wal_start: Lsn, oid: u32) -> Result<&RelationInfo, SessionError> {
    relations.get(&oid).ok_or(SessionError::UnknownRelation { wal_start, oid })
}
```

`known` takes the `relations` field rather than `&self`, so the lookup and the mutable borrow of `self.builder` touch disjoint fields. `ReplicationClient::connect` only spawns the worker, so seeding the applied LSN right after it returns happens before the first status update can be sent (Ruling R11). Never `Debug`-format the `ReplicationConfig` built here: it carries the password in plain text.

Run: `cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test stream`
Expected: 8 tests pass. Then `PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION=14 cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test stream` and the same with `18`: all pass (Postgres 14 sends empty Begin/Commit pairs for transactions on unpublished tables; the session acknowledges them at Commit).
