```rust
use std::time::Duration;

use pg_cdc_testkit::Postgres;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

use crate::support::{self, SLOT, TestResult, WAIT};

const FAST: Duration = Duration::from_millis(200);
const BUFFER: usize = 1 << 16;

#[tokio::test]
async fn toast_unchanged_under_default_identity_is_listed() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &["CREATE TABLE docs (id int PRIMARY KEY, title text, body text)", "ALTER TABLE docs ALTER COLUMN body SET STORAGE EXTERNAL"],
        &["docs"],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO docs VALUES (1, 'a', repeat('x', 5000))").await?;
    pg.execute("UPDATE docs SET title = 'b' WHERE id = 1").await?;
    pg.execute("UPDATE docs SET id = 2 WHERE id = 1").await?;
    let insert = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(insert["after"]["body"].as_str().map(str::len), Some(5000));
    let same_key = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(same_key["before"], Value::Null);
    assert_eq!(same_key["after"], json!({"id": 1, "title": "b"}));
    assert_eq!(same_key["unchanged_toast"], json!(["body"]));
    let new_key = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(new_key["before"], json!({"id": 1}));
    assert_eq!(new_key["after"], json!({"id": 2, "title": "b"}));
    assert_eq!(new_key["key"], json!({"id": 2}));
    assert_eq!(new_key["unchanged_toast"], json!(["body"]));
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn toast_filled_under_full_identity() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &[
            "CREATE TABLE docs (id int PRIMARY KEY, title text, body text)",
            "ALTER TABLE docs ALTER COLUMN body SET STORAGE EXTERNAL",
            "ALTER TABLE docs REPLICA IDENTITY FULL",
        ],
        &["docs"],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO docs VALUES (1, 'a', repeat('y', 5000))").await?;
    pg.execute("UPDATE docs SET title = 'b' WHERE id = 1").await?;
    session.next_event().await?;
    let update = session.next_event().await?;
    let body = "y".repeat(5000);
    assert_eq!(update["before"], json!({"id": 1, "title": "a", "body": body}));
    assert_eq!(update["after"], json!({"id": 1, "title": "b", "body": body}));
    assert!(update.get("unchanged_toast").is_none());
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn truncate_emits_one_event_per_relation() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &["CREATE TABLE a (id int PRIMARY KEY)", "CREATE TABLE b (id int PRIMARY KEY)"],
        &["a", "b"],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("TRUNCATE a, b").await?;
    let first = session.next_event().await?;
    let second = session.next_event().await?;
    let mut tables = Vec::new();
    for (ordinal, event) in [&first, &second].into_iter().enumerate() {
        assert_eq!(event["op"], "truncate");
        assert_eq!((&event["key"], &event["before"], &event["after"]), (&Value::Null, &Value::Null, &Value::Null));
        assert!(event["id"].as_str().unwrap().ends_with(&format!("-{ordinal:08x}")));
        tables.push(event["table"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned());
    }
    tables.sort();
    assert_eq!(tables, ["a", "b"]);
    assert_eq!(support::commit_lsn(&first)?, support::commit_lsn(&second)?);
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn table_without_key_has_null_key() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &["CREATE TABLE nokey (a int, b text)", "ALTER TABLE nokey REPLICA IDENTITY FULL"],
        &["nokey"],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO nokey VALUES (1, 'x')").await?;
    pg.execute("DELETE FROM nokey").await?;
    let insert = session.next_event().await?;
    let delete = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(insert["key"], Value::Null);
    assert_eq!(delete["key"], Value::Null);
    assert_eq!(delete["before"], json!({"a": 1, "b": "x"}));
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn schema_change_mid_stream_updates_relation() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(&pg, &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY, status text)"], &["orders"]).await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'a')").await?;
    pg.execute("ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN extra int DEFAULT 7").await?;
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (2, 'b')").await?;
    pg.execute("ALTER TABLE orders DROP COLUMN status").await?;
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (3)").await?;
    assert_eq!(session.next_event().await?["after"], json!({"id": 1, "status": "a"}));
    assert_eq!(session.next_event().await?["after"], json!({"id": 2, "status": "b", "extra": 7}));
    assert_eq!(session.next_event().await?["after"], json!({"id": 3, "extra": 7}));
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn quoted_identifiers_stream_with_exact_names() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(
        &pg,
        &[
            r#"CREATE SCHEMA "Weird Schema""#,
            r#"CREATE TABLE "Weird Schema"."Order Items" ("Order ID" int PRIMARY KEY, "naïve" text)"#,
        ],
        &[r#""Weird Schema"."Order Items""#],
    )
    .await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute(r#"INSERT INTO "Weird Schema"."Order Items" VALUES (1, 'ok')"#).await?;
    pg.execute(r#"UPDATE "Weird Schema"."Order Items" SET "Order ID" = 2"#).await?;
    let insert = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(insert["schema"], "Weird Schema");
    assert_eq!(insert["table"], "Order Items");
    assert_eq!(insert["key"], json!({"Order ID": 1}));
    assert_eq!(insert["after"], json!({"Order ID": 1, "naïve": "ok"}));
    let update = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(update["before"], json!({"Order ID": 1}));
    assert_eq!(update["key"], json!({"Order ID": 2}));
    session.stop().await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn o2_large_transaction_streams_in_stream_order() -> TestResult {
    const ROWS: i64 = 20_000;
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    support::prepare(&pg, &["CREATE TABLE items (id int PRIMARY KEY, label text)"], &["items"]).await?;
    let mut session = support::spawn_session(support::params(&pg), FAST, BUFFER);
    pg.execute(&format!("INSERT INTO items SELECT g, 'row ' || g FROM generate_series(1, {ROWS}) g")).await?;
    pg.execute(&format!("INSERT INTO items VALUES ({}, 'after')", ROWS + 1)).await?;
    let mut previous = String::new();
    let mut first_commit = None;
    let mut last_commit = 0;
    for expected in 1..=ROWS + 1 {
        let event = session.next_event().await?;
        assert_eq!(event["after"]["id"], expected);
        let id = event["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned();
        assert!(previous < id, "{previous} then {id}");
        let commit = support::commit_lsn(&event)?;
        if expected <= ROWS {
            assert_eq!(*first_commit.get_or_insert(commit), commit);
            assert!(id.ends_with(&format!("-{:08x}", expected - 1)), "{id}");
        } else {
            assert!(Some(commit) > first_commit);
        }
        last_commit = commit;
        previous = id;
    }
    pg.wait_for_slot(SLOT, WAIT, |state| state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn > last_commit)).await?;
    session.stop().await
}
```

### Task 13: Supervisor, CLI and binary
**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 2, Task 6, Task 7, Task 11
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Implements `pg_cdc::supervisor` and the `pg-cdc run --config <file>` binary (spec §10.7, §11.7, §16.1, §17.4). The supervisor reruns the session after any non-fatal error with full-jitter backoff from 1 s to 30 s (Ruling R13), calling the provider again each time; it halts after three consecutive decode faults at the same LSN and never skips (D3); it stops at once on a fatal error (a broken acknowledgement invariant or a failed sink write). The binary loads the configuration, logs JSON through `tracing` (to stderr while the stdout sink is selected, Ruling R8), builds the configured provider, and exits 0 after SIGTERM or Ctrl-C. A replay after reconnecting rebuilds the relation cache (D4), and pg-cdc never creates a missing slot (S1).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/supervisor.rs` (empty from Task 1), `crates/pg-cdc/src/main.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`, `README.md`
- Test: unit tests in `crates/pg-cdc/src/supervisor.rs`; `crates/pg-cdc/tests/cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc::session::{run_session, SessionConfig, SessionError}` (Task 11); `pg_cdc::config::{load, Config, Credentials, SinkConfig, SourceConfig}` (Task 6); `pg_cdc::connection::{ConnectionProvider, ConnectParams, PasswordProvider, RdsIamProvider, TlsConfig}` (Task 7); `pg_cdc::catalog::TypeResolver` (Task 9); `pg_cdc::sink::StdoutSink` (Task 10); `pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, parse_lsn}` (tests).
- Produces:
  - `pub const HALT_AFTER_FAULTS: u32 = 3`, `pub const BACKOFF_BASE: Duration` (1 s), `pub const BACKOFF_CAP: Duration` (30 s)
  - `pub trait Attempt { fn run(&mut self) -> impl Future<Output = Result<(), SessionError>> + Send; }`
  - `pub struct LiveSession<'a, W>` with public fields `config: &'a SessionConfig`, `provider: &'a dyn ConnectionProvider`, `types: &'a mut TypeResolver`, `sink: &'a mut StdoutSink<W>`, `shutdown: &'a CancellationToken`, implementing `Attempt` by calling `run_session`
  - `pub struct Backoff` (`Default`) with `next_delay(&mut self) -> Duration` and `reset(&mut self)`
  - `pub async fn supervise<A: Attempt>(attempt: &mut A, shutdown: &CancellationToken) -> Result<(), SupervisorError>`
  - `pub enum SupervisorError { Halted { lsn: Lsn, count: u32, last: SessionError }, Fatal(SessionError) }`
  - Binary: `pg-cdc run --config <file>`; exit 0 on SIGTERM or Ctrl-C, 1 on an invalid configuration, a halt or a fatal error

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use pg_cdc_decoder::message::DecodeError;
    use std::collections::VecDeque;

    struct Scripted {
        outcomes: VecDeque<Result<(), SessionError>>,
        runs: usize,
        cancel_after_first: Option<CancellationToken>,
    }

    impl Scripted {
        fn new(outcomes: Vec<Result<(), SessionError>>) -> Self {
            Self { outcomes: outcomes.into(), runs: 0, cancel_after_first: None }
        }
    }

    impl Attempt for Scripted {
        fn run(&mut self) -> impl Future<Output = Result<(), SessionError>> + Send {
            self.runs += 1;
            if let Some(token) = &self.cancel_after_first {
                token.cancel();
            }
            std::future::ready(self.outcomes.pop_front().unwrap_or(Ok(())))
        }
    }

    fn fault(lsn: u64) -> Result<(), SessionError> {
        Err(SessionError::Decode { wal_start: Lsn(lsn), source: DecodeError::Empty })
    }

    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
    async fn d3_three_consecutive_faults_at_same_lsn_halt() {
        let mut attempt = Scripted::new(vec![fault(0x10), fault(0x10), fault(0x10), Ok(())]);
        let result = supervise(&mut attempt, &CancellationToken::new()).await;
        assert!(matches!(result, Err(SupervisorError::Halted { lsn: Lsn(0x10), count: 3, .. })), "{result:?}");
        assert_eq!(attempt.runs, 3);
    }

    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
    async fn faults_at_different_lsns_do_not_halt() {
        let mut attempt = Scripted::new(vec![fault(0x10), fault(0x20), fault(0x10), fault(0x10), Ok(())]);
        assert!(supervise(&mut attempt, &CancellationToken::new()).await.is_ok());
        assert_eq!(attempt.runs, 5);
    }

    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
    async fn transport_errors_retry_until_the_session_ends_cleanly() {
        let mut outcomes: Vec<Result<(), SessionError>> = (0..6).map(|_| Err(SessionError::StreamEnded)).collect();
        outcomes.push(Ok(()));
        let mut attempt = Scripted::new(outcomes);
        assert!(supervise(&mut attempt, &CancellationToken::new()).await.is_ok());
        assert_eq!(attempt.runs, 7);
    }

    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
    async fn fatal_errors_stop_at_once() {
        let mut attempt = Scripted::new(vec![Err(SessionError::Ack(crate::ack::AckError::NotOpen)), Ok(())]);
        let result = supervise(&mut attempt, &CancellationToken::new()).await;
        assert!(matches!(result, Err(SupervisorError::Fatal(SessionError::Ack(_)))), "{result:?}");
        assert_eq!(attempt.runs, 1);
    }

    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
    async fn shutdown_during_backoff_returns_ok() {
        let shutdown = CancellationToken::new();
        let mut attempt = Scripted::new(vec![Err(SessionError::StreamEnded), Err(SessionError::StreamEnded)]);
        attempt.cancel_after_first = Some(shutdown.clone());
        assert!(supervise(&mut attempt, &shutdown).await.is_ok());
        assert_eq!(attempt.runs, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn backoff_uses_full_jitter_capped_at_thirty_seconds() {
        let mut backoff = Backoff::default();
        let caps = [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 30, 30, 30];
        for cap in caps {
            let delay = backoff.next_delay();
            assert!(delay <= Duration::from_secs(cap), "{delay:?} above {cap}s");
        }
        backoff.reset();
        for _ in 0..100 {
            let mut fresh = Backoff::default();
            assert!(fresh.next_delay() <= BACKOFF_BASE);
        }
        assert!(backoff.next_delay() <= BACKOFF_BASE);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the supervisor**

`crates/pg-cdc/src/supervisor.rs` (above its tests):

```rust
use std::fmt;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use tokio::io::AsyncWrite;
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;

use crate::catalog::TypeResolver;
use crate::connection::ConnectionProvider;
use crate::session::{SessionConfig, SessionError, run_session};
use crate::sink::StdoutSink;

pub const HALT_AFTER_FAULTS: u32 = 3;
pub const BACKOFF_BASE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);
pub const BACKOFF_CAP: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

pub trait Attempt {
    fn run(&mut self) -> impl Future<Output = Result<(), SessionError>> + Send;
}

pub struct LiveSession<'a, W> {
    pub config: &'a SessionConfig,
    pub provider: &'a dyn ConnectionProvider,
    pub types: &'a mut TypeResolver,
    pub sink: &'a mut StdoutSink<W>,
    pub shutdown: &'a CancellationToken,
}

impl<W> fmt::Debug for LiveSession<'_, W> {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("LiveSession").field("config", &self.config).finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

impl<W: AsyncWrite + Unpin + Send> Attempt for LiveSession<'_, W> {
    fn run(&mut self) -> impl Future<Output = Result<(), SessionError>> + Send {
        run_session(self.config, self.provider, self.types, self.sink, self.shutdown)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum SupervisorError {
    #[error("halted after {count} consecutive faults at {lsn}: {last}")]
    Halted { lsn: Lsn, count: u32, last: SessionError },
    #[error("stopped on a fatal error: {0}")]
    Fatal(SessionError),
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Backoff {
    attempt: u32,
}

impl Backoff {
    pub fn next_delay(&mut self) -> Duration {
        let cap = BACKOFF_BASE.saturating_mul(1 << self.attempt.min(5)).min(BACKOFF_CAP);
        self.attempt = self.attempt.saturating_add(1);
        let cap_ms = u64::try_from(cap.as_millis()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
        Duration::from_millis(fastrand::u64(0..=cap_ms))
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.attempt = 0;
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct FaultTracker {
    lsn: Option<Lsn>,
    count: u32,
}

impl FaultTracker {
    fn record(&mut self, lsn: Lsn) -> u32 {
        if self.lsn == Some(lsn) {
            self.count += 1;
        } else {
            self.lsn = Some(lsn);
            self.count = 1;
        }
        self.count
    }
}

pub async fn supervise<A: Attempt>(attempt: &mut A, shutdown: &CancellationToken) -> Result<(), SupervisorError> {
    let mut faults = FaultTracker::default();
    let mut backoff = Backoff::default();
    loop {
        let started = Instant::now();
        let error = match attempt.run().await {
            Ok(()) => return Ok(()),
            Err(error) => error,
        };
        if error.is_fatal() {
            return Err(SupervisorError::Fatal(error));
        }
        if let Some(lsn) = error.fault_lsn() {
            let count = faults.record(lsn);
            if count >= HALT_AFTER_FAULTS {
                return Err(SupervisorError::Halted { lsn, count, last: error });
            }
        }
        if started.elapsed() >= BACKOFF_CAP {
            backoff.reset();
        }
        let delay = backoff.next_delay();
        let delay_ms = u64::try_from(delay.as_millis()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
        tracing::warn!(error = %error, delay_ms, "replication session ended; reconnecting");
        tokio::select! {
            biased;
            () = shutdown.cancelled() => return Ok(()),
            () = tokio::time::sleep(delay) => {}
        }
    }
}
```

`LiveSession` implements `Debug` by hand because `dyn ConnectionProvider` is not `Debug`. The supervisor never skips a faulting LSN: after three faults it returns `Halted` and the process exits non-zero, leaving the slot where it was.

Run: `cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc supervisor`
Expected: 6 tests pass.

```rust
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::io::Write as _;
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::time::Duration;

use pg_cdc_testkit::{Postgres, parse_lsn};
use serde_json::Value;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, BufReader, Lines};
use tokio::process::{Child, ChildStdout, Command};

type TestResult<T = ()> = Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

const WAIT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

fn write_config(pg: &Postgres, slot: &str) -> TestResult<tempfile::NamedTempFile> {
    let mut file = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new()?;
    write!(
        file,
        "pipeline: cli\nsource:\n  host: {host}\n  port: {port}\n  database: {database}\n  user: {user}\n  credentials:\n    type: password\n  tls:\n    mode: disable\n  slot: {slot}\n  publication: cli_pub\n  status_interval: 200ms\nsink:\n  type: stdout\n",
        host = pg.host(),
        port = pg.port(),
        database = pg.database(),
        user = pg.user(),
    )?;
    Ok(file)
}

fn spawn(config: &Path, password: &str, stderr: Stdio) -> TestResult<Child> {
    Ok(Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_pg-cdc"))
        .arg("run")
        .arg("--config")
        .arg(config)
        .env("PGCDC__SOURCE__CREDENTIALS__PASSWORD", password)
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(stderr)
        .kill_on_drop(true)
        .spawn()?)
}

async fn next_event(lines: &mut Lines<BufReader<ChildStdout>>) -> TestResult<Value> {
    let line = tokio::time::timeout(WAIT, lines.next_line()).await??.ok_or("stdout closed")?;
    Ok(serde_json::from_str(&line)?)
}

async fn sigterm(child: &Child) -> TestResult {
    let pid = child.id().ok_or("process already exited")?;
    let status = Command::new("kill").arg("-TERM").arg(pid.to_string()).status().await?;
    if status.success() { Ok(()) } else { Err("kill -TERM failed".into()) }
}

async fn prepare(pg: &Postgres, create_slot: bool) -> TestResult {
    pg.execute("CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY, status text)").await?;
    pg.create_publication("cli_pub", &["orders"]).await?;
    if create_slot {
        pg.create_slot("cli_slot").await?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn run_streams_to_stdout_acknowledges_and_exits_cleanly_on_sigterm() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    prepare(&pg, true).await?;
    let config = write_config(&pg, "cli_slot")?;
    let mut child = spawn(config.path(), pg.password(), Stdio::null())?;
    let mut stdout = BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().ok_or("no stdout")?).lines();
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'paid')").await?;
    let event = next_event(&mut stdout).await?;
    assert_eq!(event["op"], "insert");
    assert_eq!(event["after"]["status"], "paid");
    assert_eq!(event["source"]["pipeline"], "cli");
    let commit = parse_lsn(event["source"]["commit_lsn"].as_str().ok_or("no commit_lsn")?)?;
    pg.wait_for_slot("cli_slot", WAIT, |state| state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn > commit)).await?;
    sigterm(&child).await?;
    let status = tokio::time::timeout(WAIT, child.wait()).await??;
    assert!(status.success(), "{status}");
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn d4_reconnect_rebuilds_relation_cache_and_replays_unacknowledged() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    prepare(&pg, true).await?;
    let config = write_config(&pg, "cli_slot")?;
    let mut child = spawn(config.path(), pg.password(), Stdio::null())?;
    let mut stdout = BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().ok_or("no stdout")?).lines();
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'before reconnect')").await?;
    let first = next_event(&mut stdout).await?;
    let mut seen: HashMap<String, Value> = HashMap::new();
    seen.insert(first["id"].as_str().ok_or("no id")?.to_owned(), first);
    pg.wait_for_slot("cli_slot", WAIT, |state| state.active).await?;
    assert!(pg.terminate_walsender("cli_slot").await?);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (2, 'after reconnect')").await?;
    loop {
        let event = next_event(&mut stdout).await?;
        let id = event["id"].as_str().ok_or("no id")?.to_owned();
        if let Some(previous) = seen.get(&id) {
            assert_eq!(previous, &event, "a replayed event changed");
        }
        if event["after"]["id"] == 2 {
            assert_eq!(event["after"]["status"], "after reconnect");
            assert_eq!(event["table"], "orders");
            break;
        }
        seen.insert(id, event);
    }
    sigterm(&child).await?;
    assert!(tokio::time::timeout(WAIT, child.wait()).await??.success());
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn s1_missing_slot_is_never_created() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    prepare(&pg, false).await?;
    let config = write_config(&pg, "missing_slot")?;
    let mut child = spawn(config.path(), pg.password(), Stdio::piped())?;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3)).await;
    assert!(pg.slot_state("missing_slot").await?.is_none(), "pg-cdc created a slot");
    assert!(child.try_wait()?.is_none(), "pg-cdc must keep retrying, not exit");
    child.start_kill()?;
    let output = child.wait_with_output().await?;
    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
    assert!(stderr.contains("replication slot missing_slot does not exist"), "{stderr}");
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn password_in_config_file_is_rejected() -> TestResult {
    let mut file = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new()?;
    write!(
        file,
        "pipeline: cli\nsource:\n  host: 127.0.0.1\n  database: postgres\n  user: postgres\n  credentials:\n    type: password\n    password: hunter2\n  slot: s\n  publication: p\nsink:\n  type: stdout\n"
    )?;
    let output = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_pg-cdc")).arg("run").arg("--config").arg(file.path()).output().await?;
    assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(1));
    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
    assert!(stderr.contains("PGCDC__SOURCE__CREDENTIALS__PASSWORD"), "{stderr}");
    assert!(!stderr.contains("hunter2"), "{stderr}");
    assert_eq!(output.stdout, Vec::<u8>::new());
    Ok(())
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the binary**

`crates/pg-cdc/src/main.rs`:

```rust
use std::io::Write as _;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::process::ExitCode;

use anyhow::Context as _;
use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
use pg_cdc::catalog::TypeResolver;
use pg_cdc::config::{Config, Credentials, SinkConfig, SourceConfig};
use pg_cdc::connection::{ConnectParams, ConnectionProvider, PasswordProvider, RdsIamProvider, TlsConfig};
use pg_cdc::session::SessionConfig;
use pg_cdc::sink::StdoutSink;
use pg_cdc::supervisor::{LiveSession, supervise};
use tokio::signal::unix::{SignalKind, signal};
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;
use tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter;

#[derive(Debug, Parser)]
#[command(name = "pg-cdc", version, about = "Stream Postgres logical replication changes to a sink")]
struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    command: Command,
}

#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
enum Command {
    Run {
        #[arg(long)]
        config: PathBuf,
    },
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let Command::Run { config } = Cli::parse().command;
    let env = std::env::vars_os().filter_map(|(key, value)| Some((key.into_string().ok()?, value.into_string().ok()?)));
    let config = match pg_cdc::config::load(&config, env) {
        Ok(config) => config,
        Err(error) => {
            let _ = writeln!(std::io::stderr(), "pg-cdc: invalid configuration: {error}");
            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
        }
    };
    init_logging(&config.sink);
    match run(config).await {
        Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
        Err(error) => {
            tracing::error!("pg-cdc stopped: {error:#}");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn init_logging(sink: &SinkConfig) {
    let filter = EnvFilter::try_from_default_env().unwrap_or_else(|_| EnvFilter::new("info"));
    let builder = tracing_subscriber::fmt().json().with_env_filter(filter);
    match sink {
        SinkConfig::Stdout => builder.with_writer(std::io::stderr).init(),
    }
}

async fn run(config: Config) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let provider = provider(&config.source).await;
    let shutdown = CancellationToken::new();
    tokio::spawn(watch_signals(shutdown.clone()));
    let session = SessionConfig {
        pipeline: config.pipeline.clone(),
        slot: config.source.slot.clone(),
        publication: config.source.publication.clone(),
        status_interval: config.source.status_interval,
    };
    let mut types = TypeResolver::new();
    let mut sink = StdoutSink::stdout();
    let mut live = LiveSession {
        config: &session,
        provider: provider.as_ref(),
        types: &mut types,
        sink: &mut sink,
        shutdown: &shutdown,
    };
    tracing::info!(pipeline = %config.pipeline, slot = %config.source.slot, "pg-cdc starting");
    supervise(&mut live, &shutdown).await.context("replication stopped")?;
    tracing::info!("pg-cdc stopped cleanly");
    Ok(())
}

async fn provider(source: &SourceConfig) -> Box<dyn ConnectionProvider> {
    let base = ConnectParams {
        host: source.host.clone(),
        port: source.port,
        database: source.database.clone(),
        user: source.user.clone(),
        password: None,
        tls: TlsConfig { mode: source.tls.mode, ca_file: source.tls.ca_file.clone() },
    };
    match &source.credentials {
        Credentials::Password { password } => Box::new(PasswordProvider::new(ConnectParams { password: password.clone(), ..base })),
        Credentials::AwsRdsIam { region } => Box::new(RdsIamProvider::from_environment(base, region.clone()).await),
    }
}

async fn watch_signals(shutdown: CancellationToken) {
    let mut terminate = match signal(SignalKind::terminate()) {
        Ok(terminate) => terminate,
        Err(error) => {
            tracing::error!(%error, "cannot listen for SIGTERM");
            return;
        }
    };
    tokio::select! {
        _ = terminate.recv() => {}
        result = tokio::signal::ctrl_c() => {
            if let Err(error) = result {
                tracing::error!(%error, "cannot listen for Ctrl-C");
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    tracing::info!("shutdown requested");
    shutdown.cancel();
}
```

`std::env::vars()` panics on a variable that is not valid Unicode, so the binary reads `vars_os()` and skips such variables; a `PGCDC__` override must be valid Unicode to take effect. The configuration error goes to stderr with `writeln!` because the logger depends on the configuration it failed to load; `ConfigError`'s messages never contain a password (Task 6 rejects a file password without echoing it).

Run: `cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test cli`
Expected: 4 tests pass.

- [ ] **Step 6: Document running pg-cdc**

Add this section to `README.md` after "Status":

````markdown
## Run

Create the publication and slot first (pg-cdc never creates or drops either):

```sql
CREATE PUBLICATION pgcdc_orders FOR TABLE public.orders;
ALTER TABLE public.orders REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
SELECT pg_create_logical_replication_slot('pgcdc_orders', 'pgoutput');
```

Then write a config file and run:

```yaml
pipeline: orders
source:
  host: db.internal
  database: app
  user: pgcdc
  credentials:
    type: password
  tls:
    mode: verify-full
  slot: pgcdc_orders
  publication: pgcdc_orders
sink:
  type: stdout
```

```sh
PGCDC__SOURCE__CREDENTIALS__PASSWORD=... pg-cdc run --config pg-cdc.yaml
```

Each change is one JSON line on stdout; logs are JSON on stderr. Any key can be overridden with `PGCDC__<SECTION>__<KEY>`, and passwords come only from the environment. For Aurora with RDS IAM auth, use `credentials: { type: aws_rds_iam }` with the default AWS credential chain. pg-cdc runs on Linux and macOS.
````

Deferred by design: order keys, lanes, the `Sink` trait, SNS and `discard` sinks, backpressure by bytes, retries (M2); StateStore, parking, PayloadStore (M3); leadership, startup validation, `pg-cdc health` (M4); admin API, OpenTelemetry and metrics, chaos and load tests, Docker image, runbook (M5).

Verification on 2026-10-14: the Rust code of Tasks 2–13 was assembled into a scratch workspace with the upstream pgwire-replication 0.4.1 source patched as Task 3 describes. With the workspace lints on Rust 1.99.0, `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` is clean for the testkit, decoder and pg-cdc crates (the vendored crate was checked without workspace lints; Task 3 Step 6 produces its `#![expect]` lists). Every unit, property and integration test in the plan passes against Postgres 17, the version-sensitive suites (`to_jsonb_reference`, `stream`, `catalog`) also pass on Postgres 14 and 18, and the golden fixture assertions hold for fixtures captured from Postgres 14, 15, 16, 17 and 18. Both fuzz targets build on nightly, `ci.yml` passes actionlint 1.7.12, the `mise.toml` tasks parse, and `cargo deny check` with this `deny.toml` reports advisories, licenses and sources ok for the dependency set.

The pre-flight check on 2026-10-14 (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-m1-stream/preflight.md`) then amended Tasks 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8, 9, 11 and 13: the shared-manifest rule, the coverage scope, R11, the testkit TLS command, the rustdoc expectation, the golden-test helpers, the decoder-only coverage step, the Task 6 dependencies of Tasks 8 and 9, the single key-column query, the seeded applied LSN, the biased shutdown select, the stronger S1 assertion and the Unicode-safe environment read. Those edits were reviewed against the spec and the upstream 0.4.1 source but not re-run in the scratch workspace; the task gates run them.

Checks run on this plan: no placeholder steps; every type and function a task consumes is produced by an earlier task with the same name and signature (`Lsn`, `TypeKind`, `map_value`, `format_commit_ts`, `ConnectParams`, `TlsConfig`, `TlsMode`, `TypeResolver`, `Builder`, `RelationInfo`, `StdoutSink`, `Delivered`, `AckProgress`, `SessionError`, `run_session`, the stream test harness); every Review Focus item has a named test in its owning task.
